Conclusion: 提出统一 Web 访问接口 Web$hell，将搜索、浏览器与文件系统访问收敛到单一访问模型；用 WebContract 定义公共 schema，统一表征网页中的可寻址信息对象与动作对象；WebCompiler 将实时网页编译为可寻址的信息与动作对象；WebCache 持久化保存这些对象，支持智能体在轨迹中回溯与复用已获取证据；共享操作集合使智能体以一致、可操作、持久的方式访问 Web

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are becoming direct users of the open web. As they take on increasingly complex and information-intensive tasks, effective web access is becoming a core capability for agentic problem solving. Existing approaches have advanced agentic web access through different access models, including search-augmented agents, browser-based agents, and, more recently, filesystem-based agents. Nevertheless, as users expect agents to complete long-horizon, end-to-end tasks, they increasingly need to conduct broad information gathering, perform browser actions, and revisit acquired evidence within a single trajectory. Yet our preliminary analyses reveal that existing access models struggle to meet these demands. They primarily support either information gathering or browser interaction, while combining their capabilities can involve substantial costs or performance trade-offs. To this end, we present Web\$hell, an interface that unifies web access under a single access model. Using a common schema defined by WebContract, WebCompiler compiles live webpages into addressable information and action objects. These objects are maintained in WebCache and handled through a shared set of operations, providing agents with consistent, operable, and persistent web access. Through extensive experiments across web search, web navigation, and deep research benchmarks, we show that Web\$hell achieves strong task performance at lower cost. Our results further indicate the importance of interface design for effective web access, with Web\$hell opening new possibilities for more capable and efficient LLM agents on the web.

</details>


### [24] [Travel Back To Move Forward: Retrospective Exploration and Evolving for Long Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13058)
*Ziang Ye,Wentao Shi,Lei Chen,Zhengzhou Cai,Yu Wang,Zhiyuan Yao,Zihan Wang,Xierui Song,Fuli Feng,Yixin Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReTRAIL 面向长时程 LLM agent 的级联失败问题，提出将环境回退（rewind）与经验复用结合的框架，并用 State-Anchored GRPO 与 branch-advancement reward 训练共享策略；在四个交互式基准上证明单纯回退无益，而回退加经验复用能有效提升长时程任务求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程环境中 LLM agent 的前期错误会导致级联失败并陷入死胡同轨迹，agent 需要能主动从无效状态恢复并复用被放弃分支的探索经验来指导后续决策。

Method: ReTRAIL 以长时程交互环境中的 LLM agent 为对象。输入为环境状态与交互历史，agent 在每一步产生动作并推进环境。核心机制是 rewind 原语：当当前轨迹被判定为无效或死胡同时，可将环境状态回退到更早的已访问状态，但保留此前的有效进展，并把被放弃分支上获得的经验/知识继续携带到后续决策中，实现“环境状态后退、agent 知识前进”。训练上采用共享策略，使用两个互补信号：State-Anchored GRPO 在重访的锚定状态上对多个备选后续分支进行比较，以相对优势优化执行策略；branch-advancement reward 则依据后续探索所取得的推进程度来引导反思行为。二者共同训练同一策略，使得回退后的探索能够被有效利用。推理/执行时，agent 在遇到无效状态时触发回退，并基于已积累的经验重新规划后续动作。实验在 MineSweeper、Sokoban、ALFWorld、ScienceWorld 四个交互式基准上进行，对比表明仅加入 rewind 原语作用有限甚至有害，而将环境回退与经验复用耦合可提升长时程任务求解能力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "长时程任务表现", "value": "ReTRAIL 使 agent 能从无收益状态回退并利用被放弃分支的洞见指导后续探索；单独添加 rewind 原语收益有限甚至导致性能下降", "setting": "四个交互式基准 MineSweeper、Sokoban、ALFWorld、ScienceWorld"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: ReTRAIL 框架：允许将环境回退到较早状态，同时保留已取得的有效进度并延续被放弃探索所获得的知识；State-Anchored GRPO：在重访状态上比较不同后续分支，改进执行策略；branch-advancement reward：利用后续探索的推进程度指导反思，训练共享策略的两个互补信号；实验表明单纯加入 rewind 原语收益有限甚至会掉性能，关键在于将环境回退与经验复用耦合

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly operate in long-horizon environments where early mistakes can trigger cascading failures and trap agents in dead-end trajectories. Agents must therefore be able to proactively recover from such states and turn their exploratory experience into guidance for subsequent decisions. We introduce ReTRAIL, a framework that enables agents to rewind the environment to earlier states while preserving valid progress and carrying forward knowledge learned from abandoned exploration. The environment state moves backward while the agent's knowledge continues to move forward. ReTRAIL trains a shared policy with two complementary signals: State-Anchored GRPO compares alternative continuations from revisited states to improve execution, while a branch-advancement reward guides reflection through the progress of subsequent exploration. Experiments across four interactive benchmark MineSweeper, Sokoban, ALFWorld and ScienceWorld show that simply adding a rewind primitive provides limited benefit and can even degrade performance. In contrast, ReTRAIL enables agents to rewind from unproductive states and use insights from abandoned branches to guide subsequent exploration. These results demonstrate the value of coupling environment rewinding with experience reuse within an ongoing task for long-horizon task solving. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [25] [A Step Harder, Not a Step Too Far: The Benefits and Limits of Curriculum Difficulty Progression in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13669)
*Ronghan Li,Fei Chen,Mingyu Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文在 GRPO 训练中探索课程难度推进策略：动态重估样本难度并分阶段将训练难度带向更难样本移动。GSM8K 实验表明适度推进（test accuracy 0.6591）优于持续中等难度（0.6449），但过度推进会下降至 0.6405，呈现非单调收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 GRPO 训练中，持续选择中等难度样本是否能获得最优最终模型性能，以及根据策略演化动态调整课程难度能否进一步提升性能。

Method: 论文在 GRPO 训练框架内引入动态课程机制。每个训练步骤前，使用当前策略模型对训练样本重新估计难度（具体难度度量方法未在摘要中披露）。基于难度估计，将样本划分为不同难度带。课程设计采用分阶段策略：训练初期聚焦于中等难度样本（因其更易产生有信息量的组相对学习信号），随后逐步将活跃难度带向更难样本推进。训练目标仍为 GRPO 的组相对策略优化目标，即在每个批次内比较同一提示下多个生成样本的奖励，更新策略。推理时仅使用训练完成的策略模型，不涉及额外模块。论文通过对比固定中等难度课程与不同推进程度的分阶段课程，验证难度推进的收益与边界。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "test accuracy", "value": "0.6591 ± 0.0077", "setting": "GSM8K，三随机种子平均，分阶段推进至中等偏难难度带"}, {"metric": "test accuracy", "value": "0.6449 ± 0.0024", "setting": "GSM8K，三随机种子平均，固定中等难度课程"}, {"metric": "test accuracy", "value": "0.6405 ± 0.0053", "setting": "GSM8K，三随机种子平均，进一步推进至更难难度带"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在当前策略下重新估计样本难度，解决固定难度估计与演化策略不匹配的问题；设计分阶段课程，将活跃难度带逐步向更难样本推进；实验揭示课程难度推进对最终性能的非单调收益：适度推进优于持续中等难度，过度推进反而下降

Abstract: Curriculum learning typically organizes training samples from easy to hard. In GRPO, medium-difficulty samples are attractive for online selection because they are more likely to yield informative group-relative learning signals, and prioritizing them can improve training efficiency. However, it remains unclear whether persistently selecting such samples also leads to the strongest final model performance, or whether training can benefit from progressing toward harder samples. Meanwhile, sample difficulty changes as the policy evolves, so fixed difficulty estimates can become misaligned with the current policy. We therefore re-estimate sample difficulty under the current policy and, inspired by the easy-to-hard principle of curriculum learning, design and evaluate a staged curriculum that progressively shifts the active difficulty band toward harder samples. Experiments on GSM8K show that persistent medium-difficulty selection does not achieve the strongest observed performance. Across three random seeds, progressing to a moderately harder difficulty band improves test accuracy to $0.6591 \pm 0.0077$, compared with $0.6449 \pm 0.0024$ for the fixed-medium curriculum. However, further progression to a harder difficulty band reduces accuracy to $0.6405 \pm 0.0053$. These results show that staged difficulty progression can outperform persistent medium-difficulty selection, but its benefit is non-monotonic. Effective GRPO curricula can therefore benefit from progressing beyond medium difficulty, while avoiding excessive progression toward overly difficult samples.}

</details>


### [26] [Branching On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13481)
*Dongyang Chen,Shudong Liu,Shubo Li,Duoxun Tang,Jinwei Liang,Zehuan Wu,Lewei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BranchOPD通过在学生不确定的决策点分支重采样，扩展有限采样预算下的教师监督覆盖，在数学与ALFWorld/WebShop代理任务上优于Vanilla OPD和多轮采样OPD，并降低token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限采样预算下，单条on-policy轨迹每个决策只实现一种延续，导致教师监督覆盖不足，学生行为覆盖受限。

Method: BranchOPD在标准on-policy蒸馏（OPD）基础上扩展：学生模型生成主轨迹时，依据学生不确定性（如token概率熵）与师生分布不一致（如KL或JS散度）选择信息丰富的决策点；在选定的决策点处，从共享前缀出发重采样学生策略延续，形成多条分支轨迹，复用已生成的前缀以节省token。对主轨迹和分支轨迹的每个token均施加教师模型的token级监督（如KL散度或交叉熵）。此外，协议边界修复（PBR）在满足协议约束（如格式、轮次边界）的条件下进行针对性监督，以缓解重复生成和无效输出。训练目标为最小化学生与教师在主轨迹和分支上的token级分布差异。推理时学生模型独立生成，无额外分支采样开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "mean mathematics accuracy (avg@16)", "value": "+18.55 percentage points vs Vanilla OPD", "setting": "数学任务，平均16个采样答案"}, {"metric": "mean ALFWorld ID/OOD success", "value": "+13.8 points vs Vanilla OPD", "setting": "ALFWorld 分布内/分布外成功率"}, {"metric": "student-generated token cost reduction", "value": "约19% on ALFWorld", "setting": "对比7条独立轨迹"}, {"metric": "student-generated token cost reduction", "value": "约31% on WebShop", "setting": "对比7条独立轨迹"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 使用学生不确定性与师生不一致选择信息丰富的决策点，从共享前缀重采样学生策略延续形成分支。；对主轨迹和分支同时施加token级教师监督，扩展有限采样预算下的监督覆盖。；共享前缀复用减少学生生成token成本（ALFWorld约19%，WebShop约31%，对比7条独立轨迹）。；提出协议边界修复（PBR），在协议约束下进行针对性监督，缓解重复与浪费生成。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides teacher supervision on student-generated trajectories, but a single trajectory realizes only one continuation at each decision, limiting supervision coverage under a finite sampling budget. We propose BranchOPD (Branching On-Policy Distillation), which selects informative decision points using student uncertainty and teacher-student disagreement, resamples student-policy continuations from shared prefixes, and applies token-level teacher supervision to both main trajectories and branches. BranchOPD operates at token-level decisions in mathematics and within-turn decisions in agent tasks. Shared-prefix reuse reduces student-generated token costs by approximately 19% on ALFWorld and 31% on WebShop relative to seven independent trajectories. We also introduce protocol-boundary repair (PBR), which uses targeted supervision under protocol constraints to mitigate repetition and wasted generation. BranchOPD outperforms Vanilla OPD and Multi-Rollout OPD (n=7) across all evaluated benchmarks, with gains over Vanilla OPD of 18.55 percentage points in mean mathematics accuracy over 16 sampled answers (avg@16) and 13.8 points in mean ALFWorld ID/OOD success. Mechanistic analyses and selection ablations show that branching at uncertain decisions expands subsequent behavior coverage and outperforms random selection and turn-initial branching.

</details>


### [27] [Rollout-Time Intervention: Unlocking Agentic Reinforcement Learning Beyond Policy Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13438)
*Chen Xi,Ding Ruixue,Wang Zhigaoyuan,Zhang Fanrui,Zhang Qiang,Chen Boli,Wang Shihang,Wang Tianfu,Ren Ailin,Wang Pengyong,Xie Pengjun,Xiong Hui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RTI在rollout阶段利用评估rubric将失败轨迹转化为成功轨迹，突破on-policy推理瓶颈，在五个基准上一致优于RL基线并超越原有能力边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体RL受限于on-policy推理瓶颈：当任务超出模型当前能力边界时，有效轨迹极为稀疏，训练常崩溃。

Method: RTI在智能体强化学习的rollout阶段介入。输入为交互环境中的任务，策略模型生成初始轨迹；由于任务可能超出策略能力，初始轨迹常为错误或失败。RTI利用奖励侧证据（即评估rubric）在rollout时对错误轨迹进行干预，将其转换为成功轨迹，从而在策略极少自行解决的困难任务上产生有效学习信号。这些干预轨迹本质上是off-policy的，会引入分布偏移；方法通过过滤掉与当前策略分布偏移过大的轨迹来控制风险。过滤后的干预轨迹与可能的on-policy轨迹一起用于策略优化。整体流程为：策略rollout生成轨迹→对错误轨迹施加带rubric的干预→基于分布偏移过滤→用于策略梯度更新，使后训练策略超越原有能力边界。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Rollout-Time Intervention（RTI）：在rollout阶段利用奖励侧证据（评估rubric）将错误轨迹转化为成功轨迹，提供超出策略自身探索能力的有效学习信号；发现干预轨迹虽为off-policy，但过滤掉分布偏移过大的轨迹后仍能有效指导策略优化

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a key paradigm for improving large language model (LLM) agents, enabling stronger reasoning, planning, and tool-use in interactive environments. However, existing agent RL methods remain fundamentally constrained by an on-policy reasoning bottleneck: when tasks exceed the model's current capability boundary, effective trajectories become extremely sparse and training often collapses. We introduce Rollout-Time Intervention (RTI), a technique designed to elicit successful trajectories beyond the policy's intrinsic exploration capacity. RTI operates by leveraging reward-side evidence (i.e., evaluation rubrics) during rollout to convert erroneous trajectories into successful ones, providing effective learning signals for tasks that the policy rarely solves on its own. Although intervention trajectories are inherently off-policy, we find that they can effectively guide policy optimization when trajectories with excessive distributional shifts are filtered out. Extensive experiments across five benchmarks demonstrate that RTI consistently outperforms RL baselines, enabling the post-trained policy to approach the performance of substantially stronger contemporary LLMs and, more importantly, transcend its original capability boundary. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [28] [SWE-Critix: Post-Training Generalist Verifiers for Assessing Coding-Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13356)
*Jiajun Hu,Chun Yong Chong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SWE-Critix，一个基于SFT和RL的后训练管道，训练通用验证器来评估编码代理的长执行轨迹。验证器预测完成标签并生成解释，在留出测试集上将分类准确率从57%提升到78%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何训练一个通用的验证器，能够可靠地判断并解释编码代理在长视野软件工程任务中的执行轨迹是否成功。

Method: SWE-Critix是一个后训练管道，用于训练编码代理轨迹的通用验证器。输入为一个任务及其对应的执行轨迹，轨迹可能包含数百次与代码仓库和外部工具的迭代交互。验证器基于Qwen3-30B-A3B推理模型，首先进行监督微调（SFT），然后在CodeForge-Preview轨迹上用强化学习（RL）继续训练。训练后，模型接收任务和轨迹，预测完成标签（分类）并生成自然语言分析来解释其判断。该设计旨在解决编码代理在长视野软件工程任务中缺乏oracle评估器的问题，现有通用LLM验证器缺乏专门训练，而现有专用评论器主要产生标量奖励，缺乏解释性。通过SFT和RL，模型能够更好地处理长轨迹并给出可解释的验证结果。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "分类准确率", "value": "从57%提升至66%", "setting": "在留出测试集上，基础Qwen3-30B-A3B推理模型经过SFT后的准确率"}, {"metric": "分类准确率", "value": "从66%提升至78%", "setting": "在留出测试集上，经过SFT的模型进一步通过RL训练后的准确率"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出基于SFT和RL的后训练流程，用于训练通用编码代理轨迹验证器；验证器在给定任务和轨迹后，同时预测完成标签并生成自然语言分析来解释判断；在CodeForge-Preview轨迹上训练了一系列30B模型，并公开模型、数据和训练代码

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly deployed for end-to-end software engineering tasks, where a readily available oracle evaluator that defines the success criteria is often absent. This motivates verifications for coding agents that can reliably judge and explain their behavior. However, verification is non-trivial as coding agents are increasingly capable of solving long-horizon software engineering tasks. Their execution trajectories can be extremely long, containing hundreds of iterative interactions with code repositories and external tools. Existing verifiers either rely on general-purpose LLMs without specialized training or train dedicated critics that predominantly focus on producing scalar rewards, highlighting the relatively limited exploration of training-based generalist verification. We introduce SWE-Critix, a post-training pipeline for training generalist verifiers of coding-agent trajectories. Given a task and its execution trajectory, the learned verifier predicts the completion label and generates a natural-language analysis that explains its judgment. Our pipeline combines supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with reinforcement learning (RL) and trains a series of 30B models on trajectories from CodeForge-Preview. On a held-out test set, SWE-Critix improves classification accuracy from 57% for the base Qwen3-30B-A3B reasoning model to 66% after SFT and 78% after RL. We release our models, datasets, and training code to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [29] [Quality, Not Just Outcome: A Structural Scoring Framework for Selecting Coding-Agent Trajectories for Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13152)
*Sida Li,Yuanxin Liu,Shicheng Li,Linli Yao,Xu Sun,Haoli Bai,Xiaohui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TQS——一种无需LLM判官、线性时间的编码智能体轨迹结构打分框架，通过信息聚焦度、意图-动作对齐和策略动态三类信号筛选监督微调数据；在15.5万条成功轨迹上按TQS排序后微调，模型在SWE-Bench-Verified上优于随机或低分子集训练，说明过程级质量过滤是LLM数据选择的可扩展替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体监督微调仅按最终补丁是否通过测试套件进行结果级过滤，忽略了成功轨迹中仍可能存在的低效交互模式（重复文件检查、工具使用不稳定、延迟修改等），这些过程质量问题会被模仿学习继承。

Method: TQS直接作用于编码智能体交互轨迹的结构，不依赖LLM评判模型。输入为完整的智能体轨迹（包含工具调用、文件检查、修改动作等交互步骤序列），输出为可排序的质量分数。框架通过三类结构化收敛信号刻画过程质量：信息聚焦度衡量轨迹是否围绕目标文件/模块集中获取信息，避免重复或发散的文件检查；意图-动作对齐衡量模型声明的意图与后续实际工具调用和代码修改的一致性；策略动态衡量交互过程的稳定性与收敛速度，例如是否出现工具使用不稳定或延迟修改。打分流程在结构信号上聚合为单一分数，计算复杂度与轨迹长度呈线性关系，因此可大规模筛选数据。训练流程为：从公开语料获取155,144条最终补丁通过测试的成功轨迹，用TQS对其排序，分别取排名靠前和靠后的子集（以及随机子集作为对照）对编码智能体进行监督微调，最终在SWE-Bench-Verified上评估。该设计的核心在于把监督信号从仅关注最终结果扩展到关注过程质量，使模仿学习目标包含更高效、更稳定的交互模式。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "SWE-Bench-Verified通过率", "value": "使用TQS高分轨迹微调的模型持续优于随机或低分子集训练的模型（摘要未给出具体数值）", "setting": "155,144条公开开放许可成功轨迹，按TQS排序后选取子集进行监督微调，在SWE-Bench-Verified评测"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出TQS（Trajectory Quality Score）结构化评分框架，直接在轨迹结构上运行，无需LLM判官，线性时间复杂度；围绕结构化收敛组织质量信号：信息聚焦度、意图-动作对齐、策略动态；在155,144条公开许可语料成功轨迹上按TQS排序，用排序子集微调编码智能体，验证过程级质量筛选优于随机或底部子集

Abstract: Open coding agents are commonly trained through supervised fine-tuning on interaction trajectories whose final patches pass the benchmark test suite. However, this outcome-level filtering criterion ignores process quality: trajectories that eventually succeed may still contain inefficient interaction patterns such as repetitive file inspection, unstable tool usage, or delayed modification behavior, all of which become part of the imitation target. We propose TQS (Trajectory Quality Score), a structural scoring framework for coding-agent trajectories that operates directly on trajectory structure, requires no LLM judge, and runs in linear time. TQS characterizes trajectory quality through structural signals organized around structured convergence: information focus, intent--action alignment, and policy dynamics. Using 155,144 successful trajectories from a public open-license corpus, we rank trajectories by TQS and fine-tune coding agents on filtered subsets. Models trained on top-ranked trajectories consistently outperform those trained on random or bottom-ranked subsets on SWE-Bench-Verified. Our results suggest that process-level trajectory quality is an important factor in coding-agent supervision and that structural trajectory filtering provides an effective and scalable alternative to LLM-based data selection.

</details>


### [30] [AdaC-GRPO: Towards Mastering Long-Horizon Visual Sudoku Puzzles via Adaptive Curriculum Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13184)
*Sida Li,Yuanxin Liu,Linli Yao,Shicheng Li,Xu Sun,Haoli Bai,Xiaohui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 FG-Sudoku 细粒度视觉数独基准，并针对长时程多模态推理中奖励稀疏问题提出 AdaC-GRPO：利用在线 rollout 准确率动态追踪模型最近发展区并构造课程批次。实验使 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 接近 Gemini-2.5-Flash，将到 Gemini-2.5-Pro 的整体准确率差距缩小 62.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长时程多模态推理任务中，标准 RLVR 因任务成功率接近零而产生严重奖励稀疏，导致强化学习训练停滞；如何动态构造训练课程以持续提供可学习信号。

Method: 论文以多模态大语言模型（Qwen2.5-VL-7B、InternVL-3.5、Qwen3-VL）为基础，输入为视觉数独图像及其问题描述，模型通过多步演绎推理输出答案。训练流程基于 GRPO：对每个输入采样一组 rollout，依据可验证答案计算组内相对奖励进行策略优化。由于 9*9 等复杂数独上模型成功率接近零，标准 RLVR 奖励稀疏。AdaC-GRPO 维护在线 rollout 准确率统计，以估计模型当前能力的最近发展区（ZPD），并据此动态选择或构造训练批次，使每个 batch 落在既非过易也非过难的难度区间，从而持续产生有效梯度。课程难度随模型准确率变化而自适应调整，而非使用固定或先验难度表。评估方面，FG-Sudoku 按网格尺寸与所需演绎步数分档，用于诊断局部约束与全局依赖链的推理能力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "整体准确率差距缩小比例", "value": "62.2%", "setting": "Qwen2.5-VL-7B 经 AdaC-GRPO 后向 Gemini-2.5-Pro 的总体准确率差距缩小；同时接近 Gemini-2.5-Flash 表现"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 FG-Sudoku 细粒度基准，按网格尺寸和所需推理步数分类评估 MLLM 长时程视觉数独能力；提出 AdaC-GRPO，通过在线 rollout 准确率追踪模型最近发展区（ZPD），动态构造训练批次；在奖励稀疏条件下验证自适应课程 GRPO 训练的可行性，并跨 InternVL-3.5 与 Qwen3-VL 验证泛化性

Abstract: While reinforcement learning with verifiable reward (RLVR) enhances reasoning in large language models, its efficacy in long-horizon multimodal tasks remains underexplored. Visual Sudoku puzzles pose a representative challenge, requiring multi-step deductive reasoning over both local constraints and global dependency chains. To this end, we introduce FG-Sudoku, a fine-grained benchmark categorized by grid size and required deductive steps. Evaluations reveal that frontier MLLMs fail on complex long-range dependencies (e.g., 9*9 puzzles). During post-training, this near-zero success rate causes severe reward sparsity, halting standard RLVR. We propose Adaptive Curriculum Group Relative Policy Optimization (AdaC-GRPO) to overcome this. Unlike rigid or prior-based schedules, AdaC-GRPO dynamically constructs training batches by tracking the model's "Zone of Proximal Development" via online rollout accuracy. Experiments demonstrate that AdaC-GRPO enables Qwen2.5-VL-7B to approach the performance of Gemini-2.5-Flash while bridging 62.2% of the overall accuracy gap to Gemini-2.5-Pro, with its generalizability further validated across InternVL-3.5 and Qwen3-VL.

</details>


### [31] [Forgetting is Not Always Bad: A Neuro-Inspired Memory Repair Mechanism for Poisoned LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13267)
*Lei Liu,Yunji Liang,Xiaowen Zhang,Jingqi Liu,Qi Li,Bin Guo,Zhiwen Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DREAM 是受神经科学启发的免训练 LLM Agent 记忆修复框架，通过扰动再激活、多维激活异常诊断和动态抑制/强化三阶段，选择性遗忘被投毒记忆。实验显示对后门投毒攻击成功率降低超95%，保持良性效用，较 A-MemGuard 最高加速2.13倍、token 消耗降低37.7%，多智能体污染场景任务成功率达92.96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM Agent 记忆投毒防御依赖外部静态隔离或持续在线审计，导致记忆效率低、计算开销高；如何在免训练、Agent 内生的条件下选择性遗忘有害记忆同时保留良性记忆。

Method: DREAM 是一个免训练的 Agent 内生记忆修复框架，其端到端流程分为三个阶段。输入为 LLM Agent 的持久化记忆存储及其在任务执行中产生的激活模式。第一阶段“扰动诱导隐式再激活”通过对记忆施加扰动，使恶意记忆在缺乏强化的情况下发生去稳定化，从而暴露其结构脆弱性；该机制受神经科学中弱再激活导致记忆衰减的启发。第二阶段“自适应异常诊断”基于多维激活模式检测记忆的拓扑异常，以识别被投毒或受损的记忆条目。第三阶段“动态记忆修复”根据诊断结果选择性抑制有害记忆，同时强化良性记忆，实现功能性遗忘。整个流程不涉及模型参数训练，而是直接在记忆层面操作，最终在推理/服务时使用修复后的记忆以完成真实任务，并支持多智能体系统中的记忆传播防护。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "攻击成功率降低", "value": "超过95%", "setting": "针对后门投毒攻击，跨多种攻击者和真实任务"}, {"metric": "运行时加速", "value": "最高2.13倍", "setting": "与 A-MemGuard 相比"}, {"metric": "token 消耗降低", "value": "37.7%", "setting": "与 A-MemGuard 相比"}, {"metric": "任务成功率", "value": "92.96%", "setting": "受污染的多智能体系统"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 受神经科学弱再激活可导致记忆去稳定与衰减的启发，提出免训练的 Agent 内生记忆修复框架 DREAM。；三阶段流水线：扰动诱导隐式再激活以暴露恶意记忆的结构脆弱性；基于多维激活模式的自适应异常诊断以检测拓扑异常；动态记忆修复模块选择性抑制有害记忆并强化良性记忆。；在多种攻击者和真实任务上验证，对后门投毒攻击成功率降低超过95%，同时保持良性效用。；相比 A-MemGuard，运行时最高加速2.13倍，token 消耗降低37.7%。；在受污染的多智能体系统中达到92.96%的任务成功率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are highly vulnerable to memory-poisoning attacks. Existing defenses primarily rely on external modules for either static memory isolation or continuous online auditing, resulting in low memory efficiency and high computational overhead. To address these limitations, inspired by neuroscientific mechanisms that weak reactivation can destabilize memories and induce decay in the absence of reinforcement, we propose an agent-intrinsic training-free memory repair framework, DREAM (Dynamic Reactivation for Engram Attenuation in Memory), that enables selective functional forgetting of poisoned memories. Specifically, DREAM implements a three-stage pipeline: perturbation-induced implicit reactivation to reveal the structural fragility of malicious memories, adaptive anomaly diagnosis based on multi-dimensional activation patterns to detect topological anomalies, and a dynamic memory repair module that selectively suppresses harmful memories while reinforcing benign ones. Extensive experiments across diverse attackers and real-world tasks demonstrate that DREAM reduces attack success rates by over 95\% against backdoor poisoning while preserving strong benign utility. DREAM also achieves competitive robustness against injection attacks. In terms of runtime and token consumption, DREAM achieves up to a 2.13$\times$ speedup and a 37.7\% reduction in token consumption compared with A-MemGuard. Furthermore, DREAM also achieves a task success rate of 92.96\% on poisoned multi-agent systems.

</details>

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出两阶段胜任度感知的多智能体编排框架：先估计LLM骨干对当前查询的胜任度，再据此自适应调整角色激活与通信图。六个基准平均93.81%；高能力骨干准确率87.22→89.97且token成本降36.26%，低能力骨干较现有方法再涨4.58点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何根据LLM骨干网络在当前查询上的胜任度，自适应决定多智能体协作的程度（角色数量与通信拓扑），以在困难任务上获得协调收益的同时避免不必要的token开销与冗余交互。

Method: 该框架为两阶段胜任度感知的多智能体编排。第一阶段：针对当前输入查询，对给定LLM骨干网络建模其任务条件胜任度，即估计该骨干在当前查询上独立完成的可靠程度，作为后续编排的决策信号。第二阶段：依据该胜任度信号自适应地决定角色激活集合与智能体之间的通信图结构——对高胜任度查询减少协作、抑制冗余交互以节省token，对低胜任度查询增强协调以弥补能力不足。系统随后按激活的角色与通信图执行多智能体推理并汇总答案。论文未披露胜任度建模的具体实现（如分类器、置信度估计或提示式判断）、通信图的构建规则、训练目标与优化细节；实验部分在六个基准与六个不同能力级别的LLM骨干上评估，并额外测试未见骨干以验证自适应趋势的泛化性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均得分", "value": "93.81%", "setting": "六个基准数据集平均"}, {"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "87.22 -> 89.97", "setting": "高胜任度骨干模型（六个骨干中的高能力组）"}, {"metric": "token成本", "value": "降低36.26%", "setting": "高胜任度骨干模型"}, {"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "提升4.58点", "setting": "低胜任度骨干模型，相对现有方法的进一步增益"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 两阶段胜任度感知的MAS自动编排框架：先建模骨干模型对当前查询的任务条件胜任度，再据此调整协作；以胜任度信号驱动角色激活与通信图的自适应变化，实现协作强度随能力缩放（高能力骨干更少协作、低能力骨干更多协作）；在六个骨干模型与未见骨干上验证了协作强度随推断胜任度自适应的泛化趋势，并报告精度-成本权衡

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have emerged as a promising paradigm for enhancing complex reasoning through role specialization and collaboration, yet a key challenge remains: how can collaboration be adapted to a backbone's competence on the current query? This problem matters because more collaboration is not always better--richer coordination may help on difficult cases, but it also incurs additional token cost and redundant interactions. We propose a two-stage competence-aware framework for automatic MAS orchestration. It first models the LLM backbone's task-conditioned competence for the current query, and then uses this signal to adapt role activation and the communication graph. Across six benchmarks, our method achieves an average score of 93.81%. Experiments with six LLM backbones of varying competence levels further show that our method adjusts collaboration according to the inferred competence: for higher-competence backbones, it improves average accuracy from 87.22 to 89.97 while reducing token cost by 36.26%; for lower-competence backbones, it yields a further average gain of 4.58 points over existing methods. The same trend also holds on unseen backbones.

</details>


### [33] [DP-MIND: Differentially private memory-guided Long context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13132)
*Zhihao Liu,Yitian Cheng,Shuang Liu,Rui Wen,Yuke Hu,Zhan Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DP-MIND 提出记忆引导的差分隐私长上下文推理框架，通过本地扰动、分块处理、RL优化的鲁棒记忆和本地去噪，将DP推理扩展到百万token上下文，并给出理论隐私分析与多基准实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私推理方法面向短或中等上下文，难以扩展到百万token级输入，受限于上下文容量且DP扰动导致严重效用下降。

Method: DP-MIND 采用记忆引导的分块处理架构：输入为用户查询及可能含敏感信息的长上下文，在本地对输入进行差分隐私扰动，然后将扰动后的长上下文按块依次送入外部LLM。跨块之间维护一个紧凑记忆，用于保留任务相关信息，避免依赖完整上下文窗口。记忆构建策略通过强化学习直接在扰动输入上优化，奖励信号结合最终答案正确性与记忆一致性，使记忆对DP噪声更具鲁棒性。对于被扰动破坏的答案片段，使用本地去噪模块进行恢复，该模块在本地运行，不将原始上下文暴露给外部模型。系统提供理论隐私分析，推理路径为本地扰动→分块处理与记忆更新→外部模型生成→本地去噪。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "utility/scalability", "value": "论文声称在多个百万token级长上下文基准上一致优于现有DP和长上下文推理基线，但未提供具体数值", "setting": "百万token级长上下文基准，与DP及长上下文推理基线对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 记忆引导的分块长上下文差分隐私推理框架，在本地扰动输入后逐块处理并用紧凑记忆保留任务相关信息；基于强化学习在扰动输入上直接优化记忆构建策略，以最终答案正确性和记忆一致性为奖励；本地去噪模块恢复被扰动破坏的答案片段，避免将原始上下文暴露给外部模型；提供DP-MIND的理论隐私分析

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Service has become a dominant paradigm for accessing large language models without requiring local deployment. However, the process raises severe privacy concerns when user queries may include sensitive or confidential contexts. Differential privacy (DP) provides a principled approach to protecting such inputs, but existing DP inference methods are primarily designed for short- or moderate-context settings and struggle with million-token-scale inputs due to limited context capacity and severe utility degradation under DP perturbation. In this paper, we propose DP-MIND, a memory-guided framework that scales differentially private inference to million-token contexts. DP-MIND enables private long-context inference by locally perturbing the input and processing it chunk by chunk with a compact memory that preserves task-relevant information across the sequence. To make this memory robust to DP noise, we optimize the memory construction policy directly on perturbed inputs using reinforcement learning, guided by both final answer correctness and memory consistency. We further recover perturbation-corrupted answer spans through a local denoising module without exposing the original context to the external model. We provide theoretical privacy analysis of DP-MIND and further conduct extensive empirical analysis across multiple long-context benchmarks with context lengths up to the million-token scale, demonstrating that DP-MIND consistently outperforms existing DP and long-context inference baselines in both scalability and utility.

</details>


### [34] [When Faster Decisions Do Not Make Better Agents: An Empirical Study of Jev in Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13092)
*Yuchen Ling,Shengcheng Yu,Zhenyu Chen,Chunrong Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文对将高效决策模型Jev集成到5种LLM智能体工作流中进行实证研究，覆盖ALFWorld和AndroidWorld，共4500次任务执行。结果显示，Jev显著降低决策成本和延迟，但任务成功率在ALFWorld上接近基线，在AndroidWorld上两个移动智能体明显下降；辅助推理和额外交互吸收了节省，失败恢复和过早完成限制了成功交付。研究强调在集成高效决策模型时需关注进展保持、恢复支持和完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当在LLM智能体工作流中集成高效决策模型（Jev）时，局部的决策成本与延迟节省能否转化为任务完成的高效性和可靠性，还是会因辅助推理、额外交互、失败恢复和过早完成等因素而抵消。

Method: 论文未提出新的模型架构，而是研究将高效决策模型Jev集成到现有LLM智能体工作流中。智能体工作流通常包含决策模块（由LLM或Jev负责）、执行模块、状态跟踪和恢复机制。Jev作为更高效（低延迟、低成本）的决策替代方案，替换原LLM决策。每个任务迭代中，智能体根据当前状态进行决策，执行动作，观察结果，并可能触发辅助推理或额外交互。论文通过4500次任务执行，在ALFWorld（具身任务）和AndroidWorld（移动应用使用）两个环境中，评估任务有效性、决策级和任务级资源使用以及执行行为。具体模块细节未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "决策成本", "value": "显著降低", "setting": "所有5个智能体，ALFWorld和AndroidWorld"}, {"metric": "决策延迟", "value": "显著降低", "setting": "所有5个智能体，ALFWorld和AndroidWorld"}, {"metric": "任务成功率", "value": "在ALFWorld上接近基线，在AndroidWorld上两个移动智能体显著下降", "setting": "与基线智能体对比"}, {"metric": "每次成功结果的平均成本", "value": "在一个智能体中增加，即使每次尝试的成本下降", "setting": "任务级成本分析"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首次对Jev在智能体工作流中的效率转移进行大规模实证研究，覆盖5种智能体和2种应用场景（ALFWorld和AndroidWorld）；联合评估任务有效性、决策级和任务级资源使用以及执行行为的多维度分析框架；揭示了决策节省被辅助推理和额外交互吸收的机制，以及失败恢复和过早完成对成功交付的限制

Abstract: AI agents depend on repeated decisions to accomplish tasks, with large language models (LLMs) commonly serving as their core decision makers. The recent emergence of Jev offers an opportunity to make these decisions faster and at lower cost. However, it remains unclear whether such local savings can be translated into efficient and reliable task completion. In this paper, we investigate this efficiency transfer across five agents in two representative application settings, embodied tasks in ALFWorld and mobile app use in AndroidWorld. Across 4,500 task executions, we jointly evaluate task effectiveness, decision-level and task-level resource use, and execution behavior. Experimental results show that Jev integration substantially reduces decision cost and latency across all five agents, with task-level gains varying by workflow. Task success remains close to baseline levels on ALFWorld and declines more substantially in two mobile agents. Auxiliary reasoning and additional interactions absorb decision savings, while failed recovery and premature completion limit successful delivery. Mean cost per successful outcome increases in one agent even as spending per attempt falls. These findings highlight the importance of preserving progress, supporting recovery, and verifying completion when integrating efficient decision models into agent workflows. Their practical value should be assessed through successful task delivery, recovery behavior, and resources per delivered outcome.

</details>


### [35] [Hindsight Structured Advice Distillation: Steering Executor Agents with Advisors Trained on Their Failures and Task Characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13000)
*O. Ozan Koyluoglu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HSAD方法，训练一个小型advisor模型为冻结的executor agent生成输入相关的结构化建议。在FrontierFinance上，8B advisor将Samaya金融研究agent的合格率从52.9%提升至56.6%，相比agent自身的高努力配置降低37%查询成本，并在WebShop、BIRD text-to-SQL、ExpertLongBench上展示泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结或闭源/共享的executor agent能力足够但常遗漏任务细节，重训练成本高且不可行；将所有缺失知识写入固定prompt会造成prompt冗长且对所有输入相同，无法提供输入特定的指导。

Method: HSAD采用advisor-executor分离架构。输入为每个任务实例（如金融研究查询），由一个小规模advisor模型读取该输入并生成结构化的文本建议。建议被追加到冻结的executor agent的prompt中，executor据此产生产出。advisor的训练分阶段：首先在带分级答案的训练池上运行executor，分析其失败案例，并用多种策略运行executor以观察每种策略在各输入上的效果；然后由一个能看到评分和轨迹的教师模型写出能够帮助该输入的理想建议；最后advisor通过监督微调学习仅从输入生成这样的结构化建议。训练目标是最大化生成建议与教师编写建议的相似度。推理时，advisor先于executor运行，产生输入相关的指导，executor在冻结参数下完成生成。该设计避免修改或重训练大型executor，降低训练成本且推理延迟增加很小。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "Agent qualification rate", "value": "52.9% -> 56.6%", "setting": "FrontierFinance公开基准，8B advisor配合Samaya proprietary research agent"}, {"metric": "Cost per query", "value": "37% lower", "setting": "对比该agent自身的高努力配置"}]}

Conclusion: Hindsight Structured Advice Distillation：利用带评分答案的训练池分析executor失败、尝试多种策略并让教师模型基于评分与轨迹写出本应有效的结构化建议；训练一个独立的小型advisor模型，通过监督微调从输入单独生成建议，追加到冻结executor的prompt；将advisor与executor分离，降低训练成本并减少推理延迟

Abstract: Language-model agents are used for specialized work such as financial research, healthcare and law. Such agents are often capable enough for a task but still miss its details. In finance, for example, these details include which filing reports a figure or how a company discloses a metric. Retraining the agent to learn these details is expensive, and often impossible when the agent is a closed or shared system. Writing the missing know-how into the agent's prompt makes the prompt long and identical for every input, although different inputs need different guidance. We propose to learn to generate this guidance instead. A small advisor model reads each input and writes structured advice, which is appended to the prompt of a frozen executor. The advisor is trained with hindsight, following work on distillation with privileged information. On a training pool with graded answers, we analyse the executor's failures, run it under several strategies to see which one worked on each input, and let a teacher that sees the grades and the traces write the advice that would have helped. The advisor then learns, by supervised fine-tuning, to write that advice from the input alone. Because the student is a separate small model rather than the executor, it is cheap to train and adds little latency. We call this approach hindsight structured advice distillation (HSAD). On FrontierFinance, a public benchmark of expert-written financial research queries, an 8B advisor raises the qualification rate of Samaya's proprietary research agent, which already leads the benchmark's leaderboard, from 52.9% to 56.6%. This is above the agent's own high-effort configuration, at 37% lower cost per query. We study the generalization of the proposed approach, and in particular show that HSAD also improves results on WebShop, BIRD text-to-SQL and ExpertLongBench.

</details>

*Yuheng Wang,Runde Yang,Zhuohan Bao,Qiran Zhang,Huatao Li,Yiyang Zheng,Lin Wu,Jingru Fan,Shu Yao,Yihan Li,Zhi Li,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出模型切片与金字塔监督的协同学习框架，用多个异构小模型协作替代大模型推理，在四类任务上性能不降且推理效率高于多智能体基线，但未披露具体模型规模与在线部署证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一大模型在推理时存在误差传播和推理盲点，后续步骤依赖先前输出难以独立重新评估。

Method: 该工作将一个大模型替换为多个异构小模型，在相近参数预算下形成协作体。输入经过多个异构模型分别处理，产生初步推理结果；通过金字塔监督（结合协议蒸馏与步骤级监督）训练模型间的交互策略，使各模型能够重新评估和修正先前的推理步骤。协作过程由学习得到的交互决策控制，最终输出经过多模型协商后的答案。训练目标包括协议蒸馏（将大模型或协作协议的知识蒸馏到各小模型）和步骤级监督（对推理中间步骤进行细粒度监督）。推理时，多个小模型根据学习到的协作模式进行交互，任务不同表现出差异化的协作模式（例如由某个模型主导或交叉验证）。该架构旨在缓解单模型误差传播，同时保持推理效率优于现有多智能体基线。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "任务性能", "value": "可比或优于原始大模型及已建立的多智能体基线（包括基于强化学习的方法）", "setting": "四类任务：多跳问答、数学推理、代码生成、开放约束生成"}, {"metric": "推理效率", "value": "高于多智能体基线", "setting": "未披露具体数值"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 模型切片：在可比参数预算下用多个异构小模型替代一个大模型，使先验推理能在不同参数化下重新评估；金字塔监督：结合协议蒸馏与步骤级监督，优化协作过程中的推理与交互决策；学习到的异构模型间协作模式，根据不同任务表现出差异化的协作行为

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel across diverse tasks, benefiting from knowledge priors, in-context reasoning, and test-time computation. Despite these strengths, single-model reasoning remains vulnerable to error propagation, as later steps depend on the model's earlier outputs, leaving reasoning blind spots difficult to independently reassess. To address this limitation, we develop \textit{collaborative learning}, encompassing the construction of heterogeneous collaborators and the learning of effective cross-model collaboration. Specifically, \textit{model slicing} replaces a large model with smaller, heterogeneous models under a comparable parameter budget, enabling prior reasoning to be reassessed across distinct model parameterizations. \textit{Pyramid supervision} combines protocol distillation with step-level supervision to refine reasoning and interaction decisions throughout collaboration. We evaluate our approach across four task categories: multi-hop question answering, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and open-ended constrained generation. The trained heterogeneous models achieve comparable or better task performance than the original large model and established multi-agent baselines, including reinforcement-learning-based methods, while also achieving higher inference efficiency than the multi-agent baselines and exhibiting differentiated collaboration patterns across tasks. These findings show that learned collaboration turns model heterogeneity into complementary reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [37] [Learning More by Training Less: Uncertainty-Guided Progressive Contraction for Efficient Reasoning Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12933)
*Suorong Yang,Jiaxin Wang,Jiaming Su,Chentao Yue,Furao Shen,Peng Ye,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出不确定性引导的渐进收缩框架，通过蒙特卡洛推理树构造过程级不确定性，两阶段收缩监督数据和优化路由，使推理后训练仅需0.22%的优化预算、超450倍更少训练曝光，同时保持或提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在推理后训练中大幅压缩监督数据和优化预算，同时不牺牲甚至提升LLM的推理能力。

Method: 给定教师生成的链式思维（CoT），不将其视为单一实现路径，而是围绕中间推理状态构建蒙特卡洛推理树，从而暴露过程级不确定性。基于该不确定性，第一阶段将原始监督语料收缩为一个紧凑的教师知情池，过滤掉对学习价值较低或不确定性过高的轨迹。第二阶段在SFT过程中进行学习者感知的在线路由，根据学生模型不断变化的学习状态动态选择优化样本，集中优化最有价值的监督。两阶段渐进收缩同时压缩监督信号和优化过程，减少学生模型训练预算。训练目标为标准的监督微调，但优化过程由不确定性引导的路由控制。推理时与常规LLM推理流程一致，无额外推理模块。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "优化预算占比", "value": "低至0.22%", "setting": "相对于全量数据SFT的优化预算"}, {"metric": "训练曝光量减少", "value": "超过450倍更少", "setting": "相对于全量数据SFT"}, {"metric": "推理性能", "value": "保持或提升", "setting": "多样推理基准和LLM家族"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 不确定性画像的推理监督构造：围绕中间推理状态构建蒙特卡洛推理树，暴露过程级不确定性。；双阶段渐进收缩：第一阶段用不确定性将原始语料收缩为紧凑教师知情池，第二阶段在SFT中做学习者感知的在线路由，根据学生模型演化状态集中优化。；在异构学生模型族、黑盒教师和弱到强迁移场景下验证泛化性。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved remarkable advances in complex reasoning. However, scaling reasoning post-training with ever-larger corpora can substantially increase training cost without proportional performance gains. In this study, we therefore ask: how far can reasoning supervision be compressed without sacrificing capability? To this end, we propose an uncertainty-guided progressive contraction framework that concentrates supervision and optimization on the reasoning trajectories most valuable for learning. Specifically, rather than treating a teacher-generated chain-of-thought (CoT) as a single realized path, we construct uncertainty-profiled reasoning supervision by building Monte Carlo reasoning trees around intermediate reasoning states to expose process-level uncertainty. This uncertainty first contracts the original corpus into a compact teacher-informed pool. A second, learner-aware contraction then performs online routing during SFT, concentrating optimization according to the student's evolving learning state. Together, the two stages progressively contract both supervision and optimization, substantially reducing the student training budget. Experiments across diverse reasoning benchmarks and LLM families show that our method uses as little as 0.22% of the optimization budget of full-data SFT, over 450x less training exposure, while maintaining or improving reasoning performance. Notably, our framework generalizes well across heterogeneous student families, black-box teachers, and weak-to-strong transfer. This indicates that it is not only a way to accelerate training but can also enhance reasoning capability.

</details>


### [38] [EvoBelief: Falsifiable World Models for Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12789)
*Janet Wang,Jiang Guo,Yi Xiang,Jiaqing Yuan,Mingwen Dong,Hideo Kobayashi,Shuaichen Chang,Chandan K. Reddy,Jiarong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoBelief 提出用可证伪假设表示 LLM agent 的环境知识，通过意外结果触发假设、观察与受控实验验证、反例修正，在冻结 LLM 下实现部署期自进化。ALFWorld 成功率较匹配的 Frozen World Model 分别提升 23.9% 和 31.3%，DiscoveryWorld Reactor Lab 完成 83.3% Normal 与 58.3% Challenge，基线均为 0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使具备程序化能力的 LLM agent，也会因对环境动态的信念不完整、错误或过时而失败；论文研究在部署阶段自进化这些信念，同时避免错误信念跨任务传播。

Method: EvoBelief 将环境知识表示为可证伪假设。输入为 agent 与环境的交互轨迹、观察结果和任务上下文；核心模块按流程包括：假设形成模块、验证模块、规则库和规划/预测接口。假设形成由意外结果（预测与实际不符）和决策相关未知触发；每条规则以假设形式写入规则库，并记录其证据来源和它所影响的决策。验证模块利用后续观察或受控实验对规则进行独立验证，只有通过验证的规则才可在其支持范围内指导预测或规划。当出现反例时，规则被缩小作用域或淘汰，从而适应环境变化。语言模型保持冻结，不更新参数；推理时，系统检索与当前任务相关的已验证规则，结合冻结 LLM 进行规划或预测，并将新观察结果反馈给验证模块，形成闭环。论文未披露具体网络结构、训练目标、参数规模、硬件、延迟或吞吐。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "ALFWorld success rate improvement over matched Frozen World Model", "value": "23.9% with Claude Haiku 4.5; 31.3% with GPT-5.4", "setting": "ALFWorld without actor-side transition memory"}, {"metric": "DiscoveryWorld Reactor Lab completion", "value": "83.3% Normal-tier; 58.3% Challenge-tier; baselines 0% for each evaluated baseline", "setting": "DiscoveryWorld Reactor Lab"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将环境知识表示为可证伪假设，而非不可检验的提示/技能记忆。；由意外结果和决策相关未知触发假设形成，并通过后续观察与受控实验独立验证后才允许用于预测或规划。；用反例缩小或淘汰规则，使世界模型在环境漂移时自动修正。；语言模型保持冻结，规则与证据及其改变的决策显式关联，实现跨任务规则复用。

Abstract: Self-evolving LLM agents typically improve how they act through accumulated experience, reflection, or skills. Yet even procedurally capable agents can fail when their beliefs about environment dynamics are incomplete, incorrect, or outdated. We study a complementary axis of self-evolution: evolving those beliefs during deployment. Such beliefs can support multiple tasks, but incorrect ones can also propagate across them. We introduce EvoBelief, a self-evolving world model that represents environment knowledge as falsifiable hypotheses. Unexpected outcomes and decision-relevant unknowns trigger hypothesis formation. Later observations and controlled experiments provide independent validation before a rule can guide predictions or plans within its supported scope. Counterexamples narrow or retire rules as the environment changes. The language model remains frozen, and each rule remains linked to its evidence and the decisions it changes. Across four benchmarks spanning static dynamics, hidden drift, and scientific discovery, EvoBelief improves task performance and reuses learned rules across tasks. On ALFWorld without actor-side transition memory, it improves success over the matched Frozen World Model by 23.9% and 31.3% with Claude Haiku 4.5 and GPT-5.4, respectively. On MiniGrid, it revises stale rules and recovers after unannounced dynamics changes. In DiscoveryWorld's Reactor Lab, it discovers and validates quantitative laws, completing 83.3% of Normal-tier and 58.3% of Challenge-tier instances, versus 0% for each evaluated baseline. These results demonstrate self-improvement through testable, revisable environment knowledge rather than model-parameter updates.

</details>


### [39] [A Privileged Teacher That Only Scores Cannot Search: Critique-Conditioned On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13744)
*Mayug Maniparambil,Ayush Rai,Ekansh Verma,Noel E. O'Connor,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文分析了on-policy自蒸馏中评分教师信号无法定位错误并纠正推理的问题，提出critique-conditioned自蒸馏：先生成critique再条件化评分教师，使蒸馏信号具有定位和纠正能力，并在测试时恢复错误rollout及冷启动GRPO中提供优势信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy自蒸馏中，仅依赖参考解进行评分的教师信号（全分布KL或token对数比）是否能定位错误并提供纠正性信用分配。

Method: 论文方法分为两个阶段：第一阶段，学生模型对问题生成rollout；第二阶段，一个教师模型（与被训练模型相同，但条件化于参考解）对学生的rollout进行评分。自蒸馏目标使用两种信号：全分布KL和token对数比，将教师的分布偏移蒸馏回学生策略。作者首先用gold错误span测试这些信号是否定位错误并纠正错误token，发现均不能。随后提出critique-conditioned自蒸馏：先让模型基于当前rollout生成一个critique，再将此critique作为条件输入给评分教师，使教师评分信号能定位错误、在错误span内具有纠正性，并推动学生概率向回溯词（如'Wait'、'But'、'Actually'）偏移。该方法还用于测试时：将critique放入上下文恢复错误rollout；以及冷启动GRPO：为可验证奖励无法提供优势的prompt提供优势信号。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 通过gold错误span分析揭示评分式教师信号既不能定位错误也不能将概率推离错误token，且无法可靠区分正确与错误rollout。；提出critique-conditioned self-distillation：先生成critique，再让评分教师基于critique进行条件化评分，使信号能定位错误、在错误span内具有纠正性，并将概率推向'Wait'、'But'、'Actually'等回溯词。；展示critique在测试时可恢复错误rollout，解决采样无法解决的数学问题；在冷启动GRPO中为可验证奖励提供无优势的prompt提供优势信号。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation promises to replace the sparse outcome signal of verifiable-reward RL with dense token-level credit: a teacher conditioned on the reference solution scores the student's own rollout, and the resulting distributional shift is distilled back into the policy. We investigate whether this scoring signal actually contains corrective credit. Using gold error spans in student rollouts, we test the two signals consumed by self-distillation objectives, the full-distribution KL and the token log-ratio, for two properties: whether they localize errors and whether they push probability away from erroneous student tokens. They exhibit neither of these properties nor can they reliably distinguish correct from incorrect rollouts within the same problem. We argue that this failure is structural: locating an error in a reasoning trace is itself a search problem, while a scoring teacher is never explicitly asked to perform that search. The same model, with the same privileged information, localizes errors substantially better when allowed to generate a critique. This forms the motivation for \emph{critique-conditioned self-distillation}: first generate a critique of the current rollout, then condition the scoring teacher on that critique. The resulting signal localizes errors, is corrective within error spans, and shifts probability toward tokens that initiate backtracking such as ``Wait'', ``But'', and ``Actually''. We further show that the critique is useful both at test time, where resuming a wrong rollout with the critique in context converts math problems the model cannot solve by sampling, and in cold-start GRPO, where it provides an advantage signal on prompts for which verifiable rewards alone provide none.

</details>


### [40] [CLEAT: Learning Value-Guided Clarification for Interactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12655)
*Jiatan Huang,Zheyuan Zhang,Ziming Li,Tianyi Ma,Mingchen Li,Yanfang Ye,Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLEAT 提出一种协同训练框架，让LLM智能体学习在信息不完整时主动提出高价值澄清问题，并利用答案改善任务决策。通过配对 rollout 估计残差查询价值，同时训练价值控制器和任务策略，使澄清指导随策略进化自适应。在 Qwen3-4B 上于四个基准的十个设置中取得最高平均分，并在 UserGym Travel 和 IN3 上分别提升 13.2 和 6.5 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何训练LLM智能体在面临用户未明确表达的意图和约束时，通过主动提问（澄清）获取信息并利用答案做出更好的决策，同时使提问价值估计随策略进化而自适应。

Method: CLEAT 是一个协同训练框架，包含两个核心组件：澄清价值控制器（clarification-value controller）和任务策略（task policy）。输入是多轮交互历史（用户未明确表述的目标与约束）。框架通过配对 rollout 从同一交互历史出发，分别执行“提问”和“直接执行”两种行为，得到两个任务回报。这两个回报的差值即为残差查询价值（residual query value），用于训练控制器预测提问的价值。任务策略则被训练来从候选问题中选择高价值问题，并利用用户的回答执行动作。控制器在训练过程中引导后续 rollout 的收集；随着任务策略更新，新的 rollout 产生新的价值标签，从而为控制器提供不断更新的监督信号，使澄清指导能适应智能体自身能力的演变。训练目标可理解为：控制器最小化预测价值与实际配对回报差异的损失，任务策略则最大化任务效用（包含正确提问和正确执行回答后的收益）。推理时，控制器可选择保留或移除；消融实验表明，即使推理时移除控制器，协同训练仍能提升任务策略，说明价值估计信号间接改进了策略对不完整信息的处理。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "mean score", "value": "最高平均分（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "Qwen3-4B，四个基准的十个评估设置"}, {"metric": "相对最强基线的提升", "value": "13.2 percentage points", "setting": "UserGym Travel"}, {"metric": "相对最强基线的提升", "value": "6.5 percentage points", "setting": "IN3 underspecification judgment accuracy"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出残差查询价值（residual query value）来量化在当前策略下提问相对于直接执行带来的任务效用增益。；采用配对 rollout（paired rollouts）从同一交互历史估计查询价值，训练一个澄清价值控制器。；协同训练价值控制器与任务策略：控制器引导后续 rollout 收集，更新后的智能体产生新的价值标签，使澄清指导随智能体学习而自适应。；证明在推理时移除控制器后，协同训练仍能提升任务策略，并且自适应澄清指导带来额外收益。

Abstract: LLM agents can execute increasingly complex tasks, but effective assistance also requires aligning their actions with goals and constraints that users may leave unstated. We study this problem as interactive intent alignment through clarification. Yet clarification is useful only when the agent can turn the answer into better decisions. As training changes how the agent handles incomplete information and uses additional answers, the same clarification can become more or less useful. We introduce CLEAT (CLarification through valuE-guided Agent co-Training), a framework that co-trains a clarification-value controller and the task policy from shared interaction outcomes. We formulate residual query value to measure the task utility gained by asking rather than proceeding under the current policy, and estimate it through paired rollouts from the same interaction history. These paired returns train the controller to predict clarification value and the task policy to select useful questions and act on the answers. The controller guides subsequent rollout collection, while outcomes from the updated agent provide new value labels that allow clarification guidance to adapt as the agent learns. With Qwen3-4B, CLEAT achieves the highest mean score in all ten evaluation settings across four benchmarks. Gains over the strongest baseline reach 13.2 and 6.5 percentage points on UserGym Travel and IN3 underspecification judgment accuracy, respectively. Ablations show that co-training improves the task policy even when the controller is removed at inference, while adapting clarification guidance to the evolving policy yields further gains.

</details>

*Ximing Lu,Aviral Kumar,Jan Kautz,Yejin Choi,David Acuna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对RLVR中零优势陷阱问题，提出MagicBeanStalk递归数据合成算法，生成策略-预算校准的训练问题，在数学和金融基准上显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR训练中大规模开源数学语料在固定策略和rollout预算下大部分问题处于零优势陷阱（约88%在所有rollout中全对或全错），过滤只能选择无法生成困难问题，导致学习信号不足。

Method: MagicBeanStalk是一个自顶向下分解与自底向上递归的数据合成算法。输入为种子问题（如数学或金融问题），目标是生成策略-预算校准的训练问题。算法首先自顶向下分解种子问题，提取其推理蓝图（reasoning blueprints），然后自底向上递归地构造新的问题家族：基于蓝图变异或组合，生成实质上新但保留原始推理操作和组成的问题。生成过程中根据目标策略和rollout预算进行校准，使得部分rollout成功、部分失败，且至少一个正确解在训练预算内。最终产出可验证的训练问题用于持续RLVR训练。论文通过8个数学基准和金融领域实验验证效果，但未提供模型架构细节。

Conclusion: 实验刻画数据集有效性关于问题、目标策略和训练rollout预算的函数关系，揭示零优势陷阱。；提出MagicBeanStalk：自顶向下分解与自底向上递归数据合成算法，将种子问题扩展为家族，生成实质新且策略-预算校准的训练问题，同时保留种子的推理蓝图。

Abstract: Data-centric scaling of RLVR has focused on collecting or generating verifiable training problems. However, corpus size alone does not determine whether the dataset provides a useful learning signal for a target policy. To guide the synthesis of effective RLVR data, we experimentally characterize dataset effectiveness as a function of its problems, the target policy, and the training rollout budget. Under this characterization, we show that a large open-source math corpus pooled from widely used RLVR datasets falls almost entirely into a zero-advantage trap: for a fixed target policy and rollout budget, roughly 88% of its problems are solved in every rollout or failed in every rollout. Filtering concentrates what remains, but it can only select, discarding the hard problems. Effective synthetic problems should therefore require the reasoning operations and compositions required to solve those hard problems. Yet their difficulty must be calibrated, so that some rollouts succeed, some fail, and at least one correct solution fits within the training budget. Prompting a teacher to vary hard problems directly does not achieve this. Guided by these empirical principles, we introduce MagicBeanStalk, a top-down decomposition and bottom-up recursive data-synthesis algorithm that grows seed problems into families of substantively new, policy- and budget-calibrated training problems while preserving the seeds' reasoning blueprints. Across 8 math benchmarks, continual RLVR on MagicBeanStalk problems improves gpt-oss-120b from 61.2 to 69.8 (+8.6) and DeepSeek v4Flash from 72.4 to 77.9 (+5.4) on a 16K-token budget. Training on the full 78K source corpus improves by only +1.1 and its best filtered real data subset by +5.8. In finance, we synthesize over 14K verifiable problems from only 340 solutions and improve over base by 6.9 and 2.4 points on SecQue and FinanceBench, respectively.

</details>

*Wei Yang,Qing Ping,Youzhi Luo,Yawei Wang,Abhishek Kumar,Panpan Xu,Jun Huan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoSyn利用学习反馈驱动的多智能体系统，通过可修订的合成状态、方向分配与执行规划，实现合成空间的自适应扩展，在τ²-Bench和CAR-Bench上任务成功率较最强基线提升13.3点，并产生更高质量、结构更广的合成经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体数据合成方法存在反馈到决策的鸿沟：学习者反馈（如失败模式、薄弱区域）无法直接转化为未来合成应如何改变的具体方案，也无法判断当前合成空间是否能表示新发现的交互因素。

Method: EvoSyn是一个学习反馈驱动的多智能体框架，围绕一个可修订的合成状态组织数据合成。该状态显式指定可被有意生成的交互因子以及合成努力的分配位置。系统包含角色专业化的多个智能体：Designer负责分配合成方向，Planner将方向落地为可执行的经验轨迹。学习者反馈被转化为对合成状态的修订。流程分两个层次：(1) 空间内自适应：在现有合成空间内，将努力重新分配到薄弱或覆盖不足的区域；(2) 合成空间扩展：将新发现的可控交互因子提升为显式的生成控制，从而扩展可合成的内容范围。训练目标或优化细节未在摘要中披露；推理/服务流程也未具体说明，但整体是一个闭环：智能体执行合成经验→评估学习者表现→诊断薄弱点→修订合成状态→重新生成经验。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均任务成功率", "value": "提升13.3点", "setting": "τ²-Bench与CAR-Bench基准，对比最强合成基线（基线具体数值未在摘要中给出）"}, {"metric": "合成经验质量与结构多样性", "value": "更高", "setting": "定性定量对比（摘要仅称'higher-quality and structurally broader'，未给出具体指标）"}, {"metric": "发现可控因子", "value": "发现无学习者反馈时遗漏的可控因子", "setting": "τ²-Bench与CAR-Bench（具体因子数量未披露）"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: EvoSyn框架：以可修订的合成状态为中心，显式描述可生成哪些交互因子以及合成努力应如何分配。；角色专业化多智能体系统：将学习者反馈转化为合成修订，包含负责分配合成方向的Designer和将方向落地为可执行经验的Planner。；空间内自适应：针对薄弱或覆盖不足区域调整合成方向。；合成空间扩展：将新发现的可控因子提升为显式生成控制，从而突破原有合成空间限制。；闭环学习流程：学习者反馈直接驱动合成策略的演进，而非仅作为数据筛选或验证信号。

Abstract: The continued improvement of large language models increasingly depends on synthetic experience, yet effective data synthesis for interactive agents requires not only executable and verifiable trajectories, but also interactions that provide learning value for the current task agent. Existing methods increasingly incorporate environment feedback, data validation, or learner failures, but still face a feedback-to-decision gap: observed weaknesses do not directly specify how future synthesis should change, or whether they can be represented by the current synthesis space. To address this gap, we propose EvoSyn, a learning-feedback-driven multi-agent framework that organizes synthesis around a revisable state specifying what interaction factors can be deliberately generated and where synthesis effort should be allocated. A role-specialized multi-agent system translates learner feedback into synthesis revisions, while a Designer allocates synthesis directions and a Planner grounds them into executable experience. This process enables within-space adaptation toward weak or under-covered regions and synthesis-space expansion that promotes newly discovered factors into explicit generation controls. Experiments on $\tau^2$-Bench and CAR-Bench show that EvoSyn improves average task success by 13.3 points over the strongest synthesis baseline, while producing higher-quality and structurally broader synthetic experience and discovering controllable factors missed without learner feedback.

</details>


### [43] [BoundaryEvo: Data-Model Co-Evolution at the Capability Boundary for Long-Chain Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13285)
*Jiaming Su,Suorong Yang,Xudong Li,Tengchao Yang,Peng Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BoundaryEvo 提出两阶段数据-模型协同演化框架，先通过 Capability Priming 建立学生基础推理能力，再依据学生能力边界反复选取尚未掌握但教师可解的样本进行蒸馏。仅用 1000 条训练样本，在 11 个推理基准上优于静态与动态数据选择基线，将 Qwen3.5-4B 在 IMO-AnswerBench 和 AMO-Bench 上的准确率分别从 62.9 提升至 75.3、26.8 提升至 46.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长链推理蒸馏中，固定训练集无法适应学生模型能力的演变，导致已掌握样本提供冗余监督、过难样本难以学习，而仅用少量教师轨迹训练时这一问题尤为严重。

Method: BoundaryEvo 是一个两阶段的数据-模型协同演化框架。第一阶段 Capability Priming：直接使用教师模型生成的一小部分长链推理轨迹对学生模型（Qwen3.5-4B）进行蒸馏，不引入独立模型进行判断或过滤，目的是让学生首先获得基础推理能力，为后续能力感知的数据选择提供基础。第二阶段 Capability-Boundary Co-Evolution：在每一轮迭代中，使用当前学生模型对候选问题进行评估，选择那些当前学生尚未掌握（即回答错误或置信度低）但教师模型能够可靠解决的问题；随后用这些问题对应的教师长推理轨迹对学生模型进行再训练/微调，更新学生模型参数。学生模型能力提升后，重新进行数据选择，如此反复迭代，使训练数据始终位于学生当前能力边界附近。该方法通过“选择-训练-更新”的闭环，避免了静态数据集的过时问题以及动态数据选择中因学生初期较弱而无法可靠判断难度的困难。推理时直接使用训练好的学生模型进行推理，无需额外数据选择模块。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "IMO-AnswerBench 准确率", "value": "62.9 -> 75.3", "setting": "原始 Qwen3.5-4B 模型，使用 1000 条训练样本"}, {"metric": "AMO-Bench 准确率", "value": "26.8 -> 46.0", "setting": "原始 Qwen3.5-4B 模型，使用 1000 条训练样本"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 两阶段数据-模型协同演化框架BoundaryEvo：先进行能力启动（Capability Priming），再进行能力边界协同演化（Capability-Boundary Co-Evolution）。；能力启动阶段直接蒸馏少量教师生成的长推理轨迹，不依赖独立模型进行判断或过滤，使学生获得初始推理能力。；能力边界协同演化阶段反复选择当前学生尚未掌握但专家能可靠解决的问题，并随着学生能力进化动态更新训练数据。

Abstract: Distilling long-chain reasoning trajectories from large teacher models provides an effective way to improve the reasoning ability of small language models. However, existing methods typically select a fixed training set before distillation, overlooking that the usefulness of a reasoning example changes as the student evolves. This mismatch becomes especially costly when only a small number of teacher trajectories can be used for training. Besides, examples already mastered provide little new supervision, while examples far beyond the capability of student may be difficult to learn from. Training data should therefore adapt as model capability evolves, yet reliable data selection is difficult when the student is initially weak. To address these challenges, our key idea is to first establish the basic reasoning capability and then adapt the training data to the evolving capability boundary of the student. We propose BoundaryEvo, a two-stage data--model co-evolution framework. In Capability Priming, we directly distill a small set of teacher-generated long reasoning trajectories without using a separate model to judge and filter them, allowing the student to acquire an initial reasoning capability before capability-aware data selection. In Capability-Boundary Co-Evolution, we repeatedly select questions that the current student has not mastered but the expert can reliably solve, and update the training data as the student evolves. With only 1,000 training examples, BoundaryEvo consistently outperforms static and dynamic data selection baselines across 11 reasoning benchmarks, raising the original Qwen3.5-4B accuracy from 62.9 to 75.3 on IMO-AnswerBench and from 26.8 to 46.0 on AMO-Bench. The code and data will be made publicly available.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向任务的对话基准假设用户是合作的，无法评估LLM销售代理在真实抵抗性对话中的表现。需要构建一个包含心理抵抗买家角色的多轮对话基准。

Method: SmartSalesBench 是一个基准测试框架。它包含五个销售代理档案、十二个买家角色、十五款智能手机产品（覆盖六个价格层级）。所有销售代理和买家角色的生成均通过检索增强生成（RAG）进行接地，以确保对话内容基于产品信息或心理学原理。基准执行四种受控实验，每种实验进行三次迭代，共产生13,680个对话会话，每次对话最多20轮。评估采用自动化LLM-as-judge评分，并辅以内部人工评估（50个样本，与BRS的Cohen kappa=0.71，Pearson r=0.81），以验证自动评分的可靠性。

Abstract: We present SmartSalesBench, the first multi-turn conversational benchmark explicitly designed to evaluate large language model (LLM) sales agents against psychologically grounded, resistant buyer personas. Existing task-oriented dialogue benchmarks assume cooperative users and measure task completion; SmartSalesBench inverts this assumption, placing agents against personas programmed to resist and challenge across conversations of up to twenty turns. The benchmark includes five agent profiles, twelve buyer personas, fifteen smartphone products across six price tiers, and four controlled experiments with 3 iterations totaling 13,680 sessions. Both sales-agent and buyer persona generations are grounded through retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). We introduce nine evaluation metrics grouped into three tiers: primary outcome metrics (CR, BRS, RIR), secondary quality metrics (SMS, PFS, ETI), and exploratory metrics planned for future reporting (ITS, ARI, SPI). Our evaluation combines automated LLM-as-judge scoring with internal human assessment (n = 50, \k{appa} = 0.71, Pearson r = 0.81 with BRS).

</details>

### [45] [Beyond Temporal Proximity: Explanatory World Models for Credit Assignment in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13504)
*Oriol Corcoll,Qiyao Wei,Ciarán Mark Gilligan-Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对生成式推荐中长延迟反馈的信用分配难题，提出用LLM解释进行奖励重分配的EWM框架，在长时程RL基准、KuaiRand序列推荐及工业级生成式推荐器上均取得提升，召回率显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习按时间邻近度而非显式相关性传播奖励，导致远距离但重要的用户行为信号被衰减，造成样本效率低下，难以在生成式推荐器中将长期业务结果正确归因到早期交互。

Method: EWM以生成式推荐器的交互序列为输入，将用户历史行为与最终结果作为事件集合。其核心模块包括：1）解释模块——使用前沿语言模型对已实现结果生成解释，识别哪些早期事件可能因果性地促成了该结果；2）归因模块——将解释转化为学习信号，通过保守分层算子递归地把奖励从结果事件重新分配给被解释为相关的前序事件，约束是重分配后保持未折扣episode回报不变；3）学习模块——用重分配后的信用信号训练策略/生成式推荐模型。论文还提出三种解释变体：基于Halpern实际因果、Lombrozo解释简洁性、Deutsch难以变易解释，用于研究何种解释更有用。推理时按标准策略执行，解释与归因仅用于训练阶段的信用分配。训练目标为在保持回报不变的前提下最大化信用分配后的策略性能。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "回报/性能", "value": "EWM显著优于PPO与LaRe，延迟越大增益越大", "setting": "Crafter与WordCraft长时程RL基准"}, {"metric": "离线推荐指标", "value": "EWM优于SFT与LaRe", "setting": "KuaiRand公开序列推荐基准"}, {"metric": "Recall", "value": "EWM显著提升Recall并增加行为代理指标", "setting": "工业级生成式推荐器的离线评估"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "在线A2C性能", "value": "EWM优于A2C", "setting": "KuaiRand在线环境（摘要表述为online A2C）"}]}

Conclusion: 提出Explanatory World Models（EWM）归因框架，利用解释识别先前可能促成最终结果的事件；用前沿语言模型实例化EWM，将其解释作为学习信号；设计保守分层算子，递归重分配奖励并保持未折扣的episode回报不变；提出三种互补解释变体：Halpern实际因果、Lombrozo解释简洁性、Deutsch难以变易解释

Abstract: Generative Recommenders increasingly use Reinforcement Learning (RL) to optimize outcomes that may appear hundreds or thousands of interactions after a decision. Standard RL propagates reward according to temporal proximity rather than explicit relevance, attenuating the signal assigned to distant but important actions and creating a source of sample inefficiency. We introduce Explanatory World Models (EWM), an attribution framework that uses explanations to identify the earlier events that plausibly contributed to a realized outcome. We instantiate EWM with a frontier language model and use its explanations as learning signals using a conservative hierarchical operator that recursively redistributes reward while preserving the undiscounted episode return. To investigate what constitutes a useful explanation, we operationalize three complementary variants inspired by Halpern's actual causation, Lombrozo's explanatory simplicity, and Deutsch's hard-to-vary explanations. We evaluate EWM on two controlled long-horizon RL benchmarks, a public sequential-recommendation benchmark, and an industry-scale generative recommender. On Crafter and WordCraft, EWM substantially outperforms PPO and LaRe, with its largest gains when rewards are most delayed. On KuaiRand, EWM outperforms SFT and LaRe offline and A2C online. Finally, in an offline evaluation of an industry generative recommender, EWM considerably improves Recall and increases behavioral proxies. These results suggest that explanations can provide structured and reusable credit for learning from delayed outcomes.

</details>


### [46] [Cross-Behavior Policy Optimization: Unified Learning from Offline Demonstrations and On- and Off-Policy Rollouts via a Shared Regression Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12830)
*Du Cheng,Junyu Lu,Jingfan Zhang,Yuzhuo Zhang,Haoxuan Jia,Shichao Weng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CBPO，用固定参考目标的统一回归损失替代SFT+RL的分阶段训练，使离线示范与在线/离策略rollout共享同一回放缓冲区，无需重要性加权或critic。在数学推理任务上，CBPO-Unified在Qwen3-1.7B上比SFT DAPO在avg@32和pass@32上分别提升2.33和4.44个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SFT后接RL的后训练范式将离线示范与策略生成rollout的目标分离，限制了已收集响应的持续复用。论文旨在用统一的回归损失兼容离线、在线和离策略数据，避免分别协调SFT和RL目标以及存储行为策略概率、重要性加权或学习critic。

Method: CBPO旨在统一离线示范与策略生成的rollout。其核心架构采用固定参考目标（fixed-reference target），使得不同来源的响应（正/负离线完成、参考响应、当前和历史rollout）能够共享同一个回归损失。具体地，每个响应被转化为带有奖励标签的通用记录格式，并存入回放缓冲区。优化过程中，无论数据来源如何，均使用相同的回归目标进行训练。由于生成策略的概率不进入目标函数，因此无需存储行为策略概率、重要性权重，也不需要训练critic网络。根据可用数据源的组合不同，可以实例化为离线、在线或离策略变体，CBPO-Unified则同时使用所有来源。训练过程中，随着新响应进入缓冲区，优化目标保持不变，实现了持续复用。推理阶段采用标准的自回归生成。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "avg@32", "value": "提升2.33个百分点", "setting": "Qwen3-1.7B模型上，CBPO-Unified对比SFT DAPO，在四个数学推理基准上取平均"}, {"metric": "pass@32", "value": "提升4.44个百分点", "setting": "Qwen3-1.7B模型上，CBPO-Unified对比SFT DAPO，在四个数学推理基准上取平均"}, {"metric": "avg@32", "value": "提升1.87个百分点", "setting": "回放消融中，保留历史rollout对比无历史配置"}, {"metric": "pass@32", "value": "提升3.72个百分点", "setting": "回放消融中，保留历史rollout对比无历史配置"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Cross-Behavior Policy Optimization (CBPO)，通过固定参考目标使离线示范和在线/离策略rollout共享单一回归损失；生成策略不进入回归目标，因此正负离线完成、参考响应、当前和历史rollout可共用统一格式和回放缓冲区；无需存储行为策略概率、重要性加权或学习critic，即可继续复用收集的响应；根据可用数据源限制，导出offline、on-policy、off-policy变体，以及组合的CBPO-Unified；通过回放消融验证保留历史rollout对性能的提升

Abstract: Reasoning post-training draws on offline demonstrations and policy-generated rollouts, yet conventional supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by reinforcement learning (RL) separates their objectives and limits continued reuse of collected responses. We propose \textbf{Cross-Behavior Policy Optimization} (CBPO), which makes these sources compatible with one regression loss through a fixed-reference target. Because the generating policy does not enter this target, positive and negative offline completions, reference responses, and current and historical rollouts can share a common reward-labeled record format and replay buffer. As new responses enter the buffer, optimization retains the same regression objective without coordinating separate SFT and RL objectives or requiring stored behavior-policy probabilities, importance weighting, or a learned critic. Restricting the available sources gives offline, on-policy, and off-policy variants, while CBPO-Unified combines them. Across two model scales and four mathematical reasoning benchmarks, CBPO delivers leading \texttt{avg@32} and \texttt{pass@32} performance across benchmarks and data regimes. On Qwen3-1.7B, CBPO-Unified improves benchmark-averaged \texttt{avg@32} and \texttt{pass@32} over SFT DAPO by 2.33 and 4.44 percentage points, respectively. In the replay ablation, retaining historical rollouts improves these metrics by 1.87 and 3.72 percentage points over the no-history configuration.

</details>

*Anthony Kostal-Vazquez,Vassil Dimitrov,Branden Butler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出代数整数数值格式（AINF）与代数整数量化（AIQ），用于 LLM 推理的高效压缩。AINF 可归约为纯整数算术，支持无误差乘加，并据此构建了矩阵乘法引擎。实验表明，AIQ 在同等比特预算下精度与工业标准 MX 格式相当，为量化推理提供了新的可配置方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何设计一种高度可配置且精确的压缩计算数值格式，在 LLM 推理中实现无损的乘法累加运算，并在同等比特预算下达到与工业标准 MX 格式相当的精度。

Method: 论文提出代数整数数值格式（AINF）作为压缩计算的框架。AINF 的核心属性是可简单归约为纯整数算术，从而支持无误差的乘法累加操作。基于此属性，作者开发了矩阵乘法引擎，利用 AINF 实现高效计算，其性能与现代硬件加速器上最高效的数值对应物处于可预测的倍数范围内。此外，AINF 允许根据特定用例的数值特征选择代数整数，为浮点或定点格式无法提供的优化机会奠定基础。在此基础上，论文引入代数整数量化（AIQ），这是一种针对 LLM 推理的有损压缩格式。实验采用二次代数整数（degree two），在多种模型和基准上，AIQ 在等价比特每参数预算下实现了与工业标准 MX 格式相当的精度。论文指出，推广到更高阶及多维代数整数有望带来更大的计算优势。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "精度对比", "value": "competitive accuracy compared to MX format at equivalent bit-per-parameter budgets", "setting": "多个 LLM 模型和基准，同等比特每参数预算"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出代数整数数值格式（AINF），可自然归约为纯整数算术，支持无误差的乘法累加。；提出代数整数量化（AIQ），一种用于 LLM 推理的有损压缩格式，可根据用例数值特征选择代数整数。；开发了利用 AINF 的矩阵乘法引擎，在现代硬件加速器上性能处于高效数值格式的可预测倍数内。；在多个模型和基准上验证 AIQ 与 MX 格式在等价比特每参数预算下的精度竞争力。

Abstract: In this article, we propose algebraic integer number formats (AINF) as a framework for highly configurable and accurate compressed computation. A key property of AINF is its straightforward reduction to purely integer arithmetic, enabling error-free multiply-accumulate operations. We develop a matrix multiplication engine that exploits this property, obtaining performance within a predictable factor of the most efficient numerical counterparts on modern hardware accelerators. Another key advantage of AIQ is the freedom to select algebraic integers tailored to the numerical characteristics of a given use case, offering optimization opportunities that are fundamentally unavailable in floating-point or fixed-point formats. Motivated by these properties, we introduce algebraic integer quantization (AIQ), a lossy compression format for large language model (LLM) inference, and provide results demonstrating that AIQ achieves competitive accuracy compared to the industry-standard MX format across a diverse set of models and benchmarks at equivalent bit-per-parameter budgets. The experiments presented in this article employ algebraic integers of degree two (quadratic integers); generalizations to higher-order and, in particular, multidimensional algebraic integers promise even greater computational advantages and remain a promising direction for future work.

</details>


### [48] [Tabular Foundation Models Are Effectively Shallow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13253)
*Irene Cannistraci,Julia E. Vogt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出用闭式仿射翻译器替换表格基础模型中最多96%的Transformer块，发现表格Transformer严重过参数化。实验表明，移除一半块的中位AUROC损失最多0.002，半深度模型仍优于全深度模型和GBDT。不同模型家族过参数化模式不同。移除深度可节省最多1.5B参数，GPU推理加速最高6倍，单块TabPFN-3缓存查询加速2.4倍且内存减少6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFM）是否可以通过移除架构冗余（深度）来简化而不牺牲性能。

Method: 表格基础模型（TFM）通过上下文学习（in-context learning）处理新任务，无需额外训练，其架构由多个Transformer块堆叠而成。本文研究移除这些块的冗余性：提出一种闭式、无标签的仿射翻译器，用于替换模型中一个连续的块窗口（最多96%的块），被替换的块不参与计算，而其余块保持不变。翻译器根据输入进行仿射变换，以近似被移除块的功能。在8个前沿TFM和171个分类数据集上的评估表明，表格Transformer严重过参数化：替换一半块后中位AUROC损失最多0.002，且半深度TabFM仍优于所有其他全深度TFM和调优GBDT。不同模型家族的过参数化模式不同：TabPFN-3依赖编码器，单轴模型只需第一块，双轴模型计算集中于一个关键块。最终，移除深度可节省最多1.5B参数，GPU推理加速最高6倍，中位AUROC代价为0.03；单块TabPFN-3对缓存查询加速2.4倍，内存减少6倍，性能损失可忽略。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "中位AUROC损失", "value": "≤0.002", "setting": "替换一半块，在8个前沿TFM、171个分类数据集上评估"}, {"metric": "参数节省", "value": "最多1.5B参数", "setting": "移除深度"}, {"metric": "GPU推理加速", "value": "最高6倍", "setting": "中位AUROC损失0.03"}, {"metric": "缓存查询加速", "value": "2.4倍", "setting": "单块TabPFN-3，内存减少6倍，性能损失可忽略"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 引入闭式、无标签的仿射翻译器，可替换最多96%的连续块，且保持其余块不变。；发现表格Transformer严重过参数化：替换一半块的中位AUROC损失最多0.002，且半深度TabFM仍优于所有其他全深度TFM和调优GBDT。；揭示不同模型家族的过参数化方式不同：TabPFN-3依赖编码器，单轴模型只需第一块，双轴模型计算集中于一个关键块。

Abstract: In-context-learning Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) are uniquely suited to scenarios with limited resources or privacy constraints, as they solve new tasks without additional training. However, their architecture consists of multiple transformer blocks, resulting in high computational and memory footprints. Meanwhile, traditional alternatives like Gradient-Boosted Decision Trees (GBDTs) remain highly competitive and run efficiently on CPUs, suggesting that tabular tasks may not inherently require deep architectures. This contrast raises a fundamental question: can TFMs be simplified by removing architectural redundancies without sacrificing performance? To answer this, we introduce a closed-form, label-free affine translator that replaces a contiguous window of up to 96% of the blocks, leaving the rest unchanged. Evaluating eight frontier TFMs on 171 classification datasets, we reveal that tabular transformers are vastly overparameterized: replacing half of a model's blocks costs at most $0.002$ median AUROC across all architectures, with the half-depth TabFM still outperforming every other full-depth TFMs and tuned GBDTs. Furthermore, different model families are overparameterized in different ways: TabPFN-3 relies on its encoders, maintaining performance regardless of which single block is retained. Other single-axis models spread computation thinly, needing only their first block, while dual-axis models concentrate it in one critical block. Ultimately, removing depth saves up to 1.5B parameters, speeds up GPU inference by up to $6\times$ at a median cost of $0.03$ AUROC, while a single-block TabPFN-3 serves cached queries $2.4\times$ faster using $6\times$ less memory at negligible cost.

</details>

*Daniel Thi Graviet,Tanmay Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文把编码智能体的失败恢复视为搜索问题，在 Daytona 全沙箱快照上实现 Go-Explore 式分支搜索。实验表明求解率与效率的提升主要来自仓库之外的状态（数据库、服务、运行时），全快照搜索对跨多服务、状态无法由 diff 重建的长程任务最有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在失败后从头重试会丢弃已建立的机器状态（数据库、服务、运行时的非仓库状态），导致长程多服务任务效率低下；如何利用中间环境状态进行恢复与分支搜索。

Method: 系统将编码智能体置于 Daytona 提供的完整沙箱环境中运行；每次执行在某一步失败或结束时，对该沙箱整体（包括仓库文件系统、数据库、后台服务及其他运行时状态）做完整快照，而非仅保存代码 diff。在搜索层面采用 Go-Explore 范式：维护已访问状态集合，从历史快照中恢复环境并继续分支探索，而不是从初始状态重启。摘要给出了高层次的搜索与快照交互，但未披露具体的状态表示、去重/归档机制、分支选择策略、奖励定义或训练目标，也未说明推理服务与并发调度细节，故仅能确认输入为沙箱全量状态、处理顺序为“执行—失败—快照—恢复/分支—再探索”。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将编码智能体的失败恢复形式化为搜索问题，并采用 Go-Explore 风格在完整 Daytona 快照上进行分支探索；实证发现求解率提升主要来自仓库之外的状态（数据库、服务、运行时状态），而非代码 diff 本身；指出全快照搜索在跨多服务、状态无法由单一 diff 重建的长程任务中最有效

Abstract: Coding agents often make useful progress before they fail, but retry-from-scratch discards the machine state created along the way. This makes failure recovery an interesting search problem: if intermediate environment states preserve meaningful progress, restoring and branching from them could outperform repeatedly restarting from the initial state. We implement Go-Explore-style search over full Daytona snapshots and find that higher and more efficient solve rates depend mainly on progress that sits outside the repository, such as databases, services, and other runtime state. Our results suggest that full-snapshot search is most effective when long-horizon coding work spans multiple services whose state cannot be reconstructed from a single diff.

</details>

*Sheng Zhao,Yuhao Zhu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练中知识的时间顺序组织如何影响后续知识更新的效率。

Method: 论文主要研究预训练数据组织对知识更新效率的影响，未提出新的模型架构。其核心方法涉及：在预训练阶段，将时间变化的知识按时间顺序组织输入模型，训练得到‘专家’模型；或随机打乱顺序训练得到‘通才’模型。后续知识更新通过微调或参数编辑等方式进行，比较不同组织方式下的更新步数。理论部分从优化动态推导更新成本法则。最终提出一种混合组织策略，在预训练中同时保留历史知识稳定性和当前知识更新效率。未披露具体模型结构、参数规模或训练细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出专家模型与通才模型的概念，并发现专家模型所需更新步数远少于通才模型；从第一性原理推导出更新成本与优化动态的关系法则，并与实证结果吻合；设计了一种保持稳定历史知识同时高效更新当前知识的预训练数据组织策略

Abstract: An ideal (language) model must continually update its knowledge. Existing work largely focuses on making these updates efficient after training. We investigate how the organization of time-varying knowledge during pre-training shapes the efficiency of subsequent updates. Models trained on chronologically ordered knowledge specialize in the most recently encountered knowledge; we call them specialists. In contrast, models trained on the same knowledge in randomly shuffled order perform well across all represented time periods; we call them generalists. Surprisingly, specialists require far fewer update steps than generalists to acquire new knowledge. To explain this advantage, we derive a law from first principles that relates update cost to the model's optimization dynamics and that closely predicts empirical results. Guided by these findings, we design a strategy for organizing pre-training data that preserves stable historical knowledge while enabling efficient updates to current knowledge. We also show that the benefits of temporal knowledge organization extend to real-world pre-trained models and existing knowledge update tools, and generalize to other knowledge orderings. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [51] [Rationally Linearized MLPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13568)
*Arijit Sehanobish,Byeongchan Kim,Avinava Dubey,Min-hwan Oh,Krzysztof Choromanski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RaLiMPs 利用有理函数近似线性化 MLP，在 LLM 等模型上实现推理加速与精度保持的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何通过低阶有理近似将 MLP 激活函数线性化，构建核表示以实现跨层高效组合，从而在保持精度的同时显著降低 LLM 等模型的推理成本。

Method: RaLiMPs 的核心是对标准 MLP 激活函数（如 GELU、SiLU）进行低阶有理函数近似，将非线性激活替换为有理函数形式。基于此，每个 MLP 层可被表示为具有窄瓶颈的核特征映射，即通过有理近似诱导的特征变换实现输入到输出的映射。多个这样的层可高效组合，形成整个 MLP 的线性化表示。理论部分给出了近似误差保证。训练时保持原 MLP 结构或对近似后结构微调，推理时使用线性化核形式替代原始非线性计算，从而减少计算量和内存访问。该方法在隐式 3D 模型和大型语言模型中评估了精度与效率的权衡。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出使用低阶有理近似替代多项式近似来线性化 MLP 激活函数；构建窄瓶颈核表示以支持 MLP 层间高效组合；为有理近似线性化提供理论保证

Abstract: We present Rationally Linearized Multilayer Perceptrons(RaLiMPs), a new approach to efficiently linearizing MLPs using low-degree rational approximations of standard activation functions. Inspired by polynomial-based MLP linearization, we show that rational approximations can provide accurate representations at substantially lower degree. RaLiMPs exploit these approximations to construct kernel representations with narrow bottlenecks, enabling efficient composition across MLP layers. We provide theoretical guarantees for the resulting approximation and evaluate RaLiMPs across applications ranging from implicit 3D models to large language models. Our experiments demonstrate favorable accuracy--efficiency tradeoffs, with RaLiMPs achieving competitive or improved accuracy while substantially reducing inference cost.

</details>

Abstract: Mamba-3 introduces input-dependent rotations into recurrent state updates, but their contribution beyond state retention remains unclear. We interpret these ro- tations as underdamped second-order dynamics, providing a framework for in- vestigating the role of oscillation in sequence modeling. Controlled comparisons show that underdamped dynamics outperform non-oscillatory damping regimes on state-tracking tasks, while language-model pretraining demonstrates benefits for long-context prediction and length generalization. Interventions in pretrained models further establish the importance of learned phase, and state analysis sug- gests that rotation regulates how retained information combines with incoming up- dates. Taken together, these findings identify underdamped dynamics as a useful mechanism for recurrent computation and suggest that oscillation supports long- context processing by regulating how information accumulates.

*Ran Tian,Courtney Paquette*

Main category: cs.LG

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预训练过程中间歇出现的loss spike现象，其成因和预测方法尚不明确。

Method: 该工作提出一个逐层训练动态的模拟框架，将深度神经网络的每一层建模为一个简单的二次模型，其输入假设服从重尾幂律分布。整体流程为：首先，将网络中的某一层抽象为二次型损失函数；其次，从该层实际的输入激活统计中抽取或拟合出重尾幂律分布的样本；接着，在该二次模型上运行与原始优化器（如Adam）相同的更新规则，模拟出该层的训练轨迹。通过逐层模拟，可以观察损失随训练步数的变化，从而预测可能出现的loss spike。论文在Transformer语言模型的一个具体Adam配置（β2接近1）上进行案例研究，该配置已知会诱发spike。模拟能够从给定检查点出发，预测出尖峰的出现。此外，作者根据模拟中观察到的指标，设计了一个随训练进程调整β2的调度方案。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "performance", "value": "empirically improves performance", "setting": "通过从观测指标推导β2调度策略，在真实训练中取得性能提升"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出具有重尾幂律分布输入的简单二次模型来复现loss spike。；构建逐层训练动态的理论框架与模拟算法，将DNN每层视为受输入分布影响的二次模型。；以Adam中β2接近1的配置为案例，从给定检查点成功预测出尖峰行为。；从观测指标推导出合适的β2调度策略，并经验性地提升性能。

Abstract: Intermittent ``spikes'' in loss, during the pre-training of Large Language Models (LLMs), remains a mysterious phenomenon both theoretically and empirically. In this paper, we show that simple quadratic models with heavy-tailed, power-law distributed inputs can replicate these spikes. We introduce a theoretical framework and simulation algorithm to model per-layer training dynamics of Deep Neural Networks (DNNs), using such quadratic models. In a case study on an Adam hyperparameter configuration (where $\beta_2$ is set close to $1$) known to induce spikes in Transformer LMs, our simulation successfully predicts bumps indicating spiky behavior from a given checkpoint. Furthermore, we derive an appropriate $\beta_2$ schedule from observed metrics, which empirically improves performance.

</details>

*Linxuan Wu,Liang Chen*

TL;DR: 该论文提出PPO-FP16，通过三个结构感知模块实现半精度PPO训练的数值稳定性，在连续控制任务上保持性能的同时实现1.16-2.03倍更新加速，优化器内存降低约72%，峰值GPU内存降低最高44.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限平台上进行本地在线训练时，直接使用FP16进行PPO训练会因数值误差在策略更新和数据收集间反馈而不稳定，同时PPO需要反复比较当前策略与固定行为策略。

Method: PPO-FP16在标准PPO训练流程上引入三个结构感知模块以稳定FP16数值。输入为环境交互产生的轨迹数据，策略网络与价值网络参与前向与反向计算。全局标量保护模块作用于minibatch或参数更新级别共享的统计量（如优势估计、损失缩放因子等），防止因FP16精度不足导致的统计偏差。策略变化残差保护模块专门处理PPO中当前策略与行为策略之间的概率比计算，提取并保留两者之间的小差异，避免因舍入误差导致比率失真。高效持久更新保护模块则优化优化器状态（如Adam动量）的存储和参数写回过程，在FP16下稳定更新并减少内存占用。训练目标保持PPO原始目标函数，超参数和训练预算不变，无需重新调参。推理/部署侧沿用FP16前向计算，但训练时的数值保护模块不影响策略网络结构。
