Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均回报AUC排名", "value": "1.90（最低）", "setting": "6个连续控制任务，5个随机种子，对比4种方法"}, {"metric": "环境-种子对比中排名第一的次数", "value": "17/30", "setting": "6个连续控制任务 × 5个随机种子"}, {"metric": "PPO更新加速比", "value": "1.16至2.03倍", "setting": "9种性能配置中的8种"}, {"metric": "优化器状态内存降低", "value": "71.7%至74.9%", "setting": "相对于Full FP32 Adam"}, {"metric": "峰值分配GPU内存降低", "value": "最高44.5%", "setting": "相对于基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 全局标量保护：稳定跨minibatch或参数更新共享的统计量。；策略变化残差保护：提取并保留当前策略与行为策略间的小差异。；高效持久更新保护：稳定优化器状态和参数写回，同时减少状态存储。

Abstract: Local online training on resource-constrained platforms requires low computational and training-state overhead. We study the numerical stability and computational efficiency of half-precision proximal policy optimization (PPO) on commodity GPUs. Directly converting PPO training to FP16 is unreliable because numerical errors feed back through policy updates and subsequent data collection, while PPO repeatedly compares a current policy with a fixed behavior policy. We propose PPO-FP16 with three structure-aware modules. Global scalar protection stabilizes statistics shared across a minibatch or parameter update. Policy-change residual protection extracts and preserves small differences between the current and behavior policies. Efficient persistent-update protection stabilizes optimizer state and parameter writeback while reducing state storage. These changes preserve the PPO objective, hyperparameters, and training budget. Across six continuous-control tasks and five seeds, PPO-FP16 achieves the lowest mean return-AUC rank (1.90) among the four methods and ranks first in 17 of 30 environment-seed comparisons. Eight of nine performance configurations yield PPO update speedups ranging from 1.16 to 2.03 times. Relative to Full FP32 Adam, optimizer-state memory is reduced by 71.7 to 74.9 percent, and peak allocated GPU memory is reduced by up to 44.5 percent. The results show that protecting a small set of structurally important quantities can deliver stable, efficient FP16 PPO without retuning.

</details>


### [55] [DuoRate: Two-Rate Per-Expert Precision Allocation for Mixture-of-Experts Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13328)
*Mengqi Guo,Zhe Wei,Yuan Yuan,Jiunn Bin Lim,Boyi Pan,Michael Bi Mi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DuoRate针对MoE服务中逐专家精度分配只优化存储率而忽略解码读取率的问题，提出两阶段率失真分配方法：离线存储统一精度权重流并烘焙失真张量，在线根据workload的prefill无标签分析动态求解每个专家的解码读取位宽。在Qwen3-30B-A3B上，2.3 bpw即超过所有2-bit类基线，2.9 bpw匹配更高读取位宽的4-bit格式，且在长解码任务上优势显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE逐专家精度分配方法仅优化存储占用，忽略了decode阶段实际读取的专家精度代价；同一组位宽下存储位宽与解码流量位宽存在显著差距（store-read gap），导致部署效率与准确性次优。

Method: DuoRate是一种面向MoE模型部署的两阶段精度分配方法。离线阶段：以统一精度等级存储一份prefix-readable权重流（所有专家共享存储格式），并为每个专家烘焙一个失真张量，表征使用不同位宽读取时的失真。在线阶段：当特定workload到来时，首先无标签地分析其prefill数据，估计该workload下的专家激活/路由分布；然后在解码率函数下求解一个拉格朗日率失真优化问题，为每个专家分配读取位宽。由于路由频率在优化中可证明约去，每个专家的决策仅依赖其失真-位宽曲线，因此在线求解仅需对每个专家计算一次点积并执行一次二分搜索查找最优λ。最终，同一个存储工件在prefill时以存储位宽读取，在decode时根据当前workload以分配的位宽读取。该架构解决了存储率与解码读取率不一致的问题，使得解码流量位宽可以动态适配workload的实际需求，从而在同等存储成本下提升准确率或在同等准确率下减少读取开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "DuoRate在2.3 bpw下超过所有2-bit类基线6.3至47.0个百分点", "setting": "Qwen3-30B-A3B"}, {"metric": "平均准确率/等效读取位宽", "value": "DuoRate在2.9 bpw下匹配读取位宽高1.4-1.6 bpw的4-bit格式", "setting": "Qwen3-30B-A3B"}, {"metric": "GSM8K准确率", "value": "DuoRate在2.9 bpw下匹配或超过所有4-bit格式，仅落后BF16 0.25个百分点；在2.6 bpw下匹配或超过4.5-bit NF4和FP4", "setting": "长解码场景，GSM8K"}, {"metric": "等效读取位宽增益", "value": "分配策略本身价值0.07-0.11 bpw的读取速率", "setting": "同格式控制实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出store-read gap概念，将逐专家精度分配问题建模为两种不同的率函数：存储率与解码读取率。；设计DuoRate框架：离线存储一个统一等级的prefix-readable权重流并烘焙失真张量；在线根据workload自身prefill无标签分析，在解码率函数下求解拉格朗日率失真分配。；证明路由频率在逐专家决策中可约去，使在线求解仅需每个专家一次点积和一次二分搜索，实现高效动态分配。；单份存储工件支持prefill按存储率读取、decode按workload特定率读取，兼顾存储与解码效率。

Abstract: Serving a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model pays for expert precision twice: the resident footprint pays for every expert the router can reach, while each batch-one decode step pays only for the experts it selects. These are two different rate functionals over the same per-expert widths, yet published per-expert MoE allocators constrain only the first. We call the difference the store-read gap; in the released BitsMoE checkpoint, 2.54 bits per weight (bpw) of storage become 7.50 bpw of decode traffic. We propose DuoRate, which stores once and reads per workload. Offline, it stores one uniform tier of a prefix-readable weight stream and bakes a distortion tensor. Online, it profiles a workload's own prefill without labels and solves a Lagrangian rate-distortion allocation under the decode functional; routing frequency provably cancels from every per-expert decision, and the solve costs one dot product per expert and a bisection. A deployment therefore keeps a single artifact that prefill reads at the stored rate and decode reads at the workload's own rate. On Qwen3-30B-A3B, DuoRate reading 2.3 bpw exceeds every 2-bit-class baseline by 6.3 to 47.0 points of average accuracy, and at 2.9 bpw it matches 4-bit formats that read 1.4-1.6 bpw more. The advantage is largest under long decoding: on GSM8K, DuoRate at 2.9 bpw matches or exceeds every 4-bit format and trails BF16 by only 0.25 points, and at 2.6 bpw it still matches or exceeds the 4.5-bit NF4 and FP4. Against controls in its own format, the allocation alone is worth 0.07-0.11 bpw of read rate. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [56] [State-OPD: State-Grounded On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13238)
*Zhentao Zhang,Yanzhao Zheng,Yanlin Liu,Yuanqiang Yu,Tianze Xu,Chao Ma,Jihuai Zhu,Lelun Wu,Baohua Dong,Hangcheng Zhu,Ruohui Huang,Gang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: State-OPD提出状态接地的在线策略蒸馏框架，解决多轮智能体蒸馏中学生推理干扰教师监督与采样效率低的问题。在ALFWorld、ScienceWorld和WebShop上，Qwen3-4B学生宏平均成功率由最强基线26.07%提升至31.74%，并在ALFWorld Seen上超过Qwen3-32B教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何将前沿大模型的推理能力有效蒸馏到小模型，并扩展到多轮智能体环境，解决多轮在线策略蒸馏中学生推理对教师监督的干扰以及采样效率问题。

Method: State-OPD框架以多轮智能体交互中的状态为核心。输入为任务描述和交互历史（包括动作-观察记录），学生模型在每个交互状态生成内部推理轨迹和动作，环境返回观察，形成多轮轨迹。关键模块包括：交互状态定义与匹配，将任务、动作-观察记录与学生内部推理分离；教师监督状态接地，在匹配的交互状态上，教师仅基于任务和动作-观察记录（不包含先前学生推理）生成监督信号，避免先前推理扭曲；采样预算分配，在高响应熵状态生成多个完整的推理-动作响应，而不仅是在固定推理轨迹下重采样动作，从而增加同状态的动作多样性；保留全历史学生生成用于训练。训练目标为在线策略蒸馏损失，学生模型在自身生成的轨迹上学习教师的token级目标。推理时，学生模型基于全历史交互自主生成推理和动作，无需教师。该设计解决了教师监督受学生先前推理干扰的问题，并提升了多轮场景下的动作探索效率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "宏平均成功率", "value": "31.74%", "setting": "Qwen3-4B学生，ALFWorld、ScienceWorld和WebShop，最强基线为26.07%"}, {"metric": "宏平均成功率（Qwen3-1.7B学生）", "value": "最高", "setting": "Qwen3-1.7B学生，ALFWorld、ScienceWorld和WebShop，在评估方法中最高"}, {"metric": "ALFWorld Seen成功率", "value": "超过Qwen3-32B教师", "setting": "Qwen3-4B学生，ALFWorld Seen"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 从交互状态视角分解多轮OPD，区分任务与动作-观察记录、学生内部语义轨迹，识别学生推理的双重角色（先前推理扭曲教师监督，当前推理影响同状态动作采样）。；State-Grounded On-Policy Distillation框架：将教师监督锚定在交互状态上，并在高响应熵状态重新分配采样预算以生成多个完整推理-动作响应，同时保留全历史学生生成。；实验表明State-OPD在ALFWorld、ScienceWorld和WebShop上取得最高宏平均成功率，且监督回合响应更少、减少持续失败动作重复。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for transferring reasoning capabilities from frontier models to smaller models, while extending its success to multi-turn agentic environments remains challenging. We study agentic OPD through interaction states, distinguishing the objective task and executed action-observation record from the internal semantic trajectory of student reasoning. Through this lens, we identify two roles of student reasoning in multi-turn OPD: prior reasoning distorts teacher supervision, while current reasoning shapes the action alternatives sampled at the same state. Specifically, at matched interaction states, removing prior student reasoning improves teacher continuation success and changes the teacher's token-level targets, with larger continuation gains at later turns. Meanwhile, resampling complete reasoning-action responses yields greater action diversity at the same state than resampling actions under a fixed reasoning trace. We introduce State-Grounded On-Policy Distillation, a framework that grounds teacher supervision in interaction states and reallocates the sampling budget to generate multiple complete reasoning-action responses at states with high response entropy, while retaining full-history student generation. Comprehensive evaluations across ALFWorld, ScienceWorld and WebShop demonstrate that State-OPD achieves the highest macro-average success rate among the evaluated methods for both Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B students. Notably, for Qwen3-4B, State-OPD raises macro-average success from 26.07\% with the strongest baseline to 31.74\% and surpasses its Qwen3-32B teacher on ALFWorld Seen. Further analyses reveal that State-OPD achieves higher task success with fewer supervised turn responses and substantially reduces persistent failed-action repetition; qualitative traces illustrate decision revision based on interaction feedback.

</details>


### [57] [CopRoute: Dependence-Aware Conformal Routing for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13276)
*Siva Rajesh Kasa,Santhosh Kumar Kasa,Karan Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CopRoute提出一种依赖感知的共形路由方法，通过考虑模型失败间的依赖关系，按每个模型能新纠正的查询数排序，并选择满足目标风险的最小模型集合。在RouterBench和RouterEval上，在相同认证风险下，调用次数最多减少79%，中位数减少24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集合式语言模型路由中，现有风险控制策略独立评分每个模型，忽略了模型失败之间的依赖结构，导致所选模型集可能冗余，造成不必要的推理调用。

Method: CopRoute是一种集合式语言模型路由方法。输入为查询和候选模型集合。首先，它在设计数据上按每个模型相对于已选集合能新正确回答的查询数对模型进行排序，形成依赖感知的排序列表。对于每个查询，它依据该排序决定截断点，然后选择最小的截断点使得认证的全漏检风险满足目标风险。该方法包含两个阶段：排序阶段和校准阶段。排序阶段优化固定顺序以最小化平均调用第一个正确模型的次数；校准阶段独立地认证部署集合的全漏检风险。推理时，对于新查询，按照排序顺序依次调用模型直到选定的截断点，从而在控制风险的同时减少不必要的调用。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "认证调用次数", "value": "在10个数据集中的9个取得最少认证调用次数", "setting": "RouterBench和RouterEval，目标风险0.05"}, {"metric": "认证调用次数", "value": "在10个数据集中的8个取得最少认证调用次数", "setting": "RouterBench和RouterEval，目标风险0.10"}, {"metric": "调用次数减少", "value": "最多减少79%（中位数24%）", "setting": "与最先进的风险控制策略在相同认证全漏检风险下比较"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出CopRoute，按每个模型相对于已选集合能新正确回答的查询数来排序模型，从而建模失败依赖；按查询决定沿排序向下选取到何处，选择满足目标风险的最小截断点；证明CopRoute的排序在平均调用第一个正确模型的次数上达到设计数据上任何高效固定排序算法的最优保证；使用单独的校准步骤认证部署集合的全漏检风险

Abstract: Set-based language model routing aims to increase the coverage of the event where at least one model answers a query correctly while controlling the number of inference calls made. Existing risk-controlled routing policies bound the all-miss risk (the probability that every model in the selected set fails) by calibrating a single conformal threshold and applying it to a nested family of model sets. Because each model is scored in isolation, these policies ignore the dependence structure of model failures. Models that share training data or architecture tend to fail on the same queries, so two individually strong models can be jointly redundant while a weaker but complementary model covers the queries that the others miss. To account for this dependence, which existing risk-controlled policies ignore, we propose CopRoute, which orders models by how many new queries each one answers correctly beyond those already selected, decides per query how far down that order to go, and then picks the smallest cutoff that still meets the target risk. We show that CopRoute's ordering attains, on the design data, the best guarantee any efficient fixed-ordering algorithm can give for the average number of calls to the first correct model, and that a separate calibration step certifies the all-miss risk of the deployed sets. On RouterBench and RouterEval, CopRoute attains the fewest certified calls on nine of ten datasets at a target risk of 0.05 and on eight of ten at 0.10, making up to 79 percent fewer calls (median 24 percent) than the state-of-the-art risk-controlled policy at the same certified all-miss risk.

</details>


### [58] [MHR-Router: Multimodal Heterogeneous Retrieval-Enhanced Adaptive Routing for VLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13278)
*Wenjuan Zhu,Zheming Yang,Yunqing Hu,Qi Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MHR-Router，一种多模态异构检索增强自适应路由框架，用于将图像-问题查询路由到最合适的 VLM 专家。通过融合冻结多模态特征并检索专家成败记忆，实现细粒度路由，实验显示准确率优于传统基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Selecting the most suitable vision-language model expert for a given image-question query under heterogeneous multimodal inputs and varying expert strengths.

Method: MHR-Router is a two-stage framework for routing multimodal queries to VLM experts. Stage 1, Multimodal Routing Representation, takes an image-question pair as input and fuses frozen multimodal features from pre-trained vision and language encoders into a comprehensive query representation. Stage 2, Heterogeneous Retrieval-Enhanced Adaptive Routing, uses this representation to retrieve expert-specific success and failure memories (local evidence) and feeds both the query representation and retrieved memories into a shared scorer. The scorer adaptively selects the optimal VLM expert for inference. The routing training likely optimizes the shared scorer to predict the best expert; the paper reports accuracy improvements over traditional baselines.

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Multimodal Routing Representation that fuses frozen multimodal features into a comprehensive query representation, avoiding costly training of the router on raw inputs.；Heterogeneous Retrieval-Enhanced Adaptive Routing that retrieves expert-specific success and failure memories as local evidence to guide routing.；A shared scorer that adaptively selects the optimal VLM expert based on the fused representation and retrieved evidence.

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit complementary capabilities, yet selecting the most suitable expert for a given image-question query remains challenging due to heterogeneous multimodal inputs and varying expert strengths. Existing routing approaches rely on text-based cues or static model profiles, failing to capture fine-grained visual evidence and expert-specific failure patterns. To address this, we propose MHR-Router, a multimodal heterogeneous retrieval-enhanced adaptive routing framework for VLM inference. It accurately dispatches each multimodal query to the most suitable VLM expert via local retrieval evidence. MHR-Router consists of two stages: (1) Multimodal Routing Representation, which fuses frozen multimodal features into a comprehensive query representation; and (2) Heterogeneous Retrieval-Enhanced Adaptive Routing, which retrieves expert-specific success and failure memories as local evidence and employs a shared scorer to adaptively select the optimal expert. Experiments show that MHR-Router effectively achieves heterogeneous modality routing and consistently improves accuracy over traditional baselines.

</details>


### [59] [Rethinking Relative Policy Differences Transfer of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13205)
*Rong Wu,Daocheng Fu,Yu Yang,Xuemeng Yang,Siqi Li,Shu Zou,Yao Zhu,Botian Shi,Bo Zhang,Cong Zhang,Pinlong Cai,Jianbiao Mei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为 on-policy distillation 建立统一理论视角，将 expert 与 reference 的对数概率比解释为隐式奖励，并系统研究 relative policy difference 何时可迁移。发现任务特定训练带来的差异可稳定迁移，而规模差异迁移弱；expert 提供能力，reference 过滤共享行为。对 LLM 后训练与蒸馏方法设计有启发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 on-policy distillation 中，哪些 relative policy difference 能提供有用监督、哪些目标模型能从中受益，以及 relative policy difference 的可迁移性由什么决定。

Method: 论文提出统一的理论框架：设定一个 proxy expert 和一个 proxy reference，定义两者对同一输入 token 的对数概率比 log(π_E/π_R) 作为隐式奖励，用于 KL 正则策略优化。在该视角下，目标策略在自身 rollout 上进行 token 级监督，其 on-policy 更新等价于用该隐式奖励进行策略梯度式更新，而传统的 on-policy distillation（直接匹配 expert 分布）成为 reference 为目标自身或均匀分布时的特例。该方法因此允许在规模、训练阶段或生成模式不同的模型之间进行 any-to-any 迁移。训练时，目标策略对每个 prompt 生成自己的 rollout，teacher（proxy expert）与 proxy reference 分别对同一 rollout 计算 token 概率，进而得到相对差异作为监督信号；目标策略根据该信号进行 on-policy 更新。推理时仅使用目标策略，不依赖 teacher 或 reference。论文通过系统性实验分析差异来源（任务特定训练 vs 规模差异）、expert/reference 的角色以及目标接受性，但未披露具体模型规模、训练数据、硬件或线上部署信息。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出统一视角：proxy expert 与 proxy reference 之间的 log-probability ratio 定义 KL 正则策略优化的隐式奖励，并诱导 on-policy 更新，将传统 OPD 作为特例。；系统实验发现：来自任务特定训练（RL/SFT）的差异可稳定迁移，而仅由模型规模产生的差异迁移效果弱或不可迁移。；阐明 expert 与 reference 的互补角色：expert 提供可迁移能力，reference 过滤共享行为并隔离相关残差。；指出迁移成功需要 induced update 使目标向 expert 移动且泛化到采样 rollout 之外；原始 policy distance 不能预测可迁移性，而合适的 reference 可挽救原本失败的迁移。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a target policy on its own rollouts using token-level supervision from a stronger teacher, but typically relies on the teacher being substantially more capable than the target. Recent work relaxes this requirement by transferring the relative policy difference between an expert and a reference policy rather than the expert's absolute distribution. Yet it remains unclear which differences provide useful supervision and which targets can benefit from them. We develop a unified view in which the log-probability ratio between a proxy expert and a proxy reference defines an implicit reward for KL-regularized policy optimization. This formulation induces an on-policy update for the target, recovers conventional OPD as a special case, and enables any-to-any transfer across models that differ in scale, training stage or generation. Our systematic experiments yield three findings. First, a stronger expert is not necessarily a better source of supervision: differences induced by task-specific training, including reinforcement learning and supervised fine-tuning, transfer consistently, whereas those arising from model scale alone transfer weakly or not at all. Second, the expert and reference serve complementary roles: the expert provides the capability to be transferred, while the reference filters out shared behavior and isolates the relevant residual. Third, even a useful difference does not transfer to every target: success requires the induced update to move the target toward the expert and generalize beyond sampled rollouts. Raw policy distance does not predict transferability, whereas an appropriate reference can rescue otherwise unsuccessful transfer. These results characterize when relative policy differences provide effective supervision for OPD.

</details>


### [60] [Monarch-MoE: Structured Experts for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13215)
*Xi Zhang,Boyu Diao,Zesen Chen,Ruiqi Liu,Hangda Liu,Yongjun Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Monarch-MoE 用块对角因子加固定置换替代 LoRA 专家的稠密因子，提供块级梯度隔离，并结合批计算、融合前向与优化反向，使结构化专家可在单张 24GB RTX 4090 上训练与推理；在 MLLM-DCL 和 UCIT 上适配器参数减少约 75% 和 50%，首任务遗忘低至 0.52 和 0.10 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态持续指令微调中的灾难性遗忘问题：多专家 LoRA 中稠密低秩专家更新仍会影响大量输出特征，难以在有限显存硬件上平衡知识保留与新任务学习。

Method: Monarch-MoE 面向多模态持续指令微调，以 LLaVA-1.5-7B 为基座模型。输入为多模态指令数据（图像与文本指令），基座模型冻结，仅训练专家适配器。其核心是将多专家 LoRA 中每个专家的稠密低秩分解矩阵（A、B）替换为块对角矩阵，并通过一个固定置换连接输入与输出维度。块对角结构使单个块对的梯度更新只作用于对应的输出块，实现块级梯度隔离，避免一次更新扰动大量输出特征；固定置换则保证所有输入块与输出块之间仍存在信息通路，维持模型表达能力。为弥补块对角专家参数紧凑但在显存受限设备上直接实现可能低效的问题，论文引入批计算将多个专家的计算合并、融合前向算子降低 kernel 启动与中间张量开销，并优化反向传播路径。训练目标仍是在持续学习设定下依次学习新任务，同时通过结构约束减轻对旧任务的遗忘。推理时，训练好的专家适配器与基座模型结合，在单张 24 GB RTX 4090 上完成多模态生成。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "适配器参数减少约 75%", "value": "约 75%", "setting": "MLLM-DCL 数据集，LLaVA-1.5-7B，对比基线未在摘要中具体说明"}, {"metric": "适配器参数减少约 50%", "value": "约 50%", "setting": "UCIT 数据集，LLaVA-1.5-7B，对比基线未在摘要中具体说明"}, {"metric": "首任务遗忘低至 0.52 个百分点", "value": "0.52 百分点", "setting": "MLLM-DCL，主对比实验"}, {"metric": "首任务遗忘低至 0.10 个百分点", "value": "0.10 百分点", "setting": "UCIT，主对比实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 用块对角因子并连接固定置换替换 LoRA 专家中的稠密低秩因子，实现块级梯度隔离，单个块对更新只影响一个输出块，同时保持所有输入输出块之间的连接。；为紧凑专家设计批计算、融合前向算子与优化反向传播，使其可在显存受限硬件上实际训练和推理。

Abstract: Learning new tasks in multimodal continual instruction tuning can cause catastrophic forgetting of previously acquired knowledge. Although multi-expert LoRA distributes learning across experts, updates to a dense low-rank expert can still affect many output features. We introduce Monarch-MoE, which replaces the dense factors in LoRA experts with block-diagonal factors connected by a fixed permutation. This structure provides blockwise gradient isolation: individual block-pair updates affect only one output block, while connections between all input and output blocks are maintained. To make these compact experts practical on memory-limited hardware, we combine batched computation, fused forward operators, and an optimized backward pass. Across three continual-learning methods evaluated on MLLM-DCL and UCIT with LLaVA-1.5-7B, Monarch-MoE improves first-task retention while reducing adapter parameters by approximately 75% and 50%, respectively. In the main comparison, first-task forgetting is as low as 0.52 percentage points on MLLM-DCL and 0.10 on UCIT, with method-dependent trade-offs in final performance. Our implementation supports fine-tuning and inference on a single 24 GB RTX 4090, with lower measured memory use and competitive runtime in the evaluated settings. Together, these algorithmic and systems improvements make structured experts a practical option for continual adaptation on memory-limited hardware.

</details>


### [61] [Can Failed Trajectories Reveal Where Agents Go Wrong? Agentic Failure Attribution Without Step-Level Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13105)
*Do Nguyen-Thanh,Junlin Fang,Bing Li,Zhen Fang,Sean Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOAP 提出在无步骤级标签的失败轨迹上进行 LLM Agent 失败归因，通过谱评分与注意力引导传播缓解下游污染，实验表明归因性能优于竞争基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅有轨迹级失败标签、无步骤级错误标注、且无成功参考轨迹的条件下，如何准确归因 LLM Agent 失败的决定性步骤。

Method: SOAP 首先利用 LLM 表征构建谱参考空间，并将每个步骤投影到该空间以获得基础错误分数；然后从注意力模式中推导软步骤依赖关系，并沿依赖关系将下游错误证据向上游传播，从而避免下游污染导致独立评分器偏好下游后果。论文未披露更多模块细节、训练目标或推理服务流程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 SOAP（Spectral scOring with Attention-guided Propagation）框架，无需步骤级训练标签和单独的成功参考集；基于 LLM 表征构建谱参考空间，根据每个步骤的投影行为分配基础错误分数；利用注意力模式推导软步骤依赖，将下游错误证据向上游潜在来源传播，以缓解下游污染

Abstract: Failure attribution for LLM agents aims to identify decisive steps responsible for task failure, which is critical for debugging and improving agentic systems. Existing methods rely on costly LLM prompting, step-level error annotations, or verified successful trajectories that lack failure signals for assistance. In this paper, we study failure attribution from step-unlabeled failure trajectories, where each trajectory is known to have failed but its decisive step is unknown. We propose SOAP (Spectral scOring with Attention-guided Propagation), an effective framework requiring neither step-level training labels nor a separate success-only reference set. SOAP first constructs a spectral reference space from LLM representations and assigns each step a base error score according to its projection behavior. Importantly, later steps may exhibit stronger failure signals because they inherit an earlier mistake, causing independent scorers to favor downstream consequences over the decisive error. To address this downstream contamination, SOAP derives soft step dependencies from attention patterns and propagates downstream error evidence toward its likely upstream sources. Extensive experiments show that SOAP consistently improves attribution performance over competitive baselines.

</details>


### [62] [Double-Take: Improving Capacity, Binding, and Selectivity In Long-Context Within Bidirectional Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13073)
*Vakul Raghavan,Utkarsh Grover,Mingyang Mao,Chengwen Wang,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究长上下文固定容量记忆的可用性，指出单步写入干扰限制容量、自联想写入无法跨位置绑定，提出 Double-Take 的多步无锚更新、跨位置混合与双向变化敏感写入，在 32kb 基因组上下文训练即可外推到 131kb（0.927 AUROC）乃至近 1Mb（0.856 AUROC），并零样本从人类迁移到小鼠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定大小记忆在上下文增长时虽然能存储信息，却无法保留跨位置关联与选择性结构，导致远距离信息不可用于检索；论文将问题分解为容量、绑定与选择性三个独立要求。

Method: Double-Take 面向 large-chunk test-time training（TTT）的记忆构建问题。输入为长序列分块后的大块数据，模型在测试时通过写入更新维护一个固定大小的记忆状态。论文诊断出 TTT 记忆的两类失败：一是单步写入会在记忆槽之间累积干扰，从而限制可存储关联的容量；二是自联想式写入只能形成自关联，无法把不同位置的信息绑定为跨位置关系。DT 的第一个模块是多步无锚更新，把一次写入拆成多个内部步骤，并在更新中移除对固定锚点的依赖，使记忆槽在写入过程中相互解耦，降低干扰、提升容量。第二个模块是跨位置混合，在写入前对当前块内不同位置的表征进行混合，使写入的记忆项编码跨位置联合关系，而不只是单点自联想。第三个模块是双向变化敏感写入，同时从序列两端计算变化信号，仅对发生显著变化、具有保留价值的关联进行写入，从而在容量有限时实现选择性保持。训练目标仍为 TTT 式的自监督预测/重建目标，并在 32kb 基因组上下文上训练；推理时模型以相同写入流程在更长的 131kb 乃至接近 1Mb 上下文上运行，无需再训练即可评估长程检索，并可从人类基因组零样本迁移到小鼠基因组。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "端到端召回准确率", "value": "0.10", "setting": "仅提升容量时，停留在随机猜测下限"}, {"metric": "端到端召回准确率", "value": "0.92", "setting": "增加跨位置绑定后"}, {"metric": "AUROC", "value": "0.927", "setting": "仅在 32kb 基因组上下文训练，测试于 131kb 上下文"}, {"metric": "AUROC", "value": "0.856", "setting": "仅在 32kb 基因组上下文训练，测试于接近 1Mb 上下文"}, {"metric": "零样本跨物种迁移", "value": "人类训练后迁移至小鼠", "setting": "零样本迁移，论文称成功转移"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 多步无锚更新（multi-step unpinned updates）缓解单步写入的干扰累积，提升记忆容量；跨位置混合（cross-position mixing）使自联想写入能够形成跨位置绑定；双向变化敏感写入（bidirectional change-sensitive writes）实现选择性保留

Abstract: Processing a long sequence does not guarantee that distant information remains usable. As context grows, a fixed-size memory must retain many associations, connect information across positions, and preserve relevant structure without being overwhelmed by everything it observes. These are distinct requirements: a memory may store more information without representing the relationships needed for retrieval, and it may transport those relationships without preserving the ones that matter. Thus, the challenge is not simply extending context length, but constructing a memory that remains usable as context grows. We study this problem in large-chunk test-time training (TTT) and identify two basic failures: single-step writes accumulate interference, limiting capacity, while self-associative writes cannot form cross-position bindings. We introduce Double-Take (DT), which repairs these failures with multi-step unpinned updates and cross-position mixing, and adds bidirectional change-sensitive writes for selective retention. Capacity alone leaves end-to-end recall at the $0.10$ chance floor; adding binding raises it to $0.92$. Trained only on $32\,\mathrm{kb}$ genomic contexts, DT reaches $0.927$ AUROC at $131\,\mathrm{kb}$ and $0.856$ near $1\,\mathrm{Mb}$, while transferring zero-shot from human to mouse. Together, these results identify capacity, binding, and selectivity as three requirements for usable long-range memory.

</details>


### [63] [CB-Orchestrator: Adaptive Workflow Optimization for LLM Agents via Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13064)
*Jiahang Sun,Zhiwei Shang,Zhipiao Liu,Hongwei Yang,Guoqing Xie,Shuang Qiu,Zhongxiang Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CB-Orchestrator，通过进化搜索构建候选工作流池，并将工作流选择建模为上下文赌博机问题，实现样本高效的自适应工作流选择。在五个基准上超越所有基线，MATH 数据集上训练阶段 API token 开销降低至少 40.55%，端到端训练 token 开销降低至少 29.73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动设计 LLM Agent 工作流成本高且难以适应不同查询；现有自动化方法在训练阶段 API 反馈收集成本高，且无法自适应复用高质量工作流，导致性能与成本效率瓶颈。

Method: CB-Orchestrator 分为两阶段。第一阶段：通过进化搜索生成一个多样化的候选工作流池，每个工作流是解决特定任务的一组操作序列（如推理步骤、工具调用等）；此阶段不依赖训练反馈，以离线方式构建。第二阶段：将工作流选择建模为上下文赌博机问题，上下文（context）是输入的查询特征，动作（action）是候选池中的某个工作流，奖励（reward）是所选工作流在该查询上的表现。通过探索与利用的平衡，样本高效地学习选择策略，大幅减少训练阶段需要的反馈（如 API 调用）次数。推理时，给定新查询，模型提取上下文特征并执行已学习的选择策略，自适应地选取最优工作流来执行该查询，无需人工干预。整体流程：查询输入 → 上下文特征提取 → 上下文赌博机选择工作流 → 执行工作流 → 输出结果。训练目标是最小化累积遗憾（未明确披露）或最大化期望奖励。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "性能", "value": "优于所有基线", "setting": "五个多样化基准（未列具体数值）"}, {"metric": "训练阶段 API token 开销", "value": "降低不少于 40.55%", "setting": "MATH 数据集，与强近期基线对比"}, {"metric": "端到端训练 token 开销（含工作流池生成）", "value": "降低不少于 29.73%", "setting": "MATH 数据集，与强近期基线对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将工作流生成与选择解耦：第一阶段通过进化搜索构建多样化候选工作流池，第二阶段将工作流选择形式化为上下文赌博机问题。；利用上下文赌博机平衡探索与利用，实现样本高效学习，显著减少训练阶段所需的反馈量。；推理时根据每个查询自适应选择最优工作流。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in tackling complex tasks through agentic workflows. However, manually designing these workflows is labor-intensive and lacks flexibility to adapt to diverse queries. While automated workflow optimization is a promising direction, existing methods often incur expensive API costs during training-phase feedback collection. Furthermore, they fail to adaptively reuse high-quality workflows, which creates a bottleneck for both performance and cost-effectiveness. To address these limitations, we propose CB-Orchestrator, an adaptive framework that decouples workflow generation from selection. In the first stage, we construct a diverse pool of candidate workflows via evolutionary search. In the second stage, we formulate workflow selection as a Contextual Bandit problem, which enables sample-efficient learning by balancing exploration and exploitation, thereby significantly reducing the feedback required for training. During inference, the model adaptively selects the optimal workflow tailored to each query. Evaluations across five diverse benchmarks demonstrate that CB-Orchestrator consistently outperforms all baselines. Notably, on the MATH dataset, CB-Orchestrator achieves better performance than strong recent baselines while reducing training-phase API token overhead by no less than 40.55% and total end-to-end training (including workflow pool generation) token overhead by no less than 29.73%.

</details>


### [64] [Trajectory-Aware Policy Optimization: Closing the State Distribution Gap in Long-Horizon LLM RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13020)
*Zhicheng Cai,Weinan Dai,Yuyan Zhou,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TAPO 是一种 LLM 强化学习框架，通过从 off-policy 策略梯度推导出状态分布校正项，并结合几何平均方差缩减与长度感知动态裁剪，弥合长程 rollout 中的状态分布差距，在八个基准上比 GRPO 有显著提升，训练更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长程自回归 rollout 中，局部策略不匹配会累积为严重的轨迹级状态分布偏移；现有的 token 级或序列级方法（GRPO、GSPO）忽略了这种偏移，导致训练不稳定，并随着训练时域增长最终崩溃。

Method: TAPO 以 LLM 的强化学习后训练循环为操作对象。输入是在策略下生成的输出 token 的自回归 rollout，构成轨迹。它将长程 rollout 识别为一种状态分布偏移问题：token 级或序列级的比率控制（如 GRPO/GSPO）无法纠正策略诱导的状态分布漂移。TAPO 从 off-policy 策略梯度目标出发，解析地推导出状态分布校正项，并将其纳入 RL 目标，从而优化加权后的策略梯度，使其考虑到轨迹级分布偏移。为了降低校正项带来的方差，它采用几何平均估计器而非算术平均。为了防止训练不稳定，它应用长度感知动态裁剪，根据轨迹长度调整允许的更新幅度，避免在长 horizon 上出现过大偏离的更新。该框架被集成到 RL 训练目标中，并在八个竞赛级基准上进行评估，显示出优于 GRPO 的数学推理与算法编程能力，同时训练稳定性得到提升。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "相对 GRPO 的改进", "value": "最高 48%", "setting": "数学推理，八个竞赛级基准"}, {"metric": "相对 GRPO 的改进", "value": "12%", "setting": "算法编程，八个竞赛级基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 从 off-policy 策略梯度出发，在 RL 目标中从理论上推导出状态分布校正，以弥合状态分布差距；采用几何平均估计器来降低方差；长度感知动态裁剪，以防止整条轨迹上出现过度偏离的更新

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a dominant paradigm for enhancing LLMs' reasoning capabilities. Yet long-horizon autoregressive rollouts introduce a fundamental limitation: local policy mismatch can compound into severe state distribution shift over trajectories. Existing LLM RL methods, such as GRPO and GSPO, control policy mismatch at the token or sequence level, but overlook this trajectory-induced shift, causing training instability and eventual collapse as the horizon grows. To close the state distribution gap, we propose Trajectory-Aware Policy Optimization (TAPO), a principled framework that theoretically derives the state-distribution correction from the off-policy policy gradient and incorporates it into the RL objective. TAPO further employs a geometric-mean estimator for variance reduction, together with a length-aware dynamic clipping that prevents overly deviated updates across the trajectory. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TAPO significantly surpasses existing LLM RL algorithms across eight competition-level benchmarks, achieving up to 48% and 12% improvement over GRPO on mathematical reasoning and algorithmic coding, with substantially enhanced training stability.

</details>


### [65] [Personalized Federated Direct Preference Optimization via LoRA Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12885)
*Zhanhong Jiang,Soumik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: pFedDPO 在联邦 DPO 框架中，让每个客户端维护私有 LoRA 适配器、共享基座模型通过 FedAvg 同步，从而在保护隐私的同时个性化对齐 LLM。理论证明其将梯度多样性替换为更小的有效残差异质性，实验表明梯度范数更低且训练损失/奖励准确率有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦直接偏好优化（FedDPO）假设所有客户端收敛到单一共享策略，与实践中异构偏好分布相矛盾，导致梯度多样性 κ² 较大、收敛分析不准确。

Method: pFedDPO 采用共享全局基座模型 + 客户端私有 LoRA 适配器的分解架构。输入为各客户端的本地偏好数据（如偏好对）。每个客户端在本地基座模型（共享参数）上附加一个小的 LoRA 适配器，并仅用本地偏好数据优化适配器参数（以及可能解冻的基座部分）。每轮通信中，服务器通过 FedAvg 聚合各客户端的基座模型参数（共享基座同步），而 LoRA 适配器保留在本地不参与聚合，从而捕获客户端特定偏好。训练目标为直接偏好优化（DPO）损失，优化客户端本地偏好。理论分析将收敛界中的梯度多样性 κ² 项替换为有效残差异质性 κ_eff²，并引入加性适配器偏差项。推理时，各客户端使用其本地基座模型 + 私有 LoRA 适配器进行个性化推理。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "gradient norm", "value": "consistent lower than vanilla FedDPO", "setting": "preference datasets"}, {"metric": "training loss", "value": "competitive with vanilla FedDPO", "setting": "preference datasets"}, {"metric": "per-client reward accuracy", "value": "competitive with vanilla FedDPO", "setting": "preference datasets"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 pFedDPO：每个客户端在共享全局基座模型上维护私有 LoRA 适配器，基座模型通过 FedAvg 每轮同步，适配器本地保留以捕获客户端特定偏好。；理论证明该分解将 FedDPO 收敛界中的梯度多样性 κ² 替换为有效残差异质性 κ_eff²（在适配器吸收客户端特定方向时不大于 κ²），代价是加性适配器偏差项随适配器收敛而消失。

Abstract: Federated Direct Preference Optimization (FedDPO) enables privacy-preserving alignment of language models across distributed agents without centralizing sensitive preference data. However, existing FedDPO analysis assumes all clients converge to a single shared policy, which is fundamentally at odds with the heterogeneous preference distributions present in practice. We propose Personalized FedDPO (pFedDPO), in which each client maintains a small private LoRA adapter atop a shared global base model. In pFedDPO, the base model is synchronized via FedAvg each round, while LoRA adapters remain local and capture client-specific preference patterns. We prove that this decomposition replaces the gradient diversity $\kappa^2$ in the FedDPO bound with a residual heterogeneity $\kappa^2_{\mathrm{eff}}$, which is no larger than $\kappa^2$ when adapters absorb client-specific directions, at the cost of an additive adapter-bias term that vanishes as adapters converge. Experiments on preference datasets show that pFedDPO consistently attains a lower gradient norm than vanilla FedDPO while remaining competitive in training loss and per-client reward accuracy.

</details>


### [66] [TreeHCA: Hindsight-Guided Exploration and Credit Assignment for Multi-Turn LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12828)
*Debajoy Mukherjee,Jeremy Carleton,Sushil Vemuri,Srinivas Shakkottai,Dileep Kalathil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TreeHCA 提出将后见引导探索与后见信用分配耦合的多轮 LLM agent 强化学习方法，通过构建推理/工具交互树并做归一化树备份，将终端结果转化为 turn 级 advantage，在七个问答基准与三个 Qwen 主干上取得最高聚合 exact match。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Outcome-supervised GRPO 对同一轨迹中所有 token 分配相同 advantage，无法区分多轮 LLM agent 轨迹中有用与无用的决策，而既有后见信用分配依赖轨迹中存在信息量高的替代分支。

Method: TreeHCA 面向多轮 LLM agent 的强化学习训练。输入是 agent 与环境（含工具调用）交互产生的多轮轨迹；训练时，标准 GRPO 只根据最终结果给整条轨迹同一 advantage，无法区分中间决策。TreeHCA 首先做后见引导探索：以参考答案似然的变化作为信号，回溯到中间历史，并在这些关键历史处采样替代续写，从而把单条轨迹扩展为一棵推理与工具交互树。由于探索采样改变了续写分布，论文推导了对应的 inverse-hindsight importance correction，并给出精确有限池恒等式，用于校正这种分布偏移带来的估计偏差。随后利用树结构进行归一化树备份，把终端结果沿树向上传播到每个 turn 的 advantage，使不同轮次获得差异化信用。训练目标仍基于 group-relative 策略优化，但 advantage 由树备份给出。推理路径是训练完成后的策略直接进行多轮问答与工具交互。论文在七个问答基准和三个 Qwen 主干上验证，未披露模型规模、训练硬件、数据量或推理延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: TreeHCA 将后见引导探索与后见信用分配耦合：利用参考答案似然变化回溯中间历史并采样替代续写，构建推理与工具交互树。；针对探索改变采样续写分布的问题，推导 inverse-hindsight importance correction，并给出精确有限池恒等式。；采用归一化树备份，将终端结果传播为 turn 级别 advantage。；以共享后见结构作为核心设计原则，把信息量探索与后见信用分配统一起来。

Abstract: Outcome-supervised group-relative policy optimization (GRPO) assigns the same advantage to every token in an LLM agent trajectory, leaving useful and unhelpful decisions undifferentiated. Hindsight credit assignment (HCA) can refine this feedback, but its estimates rely on trajectories containing informative alternatives at consequential histories. We introduce TreeHCA, which couples hindsight-guided exploration with hindsight credit assignment. TreeHCA uses changes in reference-answer likelihood to revisit intermediate histories and sample alternative continuations, forming a tree of reasoning and tool interactions. Because this exploration changes the distribution of sampled continuations, we derive the corresponding inverse-hindsight importance correction, including an exact finite-pool identity. We use this structure in a normalized tree backup that propagates terminal outcomes into turn-level advantages. This shared hindsight structure is TreeHCA's central design principle, linking informative exploration with hindsight-guided credit assignment. Across seven question-answering benchmarks and three Qwen backbones, TreeHCA outperforms all baselines in aggregate exact match, validating the effectiveness of paired exploration and credit assignment for multi-turn LLM agent training. Our code is available at this http URL .

</details>


### [67] [Deploying RDMA for Large-Scale Inference and Training Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12815)
*Yibo Huang,Yi Cui,Yukai Huang,Huaping Zhou,Jiancong Tong,Zhaoyu Wu,Gen Miao,Haoran Wei,Boyan Ding,Zhuo Jiang,Jianxi Ye,Yibo Zhu,Chuanxiong Guo,Yang Xu,Jie Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 字节跳动在生产参数服务器推理与训练系统中部署 RDMA 通信层 ByteExpress，通过 RDMA-aware 分配器、零拷贝、QP 多路复用、接收信用流控、软件发送窗口和 RPC 辅助恢复，在生产推理中降低平均/P99 参数拉取延迟 35.7%/43.7%、CPU 使用率 10.69%，训练中降低 P99 参数推送延迟最高 74.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在字节跳动的生产参数服务器推理与训练系统中，如何利用 RDMA 降低通信开销，同时应对变长消息、并发连接、突发 incast 和服务故障等硬件卸载无法解决的挑战。

Method: ByteExpress 是部署在硬件 RDMA 之上的软件通信层，服务于字节跳动参数服务器架构的推理与训练系统。其架构包含以下关键模块：1）RDMA-aware 分配器：在应用层与传输层之间统一管理缓冲区，避免变长消息带来的内存碎片与拷贝开销；2）零拷贝传输：数据直接在应用缓冲区与网卡之间传递，减少 CPU 参与；3）QP 多路复用：将多个并发连接复用到少量队列对上，降低连接管理开销并提升可扩展性；4）接收信用流控：通过接收端授予信用来控制发送速率，避免突发 incast 导致的接收端溢出；5）软件发送窗口：在软件层维护未完成数据窗口，协调发送速率与接收容量；6）RPC 辅助恢复：在传输层之上实现故障恢复，当连接或服务失败时通过 RPC 重新建立上下文。推理时，参数服务器使用 ByteExpress 拉取模型参数，训练时推送模型参数。这些模块共同解决变长消息、并发连接、突发 incast 和故障恢复问题，从而降低延迟与 CPU 开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "平均模型参数拉取延迟", "value": "降低 35.7%", "setting": "生产推理，与内核 TCP 对比"}, {"metric": "P99 模型参数拉取延迟", "value": "降低 43.7%", "setting": "生产推理，与内核 TCP 对比"}, {"metric": "总 CPU 使用率", "value": "降低 10.69%", "setting": "生产推理，与内核 TCP 对比"}, {"metric": "P99 模型参数推送延迟", "value": "降低最高 74.0%", "setting": "训练，与 TCP 对比，运行时未观察到 PFC 暂停"}]}

Conclusion: RDMA-aware 内存分配器，跨应用层与传输层进行缓冲区管理；零拷贝传输、QP 多路复用、接收信用流控与软件发送窗口协同设计；传输层之上的 RPC 辅助恢复机制，提升服务可用性；总结出三条生产部署经验：缓冲区管理、连接/接收容量/未完成数据的协调管理、传输层之上的恢复

Abstract: RDMA can reduce communication overhead in distributed machine learning systems, but realizing these benefits in production requires more than replacing TCP. Variable-size messages, concurrent connections, bursty incasts, and service failures introduce challenges that hardware offload alone cannot address. This paper reports our experience deploying ByteExpress, a software communication layer over hardware RDMA, in ByteDance's parameter-server-based inference and training systems. We present three lessons from the deployment: efficient RDMA requires buffer management across application and transport layers; scalable communication requires coordinated management of connections, receive capacity, and outstanding data; and service availability requires recovery above the transport layer. ByteExpress combines an RDMA-aware allocator, zero-copy transfers, QP multiplexing, receive-credit flow control, a software send window, and RPC-assisted recovery. In production inference, ByteExpress reduces mean and P99 model parameter fetch latency by 35.7% and 43.7%, respectively, and total CPU usage by 10.69% compared with kernel TCP. In training, ByteExpress reduces P99 model parameter push latency by up to 74.0% compared with TCP, with no PFC pauses observed at runtime.

</details>


### [68] [Byzantine-Resilient Federated Large Language Model via Hypergraph Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12711)
*Kai Li,Hanlin Cai,Zihao Liu,Yichen Li,Jose Alcaraz Calero,Wei Ni,Falko Dressler,Özgür B. Akan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出HMP-GAE，利用超图消息传递图自编码器在联邦LLM聚合中检测和抑制拜占庭攻击，通过高阶代理关系推理和历史检查点对比，识别语义不一致的更新。实验表明在AG News和Yahoo! Answers上使用Qwen-2.5和Llama-3.2时，全局准确率、语义熵和困惑度均优于现有防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦大语言模型训练中，恶意模型更新（拜占庭攻击）会破坏语义一致性和事实性，如何在无法访问本地私有数据的情况下，识别并抑制被操纵的更新，同时保持全局模型精度。

Method: HMP-GAE框架将联邦学习中的低秩模型更新表示为超图节点，利用超边捕获多个代理之间的高阶语义关系。通过迭代消息传递，模型聚合节点特征并更新节点表示，从而学习跨代理的关系推理。框架结合历史检查点（历史模型更新）作为参考，通过图自编码器重构误差来识别语义不一致的更新。训练目标包括重构损失以保持正常更新的语义一致性，以及联合优化问题最大化全局精度并抑制被操纵更新。在联邦聚合阶段，HMP-GAE对每个更新进行异常评分，据此调整聚合权重，从而抑制恶意更新，增强全局模型的鲁棒性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "全局准确率", "value": "提升1.21%", "setting": "与现有防御方法对比，在AG News和Yahoo! Answers数据集上，使用Qwen-2.5和Llama-3.2骨干的FedLLM"}, {"metric": "最终模型分类语义熵", "value": "降低44.08%", "setting": "与现有防御方法对比"}, {"metric": "困惑度", "value": "降低13.34%", "setting": "与现有防御方法对比"}, {"metric": "全局准确率", "value": "提升1.39%", "setting": "与基于GAE的防御方法对比"}, {"metric": "语义熵", "value": "降低54.5%", "setting": "与基于GAE的防御方法对比"}, {"metric": "困惑度", "value": "降低35.5%", "setting": "与基于GAE的防御方法对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将低秩模型更新表示为超图节点，通过迭代消息传递捕获多个代理之间的高阶语义关系；利用历史检查点和跨代理关系推理，识别并抑制语义不一致的更新；将防御问题形式化为同时最大化全局精度和抑制被操纵更新的联合优化问题

Abstract: Federated Large Language Models (FedLLMs) are promising to deliver privacy-preserving, collaborative intelligence. The decentralized training process is vulnerable to Byzantine attacks that corrupt semantic consistency and factual resilience through malicious model updates. This paper presents a new resilience strategy that prevents semantically corrupted knowledge from propagating during federated aggregation. A defense problem is formulated to jointly maximize global accuracy and suppress manipulated model updates, enabling trustworthy knowledge aggregation without accessing private local data. To strengthen the resilience of FedLLMs, we develop a novel Hypergraph Message Passing Graph Autoencoder (HMP-GAE) framework, where low-rank model updates are represented as nodes in a hypergraph and high-order semantic relationships among multiple agents are exploited through iterative message passing. By leveraging historical checkpoints and cross-agent relational reasoning, HMP-GAE identifies and suppresses semantically inconsistent updates while preserving knowledge alignment among agents throughout federated training. HMP-GAE is implemented using Qwen-2.5 and Llama-3.2 as pretrained FedLLM backbones and evaluated on AG News and Yahoo! Answers datasets. Compared to existing defenses, HMP-GAE improves global accuracy by $1.21$\%, reduces final-model classification semantic entropy by $44.08$\%, and lowers perplexity by $13.34$\%. HMP-GAE outperforms the GAE-based defense by improving the accuracy $1.39$\%, reducing the semantic entropy by $54.5$\%, and lowering perplexity by $35.5$\%.

</details>


### [69] [SynAct: Synergistic Active Feedback Learning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12651)
*Ning Liu,Ian Li,Kristina Klinkner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SynAct提出决策级主动反馈学习：用重复rollout中动作与结果间的互信息定位高价值决策，向专家查询并把修正蒸馏为可复用规则，在16个Agent设置上平均提升11.7pp，并可迁移至推理时路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专家反馈稀缺的约束下，如何将有限的专家注意力分配到最关键的决策点，使LLM Agent多步工作流中的可恢复失误与知识缺口得到最有效的修复。

Method: SynAct将专家注意力视为绑定约束，在决策粒度上分配反馈。输入为Agent执行多步工作流的状态与候选动作；对每个决策点进行k次重复rollout，记录所选动作与最终结果。计算动作与最终结果之间的互信息，据此对决策排序；选择互信息最高的决策向专家查询修正。专家修正被蒸馏为可迁移规则，存入规则库供Agent在部署时无需专家访问即可复用。训练目标包含两部分：一是通过互信息排序实现主动决策选择，二是将修正蒸馏为规则以泛化到新任务。推理时，Agent先按规则库执行；同一互信息信号可迁移至推理时路由，动态选择是否调用专家或更昂贵路径。理论部分给出有限重复采样下的可识别性条件：结果恒定决策互信息为0，结果分离决策互信息至少H2(m/k) bits，从而在任意阈值t∈(0,H2(m/k))下精确分离。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "held-out accuracy", "value": "+11.7pp average", "setting": "四个Agent基准、四个模型（跨三个家族），全部16个评估设置均提升"}, {"metric": "推理时路由胜出设置数", "value": "15/16", "setting": "与预算匹配的cascade、uncertainty、expert-judge路由器对比"}, {"metric": "单次定向修正 vs 七次额外无目标重试", "value": "单次定向修正更优", "setting": "未提供具体数值，仅描述性结论"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出决策级主动反馈分配：用重复rollout中动作与最终结果之间的互信息对决策排序，而非任务级或成功驱动的反馈请求。；理论刻画有限重复采样下的信号可识别性：k次rollout、每动作至少m次观测时，结果恒定决策互信息为0，结果分离决策互信息至少H2(m/k) bits，并解释成功驱动采集在全部失败时的盲区。；将专家修正蒸馏为可迁移规则，使Agent在部署时无需专家访问即可复用；同一信号可迁移至推理时路由，优于预算匹配的级联、不确定性和专家评判路由器。

Abstract: LLM agents executing multi-step workflows fail in two fundamentally different ways: recoverable slips, which further attempts can repair, and knowledge gaps, which retrying cannot resolve. Expert feedback can address the latter, but expert attention is scarce. The key challenge is therefore not whether to ask for feedback, but where feedback can change the outcome. Existing approaches either avoid expert feedback, allowing agents to reinforce their own errors, or allocate feedback at the task level without identifying consequential decisions. We present SynAct, an active learning framework that treats expert attention as the binding constraint and allocates feedback at the level of individual decisions. SynAct ranks decisions by the mutual information between the selected action and the terminal outcome across repeated rollouts, queries an expert at the highest-value decisions, and distills the resulting corrections into transferable rules that the agent can reuse without expert access at deployment. We theoretically characterize what this signal identifies under finite repeated sampling: with k rollouts and at least m observations per action, outcome-constant decisions have zero mutual information, while outcome-separating decisions have mutual information at least H2(m/k) bits, yielding exact separation for any threshold t in (0,H2(m/k)). This also explains why success-based acquisition can be blind when all rollouts fail: success is constant even when failed runs reach different outcomes. Across four agent benchmarks and four models spanning three families, SynAct improves held-out accuracy in all 16 evaluated settings, by +11.7pp on average. The same signal transfers to inference-time routing, where SynAct outperforms budget-matched cascade, uncertainty, and expert-judge routers in 15 of 16 settings, while a single targeted correction outperforms seven additional unaimed retries.

</details>


### [70] [STAPLE: Static Sparse Upcycling of Pretrained Checkpoints via Embedding Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12599)
*Ranajoy Sadhukhan,Euijin Hong,Sheng Cao,Zechun Liu,Shuowei Jin,Sha Li,Changlong Yu,Xin Liu,Qin Lu,Zhenyu Shi,Priyanka Nigam,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STAPLE提出了一种在预训练检查点上通过静态稀疏嵌入模块进行上采样的方法，保留原始FFN上投影并添加token索引嵌入作为残差。在OLMo-2 1B上相比持续密集训练，HumanEval提升4.27分，MBPP提升2.80分，GSM8K提升2.27分，DROP提升1.65分。性能随静态容量提升，并在7B模型上保持。相比MoE上采样基线，平均分数更高，解码速度快2倍，GPU内存减少超过一半。host-offloaded实现下生成吞吐损失仅1.5%，训练吞吐损失仅3%，仅缓存5%高频嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在不进行昂贵的稀疏预训练的前提下，将静态稀疏记忆高效集成到预训练模型中，以扩大参数容量而不同比例增加计算量。

Method: STAPLE构建于STEM（静态稀疏记忆）之上。标准STEM将FFN上投影替换为token索引嵌入，而STAPLE保留原有的FFN上投影，同时将静态稀疏嵌入作为残差通路添加到网络。输入经过预训练模型的计算路径不变，额外引入的token索引嵌入根据token动态更新，学习互补的token特定信息。训练时，在预训练检查点上进行上采样，只更新新增的嵌入参数（可能包括部分原有参数），采用host-offloaded实现将嵌入存储在CPU内存，仅缓存最高频的约5%嵌入在GPU。推理时，根据输入token查询对应嵌入，若未缓存则从主机内存获取，与原有FFN计算并行，作为残差相加。训练目标延续预训练的自回归语言建模损失。该方法通过静态稀疏记忆提供可预测的内存访问和token特定更新，避免MoE的专家同质化和系统开销，实现样本高效且可扩展的模型容量扩展。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "HumanEval", "value": "+4.27分", "setting": "OLMo-2 1B，对比持续密集训练"}, {"metric": "MBPP", "value": "+2.80分", "setting": "OLMo-2 1B，对比持续密集训练"}, {"metric": "GSM8K", "value": "+2.27分", "setting": "OLMo-2 1B，对比持续密集训练"}, {"metric": "DROP", "value": "+1.65分", "setting": "OLMo-2 1B，对比持续密集训练"}, {"metric": "平均性能", "value": "随静态容量增加而提升", "setting": "OLMo-2 1B"}, {"metric": "平均分数", "value": "高于MoE upcycling基线", "setting": "OLMo-2 1B，同时解码速度快2倍，GPU模型内存减少一半以上"}, {"metric": "生成吞吐", "value": "与密集基线相差1.5%以内", "setting": "host-offloaded实现，仅缓存约5%高频嵌入"}, {"metric": "训练吞吐", "value": "与密集基线相差3%以内", "setting": "host-offloaded实现"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: STAPLE方法：保留预训练FFN上投影，将token索引嵌入作为残差通路添加，既保留预训练计算又学习token特定信息。；基于STEM的静态稀疏记忆集成方案，实现样本高效、可扩展的模型扩展。；在host-offloaded实现下，仅缓存约5%高频嵌入，保持生成吞吐损失在1.5%以内、训练吞吐损失在3%以内。

Abstract: Sparse computation can scale the parametric capacity of LLMs without proportionally increasing active computation, but pretraining sparse architectures remains expensive and data-hungry. Existing sparse upcycling methods mainly target Mixture-of-Experts models, where expert homogeneity, sample inefficiency, and system overheads can limit practical scalability. Static sparse memory offers predictable access and token-specific updates that can help address these challenges, but naively integrating it into pretrained computation yields poor returns. In this work, we introduce STAPLE, a recipe built upon STEM for integrating static sparse memory into pretrained models. Whereas STEM replaces FFN up-projections with token-indexed embeddings, STAPLE retains them and adds the embeddings as a residual pathway, preserving pretrained computation while learning complementary token-specific information in a scalable manner. On OLMo-2 1B, STAPLE improves over continued dense training by 4.27 points on HumanEval, 2.80 on MBPP, 2.27 on GSM8K, and 1.65 on DROP. Average performance increases with static capacity, and improvements persist on the larger OLMo-2 7B backbone. STAPLE also outperforms static sparse baselines such as STEM and an Engram-style model, and achieves a higher average score than an MoE upcycling baseline while decoding 2x faster with less than half its GPU model memory and measured GPU energy per token in our throughput-oriented decoding evaluation. Importantly, despite introducing billions of parameters late in training, our host-offloaded implementation keeps generation throughput within 1.5% and training throughput within 3% of the dense baseline, while caching only the ~5% most frequent embeddings on GPU. These results show that static sparse memory can provide a sample-efficient, scalable, and sustainable path for expanding pretrained models without sparse pretraining from scratch.

</details>


### [71] [Sample-Adaptive Zeroth-Order Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12566)
*Haiyuan Wan,Yanyi Li,Weicheng Lin,Cong Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAZO 提出样本自适应零阶优化方法，通过在线学习样本重要性并动态分配查询来加速大语言模型微调，无需真实梯度，理论证明匹配最小方差 oracle 且收敛更快，实验显示取得更好的下游性能并最多快6倍收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零阶优化方法在微调大语言模型时均匀采样训练样本，忽略了样本重要性的动态变化，导致估计方差高、收敛慢。

Method: SAZO 在零阶优化框架上工作。给定预训练大语言模型和下游训练样本，它不利用反向传播计算梯度，而是通过随机方向上的函数差分来估计梯度。SAZO 的核心在于自适应地分配查询：为每个训练样本维护一个标量重要性权重，该权重由从已有方向差分中提取的梯度范数信息在线更新。由于只能从稀疏的小批量反馈中获取观测，SAZO 对每个样本的权重使用指数移动平均（EMA）来聚合跨更新的观测，从而跟踪样本重要性的动态变化。在每次迭代中，根据当前样本重要性分布进行采样，将更多查询分配给重要样本，以降低梯度估计的方差。该方法不需要额外的模型评估，仅比 MeZO 多出 O(N) 的标量状态（N 为样本数）。训练目标是最小化下游任务的损失；理论分析表明，SAZO 的收敛速率渐近匹配最小方差采样 oracle 的领先项，并严格快于标准方法。推理路径与训练解耦，微调后的模型用于标准下游推理。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "收敛速度（达到相同训练损失所需查询次数）", "value": "最多快6倍", "setting": "四个模型家族的实验"}, {"metric": "平均下游性能", "value": "提升（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "四个模型家族的下游任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出样本自适应零阶优化 SAZO，在线学习样本重要性并自适应分配查询以加速收敛；从已有的方向差分中提取梯度范数信息来估计样本重要性，无需访问真实梯度；为每个样本维护指数移动平均（EMA），以从稀疏小批量反馈中跟踪演变的重要性；证明了 SAZO 渐近匹配最小方差采样 oracle 的领先收敛项，并具有严格更快的收敛速率

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization offers a memory-efficient alternative to backpropagation for fine-tuning large language models. However, existing ZO methods typically sample training examples uniformly, overlooking their evolving importance and leaving avoidable estimation variance. To address this limitation, we propose Sample-Adaptive Zeroth-Order Optimization (SAZO), which learns sample importance online and adaptively allocates queries to accelerate convergence. SAZO extracts gradient-norm information from existing directional differences to estimate sample importance without access to true gradients. Furthermore, to track evolving importance from sparse minibatch feedback, SAZO maintains per-example exponential moving averages that aggregate observations across updates. The method requires no additional model evaluations and only $O(N)$ additional scalar state compared with MeZO. We prove that SAZO asymptotically matches the leading convergence term of the minimum-variance sampling oracle, and achieves a strictly faster convergence rate than standard methods. Extensive experiments across four model families demonstrate improved average downstream performance and up to $6\times$ faster convergence to the same training loss in terms of query count.

</details>


### [72] [FORTE: A Production Framework for Feature Evaluation and Deprecation in Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13806)
*Vishnu Arun,Adam Obeng,Tianyou Wang,Zilin Ren,Mukuntha Narayanan Sundararaman,Han Wang,Shivin Thukral,Zhixiang Wang,Saurabh Vishwas Joshi,Darren Reger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Pinterest 提出的 FORTE 生产框架，用于推荐系统中特征评估和删除。通过高效扰动、分组消融和谱系追踪，识别可安全删除的特征集，并在两个大型系统上验证。在线 A/B 测试显示无回归，大型排序模型参数减少 29.9%，吞吐量提升 19.8-25.5%，年训练成本节省数十万美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产推荐系统中特征删除难以优先级排序，原因包括特征间交互、重要性日变、无法同步评估、争抢 A/B 测试空间、在线回归风险以及多模型消费需协调。

Method: FORTE 是一个模型无关的生产框架，用于发现、验证和删除特征。首先，通过高效扰动实现每日评估特征重要性。然后，为处理特征交互，通过分组消融从所有低秩特征构建候选特征集，评估这些候选集的移除效果。离线结果经过验证与在线 A/B 测试高度相关，支持将删除推进到在线测试。最后，谱系追踪识别所有下游消费模型，确保从训练集中完整删除特征以获取全部节省。该框架在两个大型推荐系统上验证：搜索相关性模型和大型排序模型。在搜索相关性模型上，识别出 32、48、59 三个嵌套候选集，在线护栏拒绝最大集合，48 特征集被选中删除，去除了 43.3% 的特征。在大型排序模型上，识别出 8 个候选删除，重训和 A/B 测试确认无回归，最终减少 29.9% 可训练参数，提升 19.8-25.5% 服务吞吐，节省数十万美元年度训练成本。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "可训练参数减少", "value": "29.9%", "setting": "大型排序模型，重训和 A/B 测试确认无回归后"}, {"metric": "服务吞吐量提升", "value": "19.8-25.5%", "setting": "大型排序模型，特征删除后"}, {"metric": "年度训练成本节省", "value": "数十万美元", "setting": "大型排序模型，特征删除后"}, {"metric": "特征数量减少", "value": "43.3%", "setting": "搜索相关性模型，选取 48 个特征集删除"}]}

Conclusion: 高效的扰动实现支持每日特征评估；通过分组消融从低秩特征中构建候选特征集以处理交互；离线结果高度预测在线 A/B 测试，从而有信心推进删除；谱系追踪识别所有下游消费模型，实现完整移除训练集特征

Abstract: Production recommender systems have feature-lifecycle asymmetry: adding new features must produce gains on some metric, while feature removal must not regress any metric. In practice, feature deprecation is hard to prioritize as a feature can fail to be removed if it interacts with other features, its importance changes daily, it cannot be evaluated before production models change or data drift occurs, it is in contention for A/B test space with other launches, it causes regressions in an online A/B test, or it is consumed by multiple models that need to coordinate its deprecation. We present FORTE, a model-agnostic production framework for discovering, validating, and removing features. Its efficient perturbation implementation allows daily feature evaluation. To account for interactions, it evaluates candidate feature sets constructed from all the low-rank features through grouped ablation and its offline results are sufficiently predictive of online A/B tests to allow deprecations to proceed to online testing with confidence. Lineage tracing identifies all downstream consumer models to realize the full savings of removing a feature from the training set. We validate FORTE over 2 large-scale recommender systems and find that it identifies deprecable feature sets which do not cause online regressions, while the impact on efficiency depends on model characteristics. On a search-relevance model, FORTE resulted in 3 nested candidate sets of 32, 48, and 59 features. Online guardrails rejected the largest treatment. The 48-feature set was selected for deprecation, removing 43.3% of the model's features. On a large ranking model, FORTE identified 8 candidates for removal. Retraining and A/B testing confirmed no regression. Its launch reduced the trainable parameters by 29.9%, improved serving throughput by 19.8-25.5%, and reduced annual training costs by hundreds of thousands of dollars.

</details>


### [73] [DualQuant: Dual-scale Calibration-free Weight-and-Activation Quantization for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13767)
*Mahsa Salmani,Sayeh Sharify,Hesham Mostafa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DualQuant提出一种免校准的双尺度W4A4后训练量化框架，通过最小化权重重建MSE直接得到激活缩放因子，支持MX/NV块格式，在Llama和Qwen上精度匹配或超过SmoothQuant，并在RTX 4090上实现最高2.61倍内存降低和2.46倍吞吐加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有原生4比特支持的加速器上实现W4A4低比特推理时，权重与激活量化均需低比特，但激活量化通常依赖校准数据且难度大，现有免校准方法精度不足。

Method: DualQuant是一个后训练量化框架，输入为预训练LLM权重，目标是将权重和激活均量化为4比特并支持MX/NV等块格式。流程：1）对权重进行逐通道或块级量化，同时引入可优化的缩放因子；2）以最小化权重重建MSE为目标，将问题转化为非可微优化，采用变量分裂分解为多个子问题；3）利用块坐标下降交替更新，每个子问题有闭式解，高效求得权重缩放因子；4）将得到的缩放因子直接用于激活的逐通道再缩放，无需校准数据即可实现激活量化；5）最终使用统一的量化格式（如MXFP4、NVFP4）打包权重和激活，生成真实W4A4推理图；6）在RTX 4090上执行推理，获得内存和吞吐收益。整个方法无需反向传播，仅需少量迭代即可求解。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "精度", "value": "匹配或超过SmoothQuant（基于校准）在大多数模型和格式上的精度", "setting": "Llama和Qwen模型，WikiText-2、C4、零样本下游任务"}, {"metric": "精度", "value": "大幅优于免校准基线，MX和NV块格式增益显著", "setting": "同上"}, {"metric": "内存降低", "value": "最高2.61倍", "setting": "Llama-3.1-8B，RTX 4090，W4A4 vs BF16"}, {"metric": "吞吐加速", "value": "最高2.46倍", "setting": "Llama-3.1-8B，RTX 4090，W4A4 vs BF16"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出双尺度、免校准的后训练权重量化与激活量化联合框架DualQuant；发现最小化权重重建MSE得到的缩放因子可有效用作逐通道激活再缩放，从而避免激活校准；将非可微权重重建问题建模为变量分裂与块坐标下降，每个子问题有闭式解；统一支持MX系列与NVFP4等块浮点格式，首次正式支持MX和NV块格式的双缩放方法；在RTX 4090上实现真实打包W4A4推理路径，验证实际内存与吞吐收益

Abstract: Low-bit inference on accelerators with native 4-bit support requires quantizing both weights and activations to realize the full throughput gains these formats provide. While post-training weight quantization has been extensively studied, activation quantization remains considerably more challenging and typically requires calibration data. In this work, we introduce DUALQUANT, a dual-scale, calibration-free post-training quantization framework that directly minimizes the weight-reconstruction Mean Squared Error (MSE) and, as an immediate by-product, yields per-channel activation rescalings prior to activation quantization. Our key observation is that the scaling factors obtained by minimizing weight-reconstruction error provide effective activation rescalings, enabling competitive activation quantization without calibration data. We formulate DUALQUANT as a non-differentiable weight-reconstruction problem and develop an efficient solver based on variable splitting and block coordinate descent, where each subproblem admits a closed-form update. DUALQUANT provides a unified algorithm supporting a wide range of formats, including the full MX family and NVFP4. To our knowledge, DUALQUANT is the first dual-scaling method to formally support MX and NV block formats. Across Llama and Qwen models evaluated on WikiText-2, C4, and zero-shot downstream tasks, DUALQUANT matches or exceeds the accuracy of calibration-based SmoothQuant across most models and formats, and substantially outperforms calibration-free baselines, with large gains for MX and NV block formats. We validate these gains with a real packed W4A4 inference path for Llama-3.1-8B on an RTX 4090. The resulting implementation closely matches the accuracy of fake quantization while delivering up to 2.61x memory reduction and up to 2.46x throughput speedup over BF16.

</details>


### [74] [Matched-Context Group Policy Optimization for LLM Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13755)
*Chenxu Zhang,Yaorui Shi,Wenlong Zhang,Jianchao Tan,Zihan Wang,Yiming Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MCGPO 提出一种面向长程 LLM 智能体的组策略优化方法，通过匹配上下文构建加权动作偏好，并配合共享 GraphGPO 任务学习的辅助成对目标，在 ALFWorld 与 WebShop 上以 Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B 和 Qwen3-4B/8B 多个设置取得相对 GraphGPO 的成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Assigning credit to intermediate decisions of long-horizon LLM agents from sparse terminal feedback, so that useful action comparisons are made under matched contexts with appropriate evidence and evidence weighting.

Method: MCGPO operates on already collected long-horizon agent trajectories. Inputs are sibling rollouts within a finite group, each consisting of pre-action state summaries, observed rubric progress, task-rule annotations, and terminal outcomes. The method first defines comparison contexts from pre-action state summaries and rubric progress. Two-action support then selects among ordered exact and coarse matching rules to decide which action pairs are comparable. Fixed folds separate candidate decisions from the terminal outcomes used to orient their preferences, preventing terminal feedback from trivially determining the preference label. Support-dependent evidence weights scale an auxiliary pairwise objective, which is optimized jointly with shared GraphGPO task learning. The procedure is critic-free: it reuses rollout data and each response's original history without requiring a separate value network. Training optimizes the GraphGPO task objective plus the weighted pairwise preference objective. Evaluation spans six environment-backbone settings on ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B and 7B, and WebShop with Qwen3-4B and 8B, using three training seeds, with factorial controls for opposite-fold evidence, evidence weighting, and representation matching.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "mean success improvement", "value": "0.47-1.46 percentage points", "setting": "ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B and 7B, six environment-backbone settings, three training seeds, versus matched GraphGPO"}, {"metric": "mean success improvement", "value": "3.96 and 5.16 points", "setting": "WebShop with Qwen3-4B and 8B, versus matched GraphGPO"}, {"metric": "integrated success improvement", "value": "improves in 3 of 4 settings", "setting": "Qwen2.5 settings"}, {"metric": "highest mean in all four representation-control settings", "value": "joint state-rubric matching", "setting": "representation-control settings"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Matched-context group policy optimization that constructs weighted action preferences from already collected trajectories rather than new rollouts；Comparison contexts defined with pre-action state summaries and observed rubric progress annotated by task rules；Two-action support selecting among ordered exact and coarse matching rules；Fixed folds separating candidate decisions from terminal outcomes used to orient preferences；Support-dependent evidence weights scaling an auxiliary pairwise objective alongside shared GraphGPO task learning；Critic-free training that reuses rollout data and each response's original history

Abstract: Training long-horizon language model agents requires assigning credit to intermediate decisions from sparse terminal feedback. Within a finite group of sibling rollouts, useful action comparisons depend on their context, evidence for both alternatives, and how that evidence enters learning. We propose Matched-Context Group Policy Optimization (MCGPO), a procedure for constructing weighted action preferences from already collected trajectories. MCGPO defines comparison contexts with pre-action state summaries and observed rubric progress, annotated by task rules. Two-action support selects among ordered exact and coarse matching rules. Fixed folds separate candidate decisions from the terminal outcomes used to orient their preferences, and support-dependent evidence weights scale an auxiliary pairwise objective alongside shared GraphGPO task learning. MCGPO reuses rollout data and each response's original history, preserving critic-free training. Across six environment-backbone settings with three training seeds each, MCGPO improves mean success over matched GraphGPO: by 0.47-1.46 percentage points on ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B and 7B, and by 3.96 and 5.16 points on WebShop with Qwen3-4B and 8B. The ALFWorld gains build on baseline success above 92%, where remaining headroom is limited. Integrated success improves in three of four Qwen2.5 settings. Three-seed factorial controls support opposite-fold evidence and evidence weighting; joint state-rubric matching achieves the highest mean in all four representation-control settings.

</details>


### [75] [ReplaySSM: Cache SSM Inputs, Not State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13778)
*Ze-Wei Liou,Harris Nover,Amey Naik,Andrii Skliar,Igor Shovkun,Ido Shahaf,Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReplaySSM 通过把 SSM 状态表示为“检查点 + 近期输入缓冲”，将输出计算与检查点更新分离：大部分解码步骤免写状态，状态内存流量约减半；投机解码回滚变成指针操作，并支持仅输出的验证；同时用低精度检查点算输出、高精度检查点做递归更新，避免量化误差进入后续状态。在 vLLM 中对 Mamba-2 和 Gated DeltaNet 实现，批量 256 下标准解码较调优 vLLM 基线加速 1.20–1.47 倍，在两个 120B 级模型上吞吐约为 vLLM 投机解码的 1.8 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSM 在每一步解码时都需要读写固定大小递归状态，导致状态 I/O 大、算术强度低；投机解码缺少低成本回滚；低精度状态量化误差会进入后续递归更新，影响输出质量。

Method: ReplaySSM 面向混合 SSM 模型（如 Mamba-2、Gated DeltaNet）的解码阶段，将传统每步读写递归状态的方式改为：维护一个周期性更新的检查点，以及一个保存最近 SSM 输入的缓冲区，共同表示当前状态。输出计算不再依赖每步写回的状态，而是由检查点加近期输入缓冲重放得到，因此大多数解码步骤无需写状态，减少主导性的状态内存流量。检查点按周期更新，把状态写操作摊销到少数步骤。对于投机解码，接受/拒绝候选 token 时只需移动缓冲区指针即可回滚，避免恢复或物化中间状态；验证阶段也只需要输出，不需要中间状态。精度方面，输出计算使用低精度检查点和服务于多步输出的路径，而递归更新使用高精度检查点，二者分离使得低精度舍入误差不会进入后续递归更新。系统在 vLLM 中实现，支持 Mamba-2 和 Gated DeltaNet，并在批量 256 下展示标准解码和投机解码的吞吐收益。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将递归展开，用检查点加近期 SSM 输入缓冲区表示状态，从而把输出计算与检查点更新分离。；周期性更新检查点，使大多数解码步骤避免状态写入，减少约一半的状态内存流量。；利用近期输入缓冲区把投机解码回滚转化为缓冲区指针操作，并支持无需物化中间状态的输出验证。；使用低精度检查点服务大多数步骤的输出计算，高精度检查点服务递归更新，使低精度舍入误差不会进入后续更新，降低输出 KL 散度和下游精度损失。

Abstract: State-space models (SSMs) maintain a fixed-size recurrent state, but reading and writing it at every decoding step can bottleneck hybrid models. This eager state management causes large state I/O with low arithmetic intensity, leaves speculative decoding no cheap rollback, and feeds state quantization error into subsequent recurrent updates. We introduce ReplaySSM, which unrolls the recurrence to represent the state as a checkpoint and a buffer of recent SSM inputs. This separates output computation from checkpoint updates. By updating the checkpoint periodically, ReplaySSM avoids state writes on most steps and roughly halves the dominant state memory traffic. Keeping recent inputs also makes speculative rollback a buffer-pointer operation and enables output-only verification without materializing intermediate states. The same separation allows a low-precision checkpoint to serve output computation on most steps while a higher-precision checkpoint serves recurrent updates, so rounding error in the low-precision copy never enters later updates. We implement ReplaySSM in vLLM for Mamba-2 and Gated DeltaNet. At serving batch size 256, it accelerates standard decoding by $1.20$--$1.47\times$ over a tuned vLLM baseline across four hybrid models, and delivers about $1.8\times$ the throughput of vLLM's speculative decoding on the two 120B-class models. Separate state precisions also reduce output KL divergence and downstream accuracy losses compared with a shared low-precision checkpoint.

</details>


### [76] [Orthros: Phase-Aware Heterogeneous Attention for Efficient Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13735)
*Yuhong Chou,Zehao Liu,Yuqi Pan,Qian Liu,Xianwei Chen,Zhongzhu Zhou,Jibin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Orthros 提出阶段感知异构注意力，在预填充阶段使用线性注意力、解码阶段使用全注意力，实现线性复杂度预填充与全注意力解码的统一。实验显示相对 Transformer 获得 21.9 倍预填充加速，并在 128K 大海捞针任务上接近完美，且支持数据高效转换，性能可超越原始 Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效注意力机制（如线性注意力、滑动窗口注意力）的上下文召回能力下降，通常需要交错插入全注意力层，导致长上下文处理中二次复杂度瓶颈仍未解决。

Method: Orthros 注意力是一个统一模块，根据序列处理阶段动态切换计算模式。在预填充阶段，对输入序列采用线性复杂度的注意力计算模式，降低长上下文预填充的计算开销；在解码阶段，切换为全注意力模式，保持对完整上下文的精确召回。这种阶段感知解耦设计使得模块在重预填充、轻解码的常见长上下文场景下比 vanilla Transformer 具有更低的渐近复杂度。论文还提供了从标准 Transformer 到 Orthros 的数据高效转换方法，通过转换可使性能甚至超越原始 Transformer。具体模块组成、训练目标、优化细节未在摘要中充分披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "预填充加速比", "value": "21.9x", "setting": "相对 Transformer 的预填充阶段加速，端到端推理有增益"}, {"metric": "128K 大海捞针（needle-in-a-haystack）", "value": "近完美结果", "setting": "128K 长上下文检索任务"}, {"metric": "真实上下文召回任务性能", "value": "Transformer 级别", "setting": "真实世界 in-context recall 任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出阶段感知解耦的 Orthros 注意力，根据序列处理阶段动态切换计算模式，实现线性复杂度预填充与全注意力解码的统一模块。；设计数据高效的 Transformer 到 Orthros 转换方法，且转换后性能可超越原始 Transformer。；理论推导了在重预填充、轻解码场景下的更低渐近复杂度，并给出复杂度分析。

Abstract: Efficient attention mechanisms, such as linear and sliding window attention, aim to reduce the computational complexity of Transformers from quadratic to linear. However, their degraded in-context recall often necessitates interleaving full attention layers, leaving the quadratic bottleneck unresolved in long-context processing. To address this, we introduce Orthros attention, which shifts the efficiency paradigm from structural allocation to phase-aware decoupling. As a unified module, it dynamically switches its computation pattern across different phases of sequence processing, enabling linear complexity prefilling and full attention decoding. Our design grants Orthros attention a lower asymptotic complexity compared to vanilla Transformers in prevalent long-context scenarios characterized by heavy prefilling and light decoding. Beyond theoretical formulation and complexity analysis, extensive empirical evaluations demonstrate Orthros's superiority across multiple dimensions. First, we validate its fundamental language modeling capabilities, demonstrating scalability across varying model sizes and strong competitiveness in comprehensive architectural comparisons. Second, Orthros exhibits exceptional conversion feasibility via a data-efficient Transformer-to-Orthros conversion, yielding performance that even surpasses the original Transformer. Finally, Orthros achieves a $21.9\times$ prefilling speedup over Transformers with end-to-end inference gains, while preserving long-context capabilities, as demonstrated by near-perfect 128K needle-in-a-haystack results and Transformer-level performance on real-world in-context recall tasks. Overall, these advantages in high performance, flexibility, and efficiency position Orthros as a promising candidate to succeed the Transformer in industrial applications.

</details>


### [77] [Kronecker Product Attention: Memory-Efficient KV Compression via Structured Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12657)
*Bahareh Nikpour,Hossein Rajabzadeh,Maryam Dialameh,Vahid Partovi Nia,Parsa Omidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Kronecker Product Attention (KPA)，通过将 KV 激活表示为紧凑矩阵因子的 Kronecker 乘积之和，实现了最高 10 倍的 KV cache 压缩。KPA 严格推广了 TPA，并引入结构化旋转嵌入以保留 RoPE 特性，无需 MLA 的解耦分支。在四个模型规模和九个基准上，KPA 匹配或超越了 MHA、MQA、GQA、MLA 和 TPA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归生成中 KV cache 的内存占用随序列长度、批量大小和模型深度线性增长，成为效率瓶颈。现有方法如 MQA、GQA、MLA、TPA 在降低 KV cache 开销时，往往牺牲表达能力、需要额外的位置编码机制或施加严格的低秩结构。

Method: KPA 将标准注意力中的键和值激活重新参数化为紧凑的、依赖于 token 的矩阵因子的 Kronecker 乘积之和。具体地，对于每个键或值向量，它被表示为若干对矩阵的 Kronecker 积的线性组合。这种表示严格推广了 TPA，其中秩一向量外积对应于非退化 Kronecker 拆分的一种特殊情况，而非退化的矩阵因子能够捕获更丰富的块结构模式。在注意力计算过程中，查询与这些结构化键进行交互，而值则同样以结构化形式聚合。为了解决位置编码问题，KPA 采用了一种结构化的旋转嵌入算子，该算子与 Kronecker 表示相匹配，仅对紧凑因子应用旋转，从而保留了 RoPE 的相对位置属性。这消除了 MLA 中为了保持位置信息而需要的解耦 RoPE 分支。整个架构可以端到端训练，其中 Kronecker 头拆分是一个超参数，允许在训练前调整压缩与精度的权衡。在推理时，由于键和值以紧凑的矩阵因子形式存储，KV cache 的内存占用显著减少，而注意力计算则通过重组这些因子来执行，实现了内存效率与表达能力的平衡。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "KV cache 压缩率", "value": "最高 10 倍", "setting": "与基线模型相比，在相同训练条件下"}, {"metric": "下游基准性能", "value": "匹配或超过 TPA、MHA、MQA、GQA、MLA", "setting": "四个模型规模、九个下游基准，相同训练条件"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Kronecker Product Attention (KPA)，将 key 和 value 激活表示为紧凑的、依赖于 token 的矩阵因子的 Kronecker 乘积之和，严格推广了 TPA 的秩一向量外积分解。；设计了一种与 Kronecker 表示相匹配的结构化旋转嵌入算子，在仅旋转紧凑因子的同时保持 RoPE 的相对位置特性，无需 MLA 中的解耦 RoPE 分支。；Kronecker 头拆分作为可调超参数，允许在实际部署前权衡压缩率与精度，而无需改变架构。

Abstract: Efficient autoregressive generation is increasingly limited by the memory footprint of the key-value (KV) cache, which grows linearly with sequence length, batch size, and model depth. Existing approaches such as Multi-Query Attention (MQA), Grouped-Query Attention (GQA), Multi-Head Latent Attention (MLA), and Tensor Product Attention (TPA) reduce this cost through different trade-offs, but often sacrifice expressivity, require additional positional-encoding machinery, or impose restrictive low-rank structure. We propose Kronecker Product Attention (KPA), which represents key and value activations as sums of Kronecker products of compact, token-dependent matrix factors. KPA strictly generalizes TPA: rank-one vector-outer-product factorization arises as a degenerate Kronecker split, while non-degenerate matrix factors can represent richer block-structured patterns. We further show that a structured rotary embedding operator matched to the Kronecker representation preserves the relative-position property of RoPE while rotating only the compact factors, removing the need for a decoupled RoPE branch as in MLA. Empirically, KPA matches or exceeds TPA, MHA, MQA, GQA, and MLA across four model scales and nine downstream benchmarks under identical training conditions, while achieving up to 10x KV cache compression. The Kronecker head split is a tuneable hyperparameter that allows practitioners to navigate the compression-accuracy trade-off at design time without architectural changes.

</details>


### [78] [Generalized Proximal Policy Optimization: Unifying LLM RL Algorithms from the Perspective of Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13015)
*Zhicheng Cai,Xinyuan Guo,Hanlin Wu,Weinan Dai,Yuyan Zhou,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GPPO从散度视角统一LLM强化学习算法，建立广义信赖域理论，提出新估计器拓扑，在数学和编码基准上超越GRPO，为LLM后训练提供原则性设计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPO的裁剪代理几何退化，无法自适应调节对策略偏移的抵抗，导致过早裁剪和探索退化；后续变体修改碎片化且启发式，缺乏统一理论。

Method: GPPO将LLM强化学习中的策略更新视为一个广义f散度信赖域优化问题。输入为语言模型策略、奖励信号和采样轨迹。核心模块包括：1）散度定义模块，用户选择不同的f散度（如KL、TV、χ²等）来塑造策略更新几何；2）估计器模块，采用新的估计器拓扑（如混合样本或双重估计）来估计所选散度，而非传统单样本蒙特卡洛；3）代理目标函数构造，将散度约束融入PPO式裁剪或直接惩罚项；4）优化器执行策略梯度更新。训练目标为在满足散度信赖域约束下最大化期望奖励，并具有单调性能提升保证。推理阶段与标准LLM解码一致。该框架统一了PPO、DAPO、DPPO、RIPO等算法，表明不同选择对应不同特例，指导算法设计。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "数学任务准确率", "value": "相比标准GRPO提升高达50%", "setting": "八个竞赛级数学与编码基准"}, {"metric": "编码任务准确率", "value": "相比标准GRPO提升高达14%", "setting": "八个竞赛级数学与编码基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 基于f散度的广义信赖域理论，提供单调性能提升保证；提出新的估计器拓扑，比传统单样本蒙特卡洛估计更忠实地估计散度；建立{散度×估计器}二维设计空间，将PPO、DAPO、DPPO、RIPO统一为特例

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become the foundational paradigm for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of LLMs. Yet the dominant approach, Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), relies on a geometrically degenerate clipping proxy that cannot adapt its resistance to policy shifts, leading to premature clipping and degraded exploration. Subsequent variants alleviate these issues through fragmented and heuristic modifications. We propose Generalized Proximal Policy Optimization (GPPO), a principled and unified framework for understanding and designing LLM RL algorithms through the lens of divergence. We establish a generalized $f$-divergence trust-region theory with a monotonic performance improvement guarantee, and show that different $f$-divergences induce distinct geometries and policy update dynamics. We further introduce novel estimator topologies that provide more faithful divergence estimates than the conventional single-sample Monte Carlo estimation. Under this framework, PPO and recent variants such as DAPO, DPPO, and RIPO emerge as special cases, establishing a principled two-dimensional design space of {Divergence $\times$ Estimator}. Extensive experiments on eight competition-level math and coding benchmarks show that both dimensions are critical. The best-performing GPPO instantiation surpasses standard GRPO by up to 50% and 14% on math and coding, delivering SOTA performance.

</details>


### [79] [CAST: Credit Alignment for Cross-Tokenizer On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13257)
*Jingru Yang,Jinhang Wu,Minjing Shi,Shengfeng He,Xuyan Li,Man Tan,Biye Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAST 提出 shift-or-stay 框架，解决跨分词器在线策略蒸馏中的 credit 分配与未对齐 token 漂移问题：对齐 chunk 的 advantage 按预测不确定性和教师信号平滑度重分配到学生 token，未对齐位置则用 KL 惩罚锚定初始策略。在六个数学、科学、代码基准上，跨分词器蒸馏平均比最强基线提升 2.45/1.32 点，共享分词器提升 2.10 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨分词器在线策略蒸馏中，解码文本对齐只能提供 chunk 级信号，无法将 credit 合理分配到学生 token，且无教师对应的 token 缺乏约束。

Method: CAST 是一种面向跨分词器在线策略蒸馏的 credit 分配框架。输入为教师与学生模型在序列生成过程中产生的 token 序列；由于分词器不同，首先通过解码文本匹配构造 chunk 级对齐，得到对齐 chunk 与未对齐位置。对于对齐 chunk，教师信号仅给出 chunk 级 advantage，CAST 进一步将其重新分配到学生 token：利用学生模型在对应 token 上的预测不确定性以及局部教师信号的平滑度计算权重，强调教师反馈局部一致的 token，并保持 chunk 总 advantage 不变。论文证明该权重精确求解一个 KL 正则化的 credit 分配问题。对于未对齐位置，CAST 采用选择性锚定：通过 KL 惩罚将学生策略约束到其初始策略，限制无教师监督区域的漂移。由于 credit 分配与 chunk 构造分离，方法也可用于标点定义或固定长度 chunk，并支持共享分词器。训练目标为在原始蒸馏损失基础上加入所设计的 token 级加权 credit 及锚定 KL 项；推理路径与标准蒸馏模型一致，不引入额外推理模块。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均得分提升", "value": "2.45 点（跨分词器 Qwen3-8B 到 Mistral-7B）", "setting": "六个数学、科学、代码基准，对比最强评估基线"}, {"metric": "平均得分提升", "value": "1.32 点（跨分词器 Qwen3-8B 到 Llama-3.1-8B）", "setting": "六个数学、科学、代码基准，对比最强评估基线"}, {"metric": "平均得分提升", "value": "2.10 点（共享分词器 Qwen3-8B 到 Qwen3-1.7B）", "setting": "六个数学、科学、代码基准，对比最强评估基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 shift-or-stay 框架 CAST，同时处理对齐 chunk 的 credit 重分配与未对齐位置的锚定。；利用预测不确定性和局部教师信号平滑度，将对齐 chunk 的 advantage 重分配到 token，并证明权重精确求解 KL 正则化 credit 分配问题。；对无教师监督的位置，选择性将学生锚定到初始策略，用 KL 惩罚限制漂移。；credit 分配与 chunk 构造解耦，可扩展至标点定义或固定长度 chunk 及共享分词器场景。

Abstract: Cross-tokenizer on-policy distillation (OPD) requires aligning teacher and student sequences before their scores can be compared. Decoded-text alignment provides such correspondence at the chunk level, but leaves a distinct problem unresolved: credit alignment. A chunk-level signal specifies how much a span should change, but not how that signal should be distributed across student tokens, while tokens without teacher correspondence remain unconstrained. We propose CAST (Chunk-aware credit Assignment with Selective Token Anchoring), a shift-or-stay framework that addresses both issues. For aligned chunks, CAST redistributes each chunk's advantage across student tokens using predictive uncertainty and local teacher-signal smoothness, emphasizing learning opportunities supported by locally consistent teacher feedback while preserving the chunk's total advantage. The resulting weights exactly solve a KL-regularized credit-allocation problem. For unaligned positions, CAST selectively anchors the student to its initial policy through a KL penalty, limiting drift where teacher supervision is unavailable. Because credit assignment is separated from chunk construction, CAST also applies to punctuation-defined or fixed-length chunks under shared tokenization. Across six mathematics, science, and code benchmarks, CAST improves the average over the strongest evaluated baseline by $2.45$ and $1.32$ points for cross-tokenizer transfer from Qwen3-8B to Mistral-7B and Llama-3.1-8B, respectively, and by $2.10$ points for shared-tokenizer transfer to Qwen3-1.7B.

</details>


### [80] [Recurrent Harness for Agentic Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12668)
*Yuxin Xiao,Zhiyun Lu,Jianyu Wang,Yangtian Zhang,Yang Song,Qifan Wang,Bailin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ReHarness，一个用于智能体持续学习的循环 harness 框架，在严格在线流式协议下通过前向读取/写入与后向修订更新文本状态。在三个持续学习基准和三个 LLM 骨干上，该方法稳定优于原始上下文基线、记忆/反思方法及 prompt 优化方法，并改善持续学习中的稳定性和可塑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体在长交互流中面临分布漂移，需要在线持续适应；现有 agent harness 仅通过前向信息流演化状态，或依赖额外 replay/验证数据进行优化，无法在严格在线流式协议下有效利用反馈。

Method: ReHarness 面向长交互流中的语言模型智能体。其核心是一个文本 harness 状态，维护从历史实例中检索到的反思（reflections）。处理当前实例时，harness 先进行 forward read：从 harness 状态中检索相关反思，将其作为上下文提供给语言模型，生成当前实例的轨迹。然后进行 forward write：将当前实例产生的新经验/反思写入 harness 状态。与现有只保留前向信息流的方案不同，ReHarness 在每个实例结束后引入 backward revision：利用该实例已经生成的轨迹作为反馈，回头修订为生成该实例而检索到的反思，从而沿增强路径将反馈反向传播到文本 harness 状态中。该修订过程是严格的在线流式协议：每个实例只处理一次，不依赖额外的 replay buffer 或验证集。训练/优化目标隐式地为通过文本状态更新来提升后续实例的预测质量，整个过程不涉及梯度更新，而是通过语言模型对文本状态的读取、写入与修订完成。推理时，对每个到来的流式实例执行检索、读取、生成、写入与后向修订的循环。论文没有披露模型参数规模、训练数据量、硬件、延迟或吞吐等具体资源消耗。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 ReHarness 循环 harness 框架，在 forward read 和 write 之外加入 backward revision；将每个实例产生的轨迹作为反馈，回头修订该实例检索到的反思（reflections），使反馈沿文本 harness 状态向后传播；在严格在线流式协议下实现无需 replay 或验证数据的持续学习

Abstract: Language model agents increasingly operate over long interaction streams, requiring online adaptation to distribution shifts through continual learning.
