However, existing agent harnesses typically either evolve their states only through forward information flow or rely on additional replay or validation data for harness optimization. To address this, we propose ReHarness, a recurrent harness framework for agentic continual learning that complements forward read and write with backward revision under a strictly online streaming protocol. We ask what information could have been captured differently in the harness state to better inform the current instance in the stream. Specifically, after each instance, we leverage the resulting trajectory as feedback to revise the reflections retrieved for that instance, thereby propagating feedback backward through the textual harness state. Across three continual learning benchmarks and three language model backbones, ReHarness consistently outperforms strong raw-context baselines, memory- and reflection-based methods, and prompt optimization approaches. Further analysis shows that ReHarness produces source-grounded and meaningful revisions, corrects overgeneralized or outdated experience under distribution shifts, and improves both stability and plasticity during continual learning.

</details>


### [81] [An Input-Dependent Readout Phase for Resonant Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12496)
*Shivesh Rane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在ResonatorLM基础上，将每个头的固定读出相位改为当前token的函数，仅增加0.2%参数并保持精确卷积。在6M字符级模型上，训练长度256、测试长度1024时，困惑度从12.81降至3.76，并分析了固定相位在长记忆头上的失败机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定状态序列模型在保持O(T log T)卷积训练路径时，读出方式对每个输入相同，缺乏输入依赖性；ResonatorLM用每个头固定的学习相位读取阻尼复振荡器库，导致在超出训练长度时泛化失败。

Method: ResonatorLM使用一组阻尼复振荡器组成的固定状态库，每个头通过一个固定的学习相位读取。本文将该读出相位改为当前token的函数，即readout phase = f(token)，使得读出具有输入依赖性，而状态转移仍保持固定。因此模型仍可走O(T log T)的FFT卷积训练路径，且只有读出阶段的计算被修改，参数增加约0.2%。训练目标为标准自回归语言建模（字符级）。推理时同样按token动态计算读出相位，与训练一致。该设计旨在让模型在超出训练长度时仍能正确读取记忆，避免固定相位导致的长度外推失败。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "困惑度 perplexity", "value": "12.81 -> 3.76", "setting": "6M参数字符级模型，WikiText-2，训练长度256 tokens，测试长度1024 tokens"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将ResonatorLM的读出相位改为当前token的函数，仅增加读出阶段的输入依赖性，保持卷积精确且仅增加0.2%参数。；分析未训练振荡器库，发现相位编码经过时间，但普通token在2-8步内低于背景，且无论阻尼如何，线性重建不超过约16个token。；在6M参数字符级模型上，训练长度256、测试长度1024时，困惑度从12.81降至3.76，并追踪失败原因至长记忆头积累了超出训练暴露的历史。

Abstract: Sequence models with a fixed-size state avoid the quadratic cost of attention, but face a design choice. An input-dependent state transition gives content-dependent memory at the cost of FFT-based training, while a fixed transition keeps the O(T log T) convolutional path but reads memory the same way for every input. ResonatorLM, a recent model of the second kind, reads a bank of damped complex oscillators through a fixed learned phase per head. We make that readout phase a function of the current token, which adds input dependence to the read only, keeps the convolution exact, and costs 0.2 percent more parameters. An analysis of the untrained bank shows that phase does encode elapsed time, but an ordinary token falls below the background within 2-8 steps and no choice of damping extends linear reconstruction beyond about 16 tokens. In 6M-parameter character-level models on WikiText-2, the input-dependent phase matches the original at the training length and removes its failure beyond it: trained on 256 tokens and tested on 1024, perplexity falls from 12.81 to 3.76. We trace the failure to long-memory heads that accumulate more history than training exposed them to. The learned phase tracks the current character rather than distance and does not improve retrieval.

</details>


### [82] [Kairos: Toward a Byte-Level Foundation Model for Every Modality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12495)
*Fabien Furfaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Kairos提出字节级多模态基础模型架构，结合门控线性注意力、滑动窗口注意力、稀疏MoE与多尺度编解码器，并通过小规模收敛实验初步验证实现可优化，但尚未评估泛化与多模态能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一个统一的字节级多模态基础模型架构是否能够正常优化，而非评估其下游效用或泛化能力。

Method: 输入为文本、图像、音频、视频、激光雷达和控制流的原始字节流，经多尺度字节级编解码器转换为共享字节空间表示。核心处理模块依次为：1) LiZAttention2：对共享投影后的表示同时应用双向门控DeltaNet线性注意力和滑动窗口注意力，以捕捉长程与局部依赖；2) 块级Attention-Residual聚合：对注意力输出进行残差式块级聚合，稳定训练；3) 稀疏MoE前馈层：对前馈网络进行稀疏专家激活，提升容量效率。此外引入跨会话循环状态记忆门，在推理跨会话时传递循环状态。训练采用均匀噪声去噪目标，即在统一字节空间上对加噪输入进行去噪重建。推理时按自回归或去噪方式生成字节序列。该报告仅进行收敛性验证实验，未评估泛化或多模态训练效果。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "最小损失", "value": "约1e-3", "setting": "单句440字节，1-15M参数，仅文本，3000步，LiZAttention2与单独窗口注意力"}, {"metric": "平均最小损失", "value": "0.018", "setting": "600步，patch编解码器，三个种子"}, {"metric": "平均最小损失", "value": "0.354", "setting": "600步，深度卷积编解码器，三个种子"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: LiZAttention2：结合双向门控DeltaNet线性注意力与滑动窗口注意力，共享投影。；块级Attention-Residual聚合。；稀疏MoE前馈层。；多尺度字节级编解码器，统一文本、图像、音频、视频、激光雷达和控制流。；跨会话循环状态记忆门。；均匀噪声去噪训练目标。

Abstract: Small language models trained on a single local machine leave little room for architectural trial and error. Kairos combines bidirectional gated-DeltaNet linear attention and sliding-window attention over shared projections (LiZAttention2), block-wise Attention-Residual aggregation, a sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) feed-forward layer and a multi-scale byte-level codec, trained with a uniform-noise denoising objective over one byte space shared by text, image, audio, video, lidar and control streams; this report asks whether the combination optimizes at all before asking whether it is useful. We give code-derived algorithms for each component, including a cross-session recurrent-state memory gate, then run convergence sanity checks: overfittinga single 440-byte sentence at 1--15M parameters, text only, with three seeds for codec and attention choices and single-run sweeps for seven further factors. The two attention variants tested at 3\,000 steps (LiZAttention2 and window attention alone) reach a minimum loss near $10^{-3}$. After 600 steps, the mean minimum loss ranges from 0.018 (patch codec) to 0.354 (depthwise convolutional codec) with an identical ranking on three seeds. An apparent advantage of shared over separate attention projections closes with more steps or more diverse data and reverses when the projections are frozen: it is a convergence-speed effect, not a capacity effect. These checks suggest that the implementation optimizes; they say nothing about generalization, multimodal training or comparison with existing models, which a planned pretraining run is meant to address. The Discussion sizes a 200M-total/25M-active target analytically; none of its throughput figures is measured. Code is available at this https URL . Python package at this https URL .

</details>


### [83] [Meta-Reflective Evolution Effectively Improves Production AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12794)
*Seung Won Wilson Yoo,Chang Zeng,Aditi Somayajula,Sahithi Ankireddy,Rohan Kosalge,Avishree Khare,John Mitchell,Aditya G. Parameswaran,Rajhans Samdani,Anupam Datta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoCoEvolve，一种基于meta-reflection的进化优化器，通过检索全进化历史并探测实时系统来优化生产AI系统。在DABStep、data-eng-bench等基准上取得最优结果，证明meta-reflection对生产AI系统优化的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM引导的进化优化方法仅基于前沿候选和单一失败信息进行变异，在生产AI系统中由于存在复杂隐藏交互而效果受限。

Method: CoCoEvolve以进化优化框架为基础，包含一个代理变异器（Cortex Code）。在每一代中，代理变异器首先从整个进化历史中选择性地检索相关证据（历史候选、失败、成功记录），然后对当前生产系统进行实时探测以收集系统行为信息。接着，它验证收集到的证据并据此提出变异方案，生成新的候选系统配置。该过程迭代进行，每次变异都基于更全面的信息而非仅前沿候选的单一失败。训练目标为最大化目标指标（如准确率、解决率）。推理时，代理在每次进化步骤中执行上述检索-探测-验证-变异循环，最终输出优化后的系统配置。该架构通过利用全历史信息和实时系统反馈，解决生产系统中隐藏交互导致优化停滞的问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "held-out hard accuracy", "value": "92.8%", "setting": "DABStep，对比Meta-Harness 78.4%和GEPA 30.4%"}, {"metric": "test-set solve rate", "value": "从59.1%提升至77.4%", "setting": "data-eng-bench，超过最佳基线73.6%"}, {"metric": "accuracy", "value": "比GEPA高11个百分点", "setting": "HotpotQA"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出meta-reflection机制：代理变异器（Cortex Code）选择性地从整个进化历史中检索证据并在提出变异前探测实时系统；构建CoCoEvolve进化优化器，集成历史检索、实时探测、验证与变异提议；在多个生产AI系统类别（数据代理、LLM数据管道、数据工程工作流）上验证有效性

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-guided evolutionary optimization has successfully improved prompts, programs, and even entire AI systems. Most optimization methods select a candidate from the search frontier, then mutate it in one LLM pass based only on its failures. This focus on the frontier can stall on production AI systems, where isolated failures reveal little about complex, hidden interactions. We introduce CoCoEvolve (Cortex Code Evolve), an evolutionary optimizer built around what we call meta-reflection, wherein an agentic mutator (Cortex Code) selectively retrieves evidence from the entire evolutionary history and probes the live system before verifying and proposing mutations. We apply CoCoEvolve to optimize three classes of production AI systems: data agents, LLM-powered data pipelines, and data engineering workflows. On DABStep, CoCoEvolve tops the public leaderboard, reaching 92.8% held-out hard accuracy compared to 78.4% for Meta-Harness and 30.4% for GEPA under matched conditions. Across six LLM data pipeline workloads, it outperforms every evaluated baseline. On data-eng-bench, CoCoEvolve lifts test-set solve rate from 59.1% to 77.4%, past the best baseline of 73.6%. It also has 11 percentage points higher accuracy than GEPA on HotpotQA while remaining competitive with specialized optimizers on established mathematical and systems benchmarks. Matched ablations confirm that each component of CoCoEvolve contributes to these gains, demonstrating the value of meta-reflection for optimizing production AI systems.

</details>


### [84] [REDaCT: Collapse-Free Unlearning in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12769)
*Joshua Maccoby,Wendy Hui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REDaCT提出一种推理时遗忘学习框架，通过二分类泄漏检测器定位推理轨迹中的泄漏句，并注入离线学习的纠正嵌入来引导后续推理，从而在完美遗忘答案、近乎完美遗忘思维链的同时避免推理坍塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型推理模型中执行遗忘学习时，同时移除推理链和最终答案中的遗忘集信息，并避免推理坍塌（无法生成连贯思维链或无法正常终止推理）。

Method: REDaCT是一个推理时遗忘框架，包含两个关键组件。第一，二分类泄漏检测器：输入为推理模型生成的思维链句子序列，逐句判断是否泄漏遗忘集信息。第二，嵌入优化模块：离线学习一个纠正嵌入向量。推理时，将纠正嵌入注入到首个被检测为泄漏的句子位置，以此引导后续推理生成不包含遗忘集信息的句子。系统整体目标是在移除遗忘集信息的同时保持推理链的连贯性和正常终止，避免推理坍塌。训练目标与优化细节、模型规模、数据规模等未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "推理保持（reasoning preservation）", "value": "substantially improving compared to state-of-the-art unlearning methods", "setting": "未披露具体数据集与基线数值，仅定性描述"}, {"metric": "答案遗忘（answer forgetting）", "value": "perfect", "setting": "未披露具体数据集与基线数值"}, {"metric": "思维链遗忘（CoT forgetting）", "value": "near-perfect", "setting": "未披露具体数据集与基线数值"}, {"metric": "推理坍塌（reasoning collapse）", "value": "completely avoids", "setting": "未披露具体数据集与基线数值"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出推理时遗忘框架REDaCT，包含二分类泄漏检测器与嵌入优化模块。；泄漏检测器识别推理轨迹中泄漏遗忘集信息的句子。；离线学习一个纠正嵌入，在推理时于首个检测到的泄漏处注入，引导后续推理走向不泄漏的句子。；在避免推理坍塌的同时保持完美答案遗忘和近乎完美的思维链遗忘。

Abstract: Unlearning in large reasoning models (LRMs) requires removing forget-set information from both the reasoning trace and the final answer. Existing approaches achieve this objective at the cost of reasoning collapse, where the model fails to produce a coherent chain-of-thought (CoT) and does not properly terminate its reasoning process. To address this challenge, we propose REDaCT (REgenerate via Detection and Corrective Tuning), an inference-time unlearning framework for LRMs. REDaCT consists of two key components: (1) a binary leak detector that identifies sentences in the reasoning trace that leak forget-set information, and (2) an embedding optimization module that learns a corrective embedding offline, such that when injected at the first detected leak during inference, it guides subsequent reasoning towards sentences that do not leak forget-set information. Experimental results show that REDaCT completely avoids reasoning collapse, substantially improving reasoning preservation compared to state-of-the-art unlearning methods while maintaining perfect answer forgetting and near-perfect CoT forgetting. Our code is available at: this https URL .

</details>

### [85] [PRIME: Pre-Retrieval Index Modeling Enhancement for Structured Document Retrieval-Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13296)
*Cheuk Hei Chung,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PRIME提出索引侧优化框架，通过LLM语义重构和确定性元数据合成+分层检索，在保持检索质量的同时大幅降低嵌入计算（45.8×）和索引向量数（22.4×），并在完整部署中提升Answer-F1 13.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息检索研究主要优化查询侧检索器和生成器，而索引侧质量（文档表示与结构化）作为提升检索精度的杠杆尚未被充分探索。

Method: PRIME框架包含两条互补轨道。第一轨道：LLM驱动的语义重构（Semantic Restructuring, SR），将长文档转换为结构化、信息密集的章节，作为索引表示。第二轨道：确定性元数据合成（Metadata Synthesis, MS）与分层检索（Hierarchical Retrieval, HR），MS从文档中提取语义锚点和标题结构，HR利用这些结构进行从粗到细的章节检索。两个轨道协同工作：MS提供结构化锚点以降低索引向量数量，SR则增强语义密度。训练目标未明确披露，但框架在推理时通过分层检索先定位相关章节再细粒度检索，从而减少嵌入计算。实验在QASPER、BRIGHT、NFCorpus上进行，并分析了强重排序器对索引侧优化的影响。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "嵌入计算削减", "value": "45.8×", "setting": "确定性元数据对比结构感知分块"}, {"metric": "索引向量数减少", "value": "22.4×", "setting": "确定性元数据对比结构感知分块"}, {"metric": "Answer-F1提升", "value": "13.7%", "setting": "完整部署下语义重构（SR）对比基线"}, {"metric": "检索质量", "value": "与结构感知分块相当", "setting": "确定性元数据"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出索引侧优化框架PRIME，包含LLM驱动的语义重构（SR）和确定性元数据合成（MS）与分层检索（HR）两条互补轨道。；语义重构将长文档转换为结构化、信息密集的章节，提升检索质量。；确定性元数据合成与分层检索提取语义锚点和标题结构，实现从粗到细的章节检索，大幅降低嵌入计算量和索引向量数。；刻画了索引侧优化在不同数据集上有效或有害的三种定性模式，并发现强重排序器下索引侧效果被中和。

Abstract: High-precision retrieval over external knowledge bases is essential for LLM agents to operate effectively. However, existing Information Retrieval (IR) research primarily optimizes query-side retrievers (e.g., ColBERT, BGE) and generators (e.g., Self-RAG, GraphRAG), while reducing corpus-side representation to chunking. This leaves index-side quality an underexplored lever. We propose Pre-Retrieval Index Modeling & Enhancement (PRIME), an index-side optimization framework built on two complementary tracks: an LLM-driven Semantic Restructuring (SR) track that transforms long documents into structured, information-dense sections, and a deterministic Metadata Synthesis (MS) and Hierarchical Retrieval (HR) track that extracts semantic anchors and heading structure for coarse-to-fine section retrieval. Our evaluations show that deterministic metadata matches structure-aware chunking's retrieval quality while cutting embedding computation by 45.8 $\times$ and index vector count by 22.4 $\times$. On top of these gains, Semantic Restructuring (SR) further improves Answer-F1 by 13.7% in full deployment, with characterized quality-fidelity-cost trade-offs. Across QASPER, BRIGHT, and NFCorpus, we further characterize three qualitative regimes that determine whether index-side optimization helps or degrades retrieval, while showing that these index-side effects are neutralized under a strong reranker.

</details>


### [86] [Beyond the Final Embedding: Retrieving Memories from Token-Level Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13277)
*Han Luo,Zhan Zhuang,Xu He,Jujia Zhao,Yu Li,Suqin Yuan,Dong Li,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的token级稀疏匹配检索方法，将局部token证据与全局向量相似度结合，在八个检索基准和长期记忆QA上提升记忆检索与问答表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一向量表示难以捕捉记忆内容中仅局部片段与查询相关的情况，导致记忆检索召回不足。

Method: 方法建立在已有embedding模型之上。对查询和记忆内容，先由同一编码器计算上下文token级表示与整段记忆的单一向量表示。检索时，每个查询token在候选记忆的token表示中检索一小部分匹配token；将每个记忆内部的所有token匹配分聚合为一个稀疏匹配分，再与整记忆相似度结合，形成最终排序分数用于召回。可选的低秩query adapter在保持编码器与已存储记忆表示固定的前提下，用监督信号训练，使查询表示更适配token匹配目标；无adapter时则无需训练。检索结果进一步用于长期记忆问答，提升下游QA准确率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均Recall@10", "value": "13.3 percentage points", "setting": "Qwen3-Embedding-4B（结合query adaptation，八个基准平均，无额外训练+适配）"}, {"metric": "平均NDCG@10", "value": "14.3 percentage points", "setting": "Qwen3-Embedding-4B（结合query adaptation，八个基准平均）"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 利用同一embedding模型已计算的上下文token表示做稀疏token匹配，无需额外训练；逐query token检索跨候选记忆的少量匹配token，并按记忆聚合匹配分；将稀疏token匹配分与整记忆相似度结合排序；可选低秩query adapter，仅训练adapter并冻结编码器与存储表示

Abstract: Memory-augmented assistants retrieve evidence from stored conversations and documents to answer queries. Most embedding-based retrievers compare single-vector representations of queries and memories, even when relevance depends on a small part of the content. Such local evidence can be matched to queries using the contextual token representations already computed by the same embedding models. Our method uses sparse token matching to improve retrieval without additional training. Each query token retrieves a small set of matching tokens across candidate memories. The matching scores are aggregated for each memory and combined with whole-memory similarity to rank memories. An optional low-rank query adapter can be trained with supervision while keeping the encoder and stored memory representations fixed. Our method improves retrieval across eight benchmarks and multiple model families without additional training. Query adaptation further increases the gains to 13.3 and 14.3 percentage points in average Recall@10 and NDCG@10 on Qwen3-Embedding-4B. These retrieval gains translate into higher question-answering accuracy on long-term memory benchmarks.

</details>


### [87] [Tracing and Taming Hallucinated Terms via Subsequence-Association Filtering and Breadth-Aware Selection for LLM Query Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13810)
*Amit Kumar Jaiswal,Sagar Uprety,Prayag Tiwari*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 查询扩展中的幻觉术语问题，提出免训练、检索器无关的 SAFE-QE 方法，通过子序列关联过滤虚假扩展词，并以广度感知覆盖最大化选择保留词集；在 TREC/BEIR 上 nDCG@10 与生成式扩展相当，但每查询不受支持术语从 Query2doc 的 27.8、HyDE 的 57.5 降至 4.3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 作为查询扩展词来源时会产生幻觉，注入不存在的实体和无关术语，增加排序噪声并可能损害强检索器。现有方法将幻觉术语视为无差别的噪声，缺乏机制性解释和保留词数的原则性标准。

Method: SAFE-QE 是一个无需训练、与检索器无关的方法。输入为查询和 LLM 生成的候选扩展词集合，以及伪相关反馈文档集（pseudo-relevant set）。核心模块包括：（1）子序列关联过滤：对每个候选扩展词计算其在语料条件下的跨上下文可复现性，即在随机化查询上下文下重新出现的程度；若某词在随机上下文下反复出现但在伪相关集中缺乏支持，则判定为优势幻觉关联的产物并降权。（2）广度感知选择：将术语选择转化为在伪相关集上的约束覆盖最大化问题，在保持近似广覆盖（breadth）的同时满足一致性（consistency，即无幻觉）与重采样稳定性（stability）约束。（3）最终输出经过滤和选择的扩展词集合，可直接与任意检索器（如 BM25、学习稀疏检索器）组合用于检索。训练目标不适用，因为方法为免训练；推理流程为对每个查询执行过滤与选择，无需额外训练。评估使用 TREC 与 BEIR 集合，并以 Hallucinated Expansion Term Rate 衡量查询侧忠实度。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "幻觉扩展词率（每查询注入的不受支持术语数）", "value": "4.3", "setting": "TREC/BEIR 基准，SAFE-QE"}, {"metric": "幻觉扩展词率（每查询注入的不受支持术语数）", "value": "27.8", "setting": "TREC/BEIR 基准，Query2doc"}, {"metric": "幻觉扩展词率（每查询注入的不受支持术语数）", "value": "57.5", "setting": "TREC/BEIR 基准，HyDE"}, {"metric": "nDCG@10", "value": "统计上与生成式及语料引导扩展无显著差异", "setting": "TREC/BEIR 基准"}, {"metric": "强学习稀疏检索器上的表现", "value": "所有扩展方法均出现回退", "setting": "强学习稀疏检索器"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 基于子序列关联（subsequence-association）的幻觉解释，对每个候选扩展词按“语料条件跨上下文可复现性”打分，将随机查询上下文下仍出现但伪相关集不支持的词标记为幻觉产物并降权。；将术语选择建模为在伪相关集上的约束覆盖最大化问题，兼顾广度（覆盖）与一致性（无幻觉），保持近似广覆盖且重采样下稳定。；提出 Hallucinated Expansion Term Rate（幻觉扩展词率），一种查询侧忠实度指标，统计无相关文档支持的注入扩展词数量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are now a standard source of query expansion terms but they hallucinate. They inject non existent entities and off-topic terms that add ranking noise and can hurt strong retrievers. Existing remedies such as corpus grounding, mutual verification and preference alignment treat hallucinated terms as an undifferentiated nuisance to be filtered by agreement heuristics, without a mechanistic account of why a given term appears or a principled criterion for how many terms to keep. We propose Subsequence-Association Filtered, breadth-aware query expansion (SAFE-QE), a training-free, retriever agnostic method that draws on the subsequence-association account of hallucination. We score each candidate expansion term by its corpus conditioned cross-context reproducibility, terms that reappear under randomised query contexts yet lack support in the pseudo-relevant set are flagged as products of dominant hallucinatory associations and down-weighted. Drawing on the characterisation of language generation as a trade-off between consistency (no hallucination) and breadth (coverage), we cast term selection as constrained coverage maximisation over the pseudo-relevant set, keeping a set that is approximately broad but consistent and stable across resampling. We also introduce the Hallucinated Expansion Term Rate, a query side faithfulness metric that counts injected expansion terms no relevant document supports. We evaluate our approach on benchmarked TREC and BEIR collections. Our SAFE-QE is statistically indistinguishable from generative and corpus-steered expansion on nDCG@10 while injecting fewer unsupported terms 4.3 per query against 27.8 for Query2doc and 57.5 for HyDE and on a strong learned sparse retriever, where every expansion method regresses. Faithfulness is therefore available at no measurable cost in ranking quality.

</details>


### [88] [SPARQL-LLM in the TEXT2SPARQL 2026 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13648)
*Panayiotis Smeros,Vincent Emonet,Ruijie Wang,Ana-Claudia Sima,Tarcisio Mendes de Farias*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SPARQL-LLM 是一个开源 KGQA 系统，在 TEXT2SPARQL 2026 挑战赛中排名第一，准确率比第二名高出 59%，已部署于生产环境的生物信息学知识图谱，但其技术细节在摘要中披露有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过大型语言模型自动将自然语言问题翻译为 SPARQL 查询，以查询分布式知识图谱。

Method: SPARQL-LLM 将自然语言问题转换为 SPARQL 查询。系统接收自然语言输入，利用大型语言模型生成针对特定知识图谱端点的 SPARQL 查询，并通过端点执行查询以获取答案。该系统在 TEXT2SPARQL 2026 挑战赛中进行了评估，支持多种语言，并已部署在生产环境中用于去中心化生物信息学知识图谱。提供的摘要中未详细描述具体的模块、训练目标和推理流程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "准确率", "value": "比第二名高出 59%", "setting": "TEXT2SPARQL 2026 挑战赛，八个参赛方案"}]}

Conclusion: SPARQL-LLM 系统架构用于 KGQA；在 TEXT2SPARQL 2026 挑战赛中排名第一，准确率比第二名高出 59%；支持英语、德语和西班牙语的多语言问答

Abstract: Querying distributed data represented as Knowledge Graphs (KGs) via SPARQL remains a difficult task, requiring fluency in query syntax, endpoint specifications, and schema details. Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA) systems address this by automatically translating natural language into SPARQL, and recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have made this translation increasingly feasible. In this paper, we describe SPARQL-LLM, our open-source KGQA system, in the context of the TEXT2SPARQL 2026 challenge. In this challenge, SPARQL-LLM ranks first overall among eight competing approaches across all datasets, achieving up to 59% higher accuracy than the second-best approach and supporting multilingual question answering in English, German, and Spanish. SPARQL-LLM is publicly released at this https URL and deployed in production over decentralized bioinformatics KGs at this https URL .

</details>
